CURRENT RUNG: 2, mechanically preventable. The refusal arm is loud and decidable, which is a real climb from silent and fabricating, but it is VALIDATION. The invalid state -- a probe citing a path the item no longer occupies -- remains perfectly writable, and safety depends on that arm running and staying enrolled. The tell that construction was available is the one DESIGN section 5 gives: the check can be satisfied by editing the ROSTER while the construction still permits the bad state to be written again tomorrow. CEILING: 4, structurally impossible -- and the move is NOT a stronger check. It is DELETING crate_relative_file from HostRustItemProbe. A stale citation cannot be written when there is no path field to go stale. The probe would carry ITEM IDENTITY ONLY, and resolution becomes a lookup in the item-grain population gunbc.rust_item_scan already produces over src/v1/stage0/src/, so a MOVE becomes invisible because the file was never part of the question. Membership is therefore decidable from a population that exists today, which is what puts the ceiling at 4 rather than leaving this a ratchet. THE ONLY REAL OBJECTION IS NAME COLLISION, AND IT IS THE HONEST REFUSAL: two items of one name resolve to Ambiguous -- a typed refusal rather than a wrong answer -- which is the same shape DagDeclarationProbe already lives with, and is why the sibling arm never had this defect. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, NAMING THE CAPABILITY AND NOT AN ARTIFACT: the host probe shape carries item identity with NO path component, resolved against the item-grain population, together with a fixture harness that keeps the refusal authorable. The artifact-shaped near-miss is worth stating because it would be satisfied while the class stood still: adding a Rust MODULE path in place of a file path replaces one positional scheme with a slightly better positional scheme and leaves the class exactly where it is. Any citation carrying a position rots on the next move. WHAT THE CLIMB OWES, so it is not discovered as a surprise: at rung 4 a path-shaped stale citation becomes UNAUTHORABLE in the accepted corpus, because there is no field left to make stale -- so the discriminating RED for this class cannot live in accepted source at all. That does not retire the evidence, it RELOCATES it. DESIGN is explicit that unrepresentable-in-the-accepted-corpus is not the boundary that decides -- a fixture handing the probe a path-shaped citation can still express the refusal, and where no harness can express it, that missing harness is the trigger rather than a permanent ceiling. So the climb owes a fixture-level red, AND THAT DEBT IS STILL OPEN -- a correction to an earlier form of this sentence, which claimed it was discharged by test.claim.namespace_cut_stale_citation_witness. It is not, and the reason is exact: that witness CONSTRUCTS HostRustItemProbe { crate_relative_file: ... } and exercises RUNTIME behaviour of the current probe shape. At rung 4 the field it constructs does not exist, so the witness does not survive the climb -- it fails to compile rather than continuing to discriminate, and the property at rung 4 is not a runtime verdict at all but the COMPILER REFUSING invalid source. A fixture testing today's runtime cannot be evidence for tomorrow's construction, and saying otherwise told the cut executor an obligation was met while the evidence for it did not yet exist (codex review 60652). What is owed at the climb is stated as a CAPABILITY and not as an artifact, because a trigger naming less than the capability is satisfied while the capability stays dead: the ability to hand the COMPILER source carrying a path-shaped citation and obtain a located refusal from it, SUFFICIENT FOR this class's red to remain discriminating once crate_relative_file has no constructor. A file existing under dag/test/claim/ does not satisfy that and must not be read as satisfying it. DESIGN section 4b is explicit that where no harness can express the subject, that missing capability is the trigger rather than a permanent ceiling. The current witness remains the enrolled evidence for the RUNTIME arm under 4b(4) and is not retired by any of this; it simply is not the thing the climb needs. A further earlier form pointed the next author at a do-not-fix block in gunbc.namespace_cut_subject_roster; that block described stale citations which have since been corrected, so it had no subject left and was deleted with them, and pointing at it now would name an artifact that does not exist. RECOGNITION RULE FOR THIS VARIANT: when a check's PASSING verdict is 'the thing is gone', ask what else produces 'not found' -- a moved file, an unread file, a renamed symbol, a scope the scanner never entered. Every one of those is indistinguishable from success unless the check searches the population rather than testing the citation, and each one silently converts a stale pointer into a manufactured acceptance.
- **bound-shaped closure** (a requirement is satisfied by a number that is real, and the row it satisfies stops ranking for work. Distinct from prefix-of-the-truth, which is about what a PRODUCER reports: this is about what a CONSUMER concludes from a satisfied clause. Receipt (gunbc#9418): §4b(3) asks a declared drop for a bounded population; a whole-root emit census answers three; the emitter's first gate had returned ahead of every later check, and clearing it showed eight more that had been standing the whole time. Publishing three as THE population would have discharged 4b(3) with an honest measurement and left the class reading as closed — worse than the UNCOUNTED it replaced, because an unmet clause still ranks and a met one does not. The tell is that the satisfying figure comes from a mechanism that STOPS: a first match, an early return, a capped scan, a truncated list. **Recognition rule: before a number discharges a requirement, ask what the producer does AFTER it has enough to answer — if it returns, the number bounds the producer's patience and not the population.**)
- **authority substitution** (fact F lives in carrier A; operation O is governed by carrier B; A is cited as the evidence that O changed; no A→B relation is claimed by either carrier. Nothing asserted is false — the *entailment* is invented, which is why review reads past it: both halves check out and only the arrow between them is missing. It is §3's single-authority rule read in the direction authors actually violate it — not two authorities for one fact, but one authority borrowed to answer for another's. Receipt (gunbc#8865): an expecting-red probe was enrolled in `gunbc.explicit_witness_admission`, which DOCUMENTS a known red, and the PR asserted the required floor therefore held it; the floor gates on `v2.workflow.floor_expected_red` and nothing relates the two, so CI went red on a row its author had recorded as expected. The general tell is a sentence of the form *I registered it in A, so B now does X* where B is never named — and its structural fix is never a broader guard joining every A row to B, since that manufactures exactly the entailment being invented; the guard must carry the narrow predicate, with its exceptions declared.)**SELF-SUCCESSION WITH THE RETIRED IMPLEMENTATION STILL INSIDE THE EXECUTABLE (L4 source-trace receipt, 2026-09-05, gunbc#10578).** At source revision 06a30d90376d5fb1fef91d6963604e3b053b9df1, `v1_compiler.required_regen_host` `run_built_seed_regen` obtains current_exe, hashes the on-disk executable, compares it with admitted_executable_digest, and launches that executable with --required-regen. Its return type is FirstGenerationConvergenceObservation. The downstream `compile_stage0` calls `v1.compiler.compile` `compile_sources_selected`, linked into the same binary. This is a source-body and call-chain observation, not a newly executed generation run or a report that native promotion was actually granted. **THE INVALID INFERENCE IS CONVERGENCE THEREFORE SEED INDEPENDENCE.** The executable may really be the artifact named by the digest and may really produce the next generation; those facts do not establish which implementation performs compilation inside it. Binding the observation to the launched artifact answers the identity question correctly and still cannot discharge seed retirement. Fact F belongs to the rebuilt-seed convergence carrier; native promotion and operational retirement ask a different question of `v2.compiler.self_host.promotion_admission` and `gunbc.v1_consumer_census`. Treating the first as sufficient evidence for the second invents exactly this row's missing entailment. The existing convergence operation is not defective for its own seed-regeneration contract. This receipt extends authority_substitution rather than minting a succession ledger or a second failure class. `a_live_authority_name_carries_a_superseded_claim` requires a name-refresh edit preserving a superseded proposition; no name refresh occurred here. `execution_provenance_loss` concerns an observation path that may not have run; the trap here survives a genuine run and a genuine artifact binding. `total_at_the_level_examined_blind` concerns a judgment collapsing nested alternatives; this specimen instead borrows one carrier's established property to answer an unestablished replacement property. **RECOGNITION AND NEXT CONTROL.** A replacement receipt that proves the outer executable's identity must also account for the implementation doing its inner semantic work. Process isolation, missing seed files, denied subprocesses, and an empty PATH cannot exclude a seed already statically linked into G1. Build the candidate from an admitted emitted-source population with retired seed dependencies unavailable at compile/link time; join the full source and dependency manifests to that build, including build scripts and copied bodies. A package-name ban or shared-library list alone is insufficient. The discriminating specimen is a correctly identified candidate wrapper that still links the retired compiler: it must refuse replacement admission even when its self-launch and behavioral comparison succeed. That control is specified, not executed here. Current defense at this boundary is source-trace diligence; no new enforced rung or seed-independence receipt is claimed. 
- **reachability read as occupancy** (a guard's arm, disposition or check is deleted because nothing currently lands in it. Three questions are in play and only the first two decide whether it should exist: *mechanism existence* — can any governing authority produce this state; *join reachability* — can that mechanism classify an element of THIS operation's denominator; *current occupancy* — does anything receive it today. **Yes / yes / zero is a healthy guard being quiet, not a dead one**, and deleting it removes a live wall while looking principled. The distinction is load-bearing because it borders a true rule pointing the other way: a check whose RED IS UNREACHABLE — no authorable input can produce it — is a decoration, permanently green and worse than absent, because it is cited as coverage. Unreachable means the red cannot be produced at all; empty means it can and nothing does. Two corollaries, each from a lane that got it wrong first. Reachability is judged against what a FIXTURE MAY AUTHOR, never what the accepted corpus contains — a regression probe is an invalid program by definition, so a state no `Accepted` program holds is usually still authorable as source, and scoping reachability to corpus-expressible is this same conflation one layer out. And an arm is reachable relative to ONE operation: `gunbc.scm`'s path-exclusion disposition is unreachable from the floor's own fold, which drops excluded subjects before inventory, yet reachable from a join over admission ROWS, which exclusion never removes — so *the arm's reachability is a property of the denominator of the operation that returns it, not of what some lower mechanism observes internally*. Both halves were found the same day by separate lanes, from opposite directions. **THE DISCRIMINATING COUNTER-SPECIMEN, which this row needed and did not have (gunbc XL-0-RECEIPT, 2026-09-01):** `gunbc.floor_component_receipt` and `gunbc.floor_component_receipt_document` were a well-built pair — schema, artifact name, path, a typed subject carrying workflow name / run id / head sha, a decoder with typed `ReceiptMemberAbsent` and `ReceiptMemberMalformed` causes, and an event-subject join refusing over three distinct mismatch arms. Read against the three questions the answer is not yes/yes/zero but **NO at the first**: nothing anywhere writes `target/floor-component-receipt.json`, no Rust carries the schema string, and `witnesses.yml` uploads four artifacts of which the receipt is not one. Their reader was the `falsifier-alert.yml` job, deleted in the 2026-08-15 CI cut. **The join is UNREACHABLE, not merely unoccupied, and that is the whole difference between retiring a dead model and deleting a live guard** — a quiet guard has a producer that could fill it, this had none. The census must be run per medium, since a survivor stops being reached silently: import graph (only its own witness test), `*_test.dag` discovery (that test is a PEER of the subject, not a guard over a survivor), workflow argv, Cargo `[[bin]]`, Rust (one string literal used as a test label, not a call), and roster rows (two `gunbc.non_fold_residue` frontier rows). **The second, costlier half — what the prose did while the model sat dead — is its own class and is rostered separately as `unbacked_execution_claim`;** what belongs HERE is only the consequence for this rule: a dead model is not harmless while it waits, because **a good model with no consumer is still residue (§6), and residue that describes itself in the present tense actively manufactures false premises.** Disposition landed: both modules and their witness test deleted, the two frontier rows removed, and every surviving citation of them rewritten to the past tense with the deletion and its date recorded — because a citation left naming a deleted symbol fakes a grep hit for the next reader.)
- **total at the level examined, blind one level down** (a judgment is exhaustive over the type it matches and says nothing about the distinction that actually decides the outcome, which lives inside one of its arms. **The pairing is the whole content: the match is exhaustive AND wrong**, and the exhaustiveness is what hides it — there is no missing arm to report, no warning, nothing incomplete, so neither a reviewer reading the match nor a wildcard-free lens can see it. Specimen with a mutation receipt (gunbc.scm, 2026-08-22), **repaired on main 2026-08-25 by gunbc#8973 and kept in the past tense because the mechanism recurs while this instance does not**: a publication fold matched one outer arm carrying a JSON codec's entire refusal enum. It was total over that codec's refusal type, and the enum's arms carried three different publication remedies — a reader limitation, an absent-object problem over a well-formed document, and malformed bytes. Only the third may supersede a head slot, so the collapsed form let an OLDER READER OVERWRITE A NEWER WRITER'S DOCUMENT: data loss produced by the reader's own limitation, from a match that compiled and looked complete. Executed both ways — descending, four witnesses passed; collapsed, the witness driving all three remedies through the single outer arm went red; a temporary decode arm made the fold non-exhaustive; restored control passed. **None of this row's three cited symbols resolved on main, and they failed in two different ways** — measured across main's whole history, not just its head. `repository_load_disposition`, the function this row said performed the collapse, has NEVER existed as code on main: it appears only in this carrier and in the projection, and it names a declaration from the CLOSED attempt gunbc#8940, whose branch was retained in history and never landed. So the row cited a function that was fabricated on the day it was written. `RepositoryDecodeImageRefusal` and `ImageRefusal` were real — they landed with gunbc#8807 and gunbc#8840 and were deleted by gunbc#8965 on 2026-08-23 when the fused format was cut — so those two went stale one day later, by the ordinary route a citation rots when the thing it names is removed. The two halves matter separately because they have different remedies: a fabricated name is caught by resolving symbols before writing them, a deleted one only by a resolver that reruns. Either way a reader applying this entry's own recognition rule grepped for a match that was not there. That is §3's stale-citation class sitting inside the authority that names it, and it is recorded here rather than quietly deleted because a specimen whose symbols cannot be resolved is the failure this row exists to teach, one level up. **What landed is stronger than descending**, and the difference is the reusable part: the remedy vocabulary was hoisted into a module that names no format — `gunbc.scm.load_standing` `LoadStanding`, and each format now classifies its own refusals into it (`gunbc.scm.commit_closure_json_v2` `closure_document_load_standing`). Descending inside one codec's enum would have closed the collapse and left the consumer still matching on a format's vocabulary; hoisting leaves the consumer no format arms to collapse, so the next format is a new classifier rather than a new way to reintroduce this defect. **THE HOISTING WAS NECESSARY AND NOT SUFFICIENT, and this sentence is the fourth citation in this row to have gone stale.** It read that the permission was derived `by one total function`, naming `standing_may_supersede_generation` — a `LoadStanding -> Bool` carrying neither the slot the observation was made on nor the generation examined, so a caller could apply it to a slot the evidence never described or to a generation that had moved since it was read. gunbc#9258 deleted it and replaced the destination with `gunbc.scm.supersession` `supersede_head_slot`, where evidence is minted FROM the slot by `observe_generation` — a caller cannot state which generation it examined, only examine one — and the swap refuses unless the slot still holds it. The deletion left this sentence naming a declaration that no longer resolves, one day after it was written, which is the same rot this row already records twice above and is corrected here for the same reason. Second, independent specimen the same day, in the compiler itself: a census of the fourteen declared-type positions the grammar admits found five accepting a plain kernel value where a coproduct is declared — the judgment is present and total at the level it examines, and never descends. **The tell is a `_` or a bare binding in the arm that carries the payload**, and the test is not whether the match is complete but whether every cause it collapses shares one remedy; the fix is to descend so a new inner arm fails to compile HERE, rather than silently inheriting whichever remedy the outer arm happened to name.)
- **execution-provenance loss** (a reported value is consumed as though an observation produced it, while the carrier holds no proof the observation path ran. **Distinct from state-space conflation, and the repair law is what separates them**: there the carrier cannot represent the distinction and the fix is to split the states; here the carrier already has the state — the consumer simply cannot tell whether it was *reached*, and the fix is to bind an execution receipt to the value. Three specimens in one night, all on main, none of them a missing variant. `known_red_held=206` is `36 held + 170 that RUNTIME-ERRORED`: the substrate splits those into distinct arms, so the states exist; what the quoted figure loses is *did the assertion run*, and an expected-red row that threw is not evidence the red is expected — it is a row whose assertion never executed. A floor whose `TerminalLedgerUnrenderable` count is `0` reads identically whether the ledger checks passed or preparation refused before a ledger existed. And a `.dag` board of `E0599=23 E0308=128` reads identically whether cargo ran or normalize refused upstream of it. **The receipt that makes this worth a row rather than a caution:** for three hours after #8976 put a §4c-illegal annotation inside a match body, the compiler refused at strict preparation while downstream measurements kept producing valid-looking values — one lane concluded a defect was closed, another concluded the same defect was still firing, both citing runs where it could not appear at all; three separate A/B comparisons reported perfect agreement, one because both arms were broken identically, one because a missing flag meant no arm executed, and one because the defect had been fixed between runs so both arms were the same bytes. Every one of those is downstream of a single fact: **a masked run and a clean run rendered identically.** Recognition rule: if a count of zero and an unreached stage produce the same output, the number is not an observation. The construction move, and it is stronger than naming known masks because it also forecloses the undiscovered ones: any stage capable of preventing a downstream observation from executing must participate in the observation carrier, so `refused before stage N` cannot inhabit the same carrier as `stage N executed with zero findings` — a known mask is a hole someone found, execution provenance is what stops an unfound hole from masquerading as a measurement.)**A HUMAN-SIDE SPECIMEN, AND ITS ORIGIN IS A MECHANISM RATHER THAN A LOOSE PROXY (2026-09-04/05, gunbc#10490 / gunbc#10491).** A reviewer offered a matched pair as evidence for a failure-mode row: a RED arm at head c30c049f68 and a GREEN arm at head 5888fb0106 after integrating main. The red arm was executed and real. The green arm was never queried. What was actually read was the TREE at that head -- the function present, and git merge-base --is-ancestor of the commit that added it true -- and that property was reported as the job result. THE TIMES, because the adjective is not the finding: the message asserting the green arm was sent 00:19:10Z; heal-generated-artifacts on 5888fb0106 STARTED 00:18:32Z and COMPLETED 00:35:42Z. Sixteen minutes early, and the row it was offered for was about misattributed evidence, so accepting it would have reproduced the class inside its own filing. It was refused at intake and gunbc#10479 at 245416762b -- where heal actually ran and passed in 18m56s after exactly the prescribed remedy -- was substituted as the executed control. **WHY THAT SUBSTITUTION IS THE HARD CASE OF THIS ROW: the tree property is CAUSALLY UPSTREAM of the green, not an unrelated proxy.** It is the actual mechanism by which the job passes, which is what made reading it feel like verification rather than prediction -- a proxy known to be loose gets checked, and a mechanism believed gets reported. Every ordinary defence here fails: the reasoning was correct, the measurement was correct, the prediction was correct, and the claim was still unbacked, because the proposition asserted was about a JOB and the evidence held was about a TREE. **AND IT COMPLETED SUCCESS, WHICH IS WHAT MAKES THE CLASS EXPENSIVE RATHER THAN EMBARRASSING.** Afterwards a lucky prediction and a verified control are indistinguishable: nothing goes red, and re-checking reproduces the same true-of-nothing answer. Had it failed, the row would have carried a false positive control and nothing in the offer would have warned the reader. That asymmetry is the one receipt_names_a_property_not_the_tree_it_holds_of records for expired greens -- same shape, different subject: there a green stops being true when its base moves, here a green was never observed at all. **RECOGNITION RULE, AND THE SPECIMEN CARRIES ITS OWN WARNING ABOUT IT: name the observation you queried, not the state that implies it.** This row was offered by that same reviewer as the candidate identity for the OTHER lane's class twenty minutes earlier, read carefully, and correctly judged a poor fit there -- and was not recognised when the next error was their own. Reading a class is not detecting it; the question that would have caught it is mechanical and takes one call: WHICH API DID I QUERY FOR THIS OUTCOME. If the answer names a file, a commit, an ancestry test or a diff, the outcome is predicted and must be reported as a prediction. 
- remediation-mutated view (the standard response to a signal OVERWRITES the surface most readers query it through, so the population becomes unreconstructible AT THAT LEVEL and the reader concludes the evidence is gone. It usually is not: the defect is reading a MUTABLE PROJECTION as if it were the record, and a level below it there is generally an immutable one the same query reaches intact. Receipt, and the inversion belongs BESIDE it rather than after it: a fleet event terminated eight witnesses runs inside 49 seconds; every lane re-ran; a re-run REPLAYS the original merge ref and OVERWRITES the run-level conclusion, so queried through run conclusions the population was invisible -- zero of twenty-one -- while queried through the run's attempt records it was complete and exact hours later. Two independent reconstructions from the mutated view reported five and six of the eight: each correct, each incomplete, same cause -- so the mutated view fails SILENTLY and PARTIALLY, which is worse than failing empty. The rule is therefore NOT capture-or-lose-it: capture before remediating, or reconstruct from the immutable record, never from the mutable one. Stating only the first half is worse than saying nothing, because a lane that has already remediated reads it as a verdict that its evidence is gone and stops looking -- which is what produced the zero of twenty-one. Recognition rule: before concluding a population is unrecoverable, ask WHICH LEVEL you queried and whether the remedy WRITES to it.)
- diagnostic name accurate about the situation, silent about the mechanism (a diagnostic's NAME describes the state it was raised in faithfully and says nothing about WHICH PREDICATE produced it, so a reader who treats the name as a cause builds a coherent story with no load-bearing contact with the code. Nothing in the name is wrong, which is why review reads past it. THREE RECEIPTS IN ONE NIGHT FROM THREE READERS, provenance stated per receipt because a row about untraced names must not itself rest on an untraced relay. FIRST, measured by this row's author, and it leads because the reader was the author of the story: rustc labels reading `expected Rc<Vector<i64>>, found String` at 49 sites plus 2 reversed, from which a representation fork was inferred -- the model type `Symbol` rendering as `String` in parameter position and a vector in field position -- and published. Measured: `Symbol` renders CONSISTENTLY as `String`, `Connective.Atom` carries `identity: String`, and the `.dag` is well-typed with the same declared type on both sides. The published mechanism was wrong; the label was a faithful description of a mismatch the type-checker had computed. AND THE CHECK FAILED THE SAME WAY THE READING DID, which is the most instructive part rather than decoration: a grep for the printed type name returned zero and zero was read as absence, when the emitted source spells `Rc<Vec<i64>>` and `lib.rs` carries `use im::Vector as Vec`, so rustc was printing the resolved underlying name. A name was read as a fact about the tree twice in one investigation, once in the diagnostic and once in the refutation of it. WHAT THE 49 SITES ACTUALLY ARE, and it is UNCHASED: `Rc<Vec<i64>>` is how `List<Int>` renders, so they read expected `List<Int>` found `String` -- an ordinary mechanism, and a real one. The amendment closed the wrong explanation, not the defect. SECOND, reported by quick-bat-372 and independently verified: `MethodNotFound` names the situation correctly and does not say which predicate gates it -- `method_existence_decision` gates on `kernel_profile_lookup`, so a fully resolved USER product with no kernel profile answers `MethodExistenceUndecided`. THIRD, same provenance: `FieldNotFound` names a concept never emitted for field ACCESS at all, established by exhausting its three construction sites -- two pattern bindings and one guarded by a record literal's field names. WHY IT EARNS A ROW: two of the three would have shipped as PERMANENTLY-GREEN WITNESS ARMS, an assertion that a wall fired over a wall that cannot fire for that input, which is section 4b's decoration-cited-as-coverage reached by a route no other row names -- and all three were caught by reading the PRODUCER rather than by review. WHAT IS NOT CLAIMED: three instances in one night is a suggestive rate and not a base rate; all three surfaced while several lanes were unusually deep in diagnostic populations, so the frequency is a property of that work rather than of the corpus. Recognition rule: before treating a diagnostic name as a mechanism, read the function that produces it and find the predicate that gates it; if you cannot name that predicate, you have a situation and not a cause. A grep for the type name is a cheap first pass, not a sufficient check: rustc prints resolved names while source spells aliases, so `use X as Y` defeats the search in both directions and a zero can be confident and wrong. Read the producer. AMENDED 2026-08-28 -- THE PRODUCER'S OWN PROSE IS ALSO A NAME, so `read the producer` is necessary and NOT SUFFICIENT. The three receipts above are all DIAGNOSTIC names; the same failure reaches COMMENTS, and it is harder to see there because a comment is where a reader goes to ESCAPE the name. Two variants, and the ranking is the whole point. STALE prose is FALSIFIABLE BY READING: what it says is contradicted by the code as it stands, so a reader who checks the producer can always catch it. THAT IS NOT THE SAME AS SELF-CORRECTING, and an earlier revision of this row said it was -- `it rots when the code moves, and whoever next touches that code notices` -- which THIS ROW'S OWN SECOND RECEIPT REFUTES (review 57329): the deleted/still-here contradiction is LIVE in the tree today, and it was found by a reader who had gone there for an unrelated reason, NOT by the next person to touch the code. So the ranking below is about FALSIFIABILITY and not about correction rate. Stale prose persists exactly as long as nobody reads it, which is indefinitely; what makes it the lesser of the two is that a reader who does check is guaranteed to see the disagreement. WRONG-MECHANISM-CORRECT-CONCLUSION is NOT self-limiting: nothing it says is falsified by moving the code, so it survives every refactor, and the defect it names really existing READS AS CONFIRMATION of the explanation it gives -- load-bearing misinformation with a correct headline, routing every future reader to the wrong repair. RECEIPT, read independently by three readers rather than relayed: the comment above the floor's live-tree scan says its two computations `cannot agree except by coincidence` because one is a syntactic scan and the other falls through to `effect_reach_derived_reads_live_tree_for_entry`, a SEMANTIC reachability derivation -- locating the cause in the import graph. Read the control flow: `reads_live_tree_effective` calls `parse_entry_live_tree_disposition` first, and an UNDECLARED file reaches `Ok(declared.unwrap_or(true))`, so the function RETURNS before the derivation is consulted. The largest and simplest disagreement class is decided before the import graph is reached, and the mechanism the comment names has nothing to do with it. A SECOND RECEIPT FROM THE SAME PARAGRAPH, in the stale variant: it states the floor's copy is DELETED rather than reconciled, in the past tense, four lines above a section headed WHY IT IS STILL HERE explaining that the scan is live. RECOGNITION RULE, extending the one above rather than replacing it: THE CODE PATH IS THE FACT, and every sentence around it -- in a note, in a doc, or in the producer's own comment -- is commentary that may be stale or may be wrong about WHY. And having reached the producer, do not stop at the sentence that confirms your framing: this amendment exists because a reader quoted IT IS NO WALL from such a comment and dropped `and it is a partial classifier, not an enforcement` from four lines below, inside the same citation.)
- identity-absent graph traversal (a walk over a graph the COMPILER built re-enters shared nodes because it cannot carry a `visited` or memo key — the nodes have no identity the model is allowed to name — so the only two arms available locally are to PAY the re-entry, or to key on a substitute the substrate forbids. THE TRAP IS THAT THE ARMS DIFFER IN KIND AND THE CHEAP-LOOKING ONE IS THE UNSAFE ONE: paying is a cost, visible and bounded; keying on a substitute silently skips a DISTINCT node believed already visited, drops whatever that node contributed, and reports nothing. THE MECHANISM, structural and checkable by reading rather than by measurement: `v1.compiler.infer_emit_info` `collect_type_node_import_surface_names` recurses on BOTH `children` AND the `inferred` resolved node, so a shared type DAG is traversed as a tree and a node reachable by k paths is walked k times. The key does not exist. `std.occurrence_identity` `occurrence_identity_scope_law` names filename, SourceSpan, authored name, structural Node equality and content hash as FORBIDDEN identity inputs, and `NodeOccurrenceIdentity` `OccurrenceSynthetic` — which is what the compiler mints for a node no author wrote — carries no id at all, so every synthesised node compares equal to every other and a visited set built on it would skip the wrong one. The remaining candidate is host allocation identity, which exists only in the realization and not in the model. RUNG: OUTSIDE THE LADDER, and the earlier revision of this row said `mitigatable`, which was inflation of exactly the kind section 4b(1) names. The invalid state is writable, nothing refuses it, and its failure is a silent skip — section 4b puts silent wrongness outside the ladder rather than low on it. What that revision offered as the mitigation was that no author has yet needed such a traversal and that review might catch one; neither is containment by a total operation, a typed outcome, a bound, a rollback or an isolation, and no discriminating RED exists on any acceptance path, so no rung above the floor is claimable by executed evidence. ONE PARTIAL CONTAINMENT IS REAL AND DOES NOT LIFT THE CLASS: at the measured seam a dropped name reaches an emitted Rust crate, where rustc refuses it as E0422/E0425/E0433 far from the cause, and the regen byte-identity gate would show the drift. That is a property of THAT seam's consumer being a compiled artifact, not of the class — a traversal whose consumer is a verdict rather than a compilation has no such backstop — and section 4b sets a class's rung at the MINIMUM across its in-scope paths. CEILING: structural impossibility, and per section 4b(3) the trigger names the CAPABILITY rather than an artifact — an identity for synthesised occurrences, sufficient for a `visited` set over inferred type nodes to be authorable in `.dag` at all. Nothing less retires it: a faster traversal, a memo at one seam, or a counter measuring the amplification all leave the key absent and the unsafe arm equally reachable. An intermediate rung is available and is NOT claimed here because nothing implements it: a typed refusal at the traversal seam would make the class mechanically preventable while leaving the invalid state writable. THE ESCAPES THAT LOOK AVAILABLE AND ARE NOT, listed because this class is mostly discovered by trying them, and each was closed by EXECUTION rather than by argument — deleting the producer, replacing it with the cheaper sub-producers beside it, reading `TypeSummary.field_import_surface_names` instead (which is CIRCULAR, being produced BY this walk in `build_field_type_map`, and answers a different relation), and hoisting the work earlier (there is nothing to hoist: each item belongs to one module and each module is emitted once). Two further hypotheses about the COST were falsified the same way and are worth not repeating: the list-copy lowering, and the match-scrutinee deep clone. The measured populations, the amplification, the per-escape figures and the two falsifications are recorded once, dated, with their method and their non-enrollment stated, in the plan document `emit-type-surface-traversal-cost`, which is bound to this traversal and its two consumers in `gunbc.doc_graph_roots` and retires when they move; they are deliberately not transcribed here, because a number copied into a roster row is the positional-citation failure one entry up wearing different clothes. RECOGNITION RULE: when you reach for a visited or memo key over a graph the compiler built rather than the author wrote, enumerate the keys available to you; if every candidate is one the identity authority forbids, the deficit is upstream in the identity algebra and NEITHER local arm is a repair — the amplification is the symptom and the missing identity is the class.)
- generated binding shadows a bare render (an emitter that renders a name by SPELLING while a different generator in the same emission adds a binding for that spelling — a use line, an alias, a prelude — composes into a silent rebinding: each generator is locally correct and the artifact realizes the wrong declaration, with the diagnostic landing at the CONSUMER of the emitted file, far from either producer. - resolved reference outside the execution closure (an Accepted program contains a resolved call edge whose target declaration's OWNER MODULE is absent from the module closure loaded for that execution, so resolve and typecheck both report success and the program dies at run time. MECHANISM: bare-reference resolution consults a declaration population BROADER than the explicit-import closure from which the runtime index is built, so the two phases answer to two different denominators and nothing joins them. THE SPECIMEN, found by execution rather than by reading, and recorded here because a class whose only evidence is an argument is not a class: three witness files -- test.claim.spark_serving_observe_witness_test, test.claim.spark_serving_model_store_witness_test and test.claim.spark.spark_cell_role_retirement_witness_test -- CALLED hermetic_fixture_provenance with no import of the module declaring it. The positive control is exact and cheap: adding the one import admits and executes the UNCHANGED call. WHY IT IS NOT ONE OF THE ROWS BESIDE IT, checked rather than assumed. It is not `reachability_read_as_occupancy`: nothing here mistook reachability for occupancy, and the declaration is genuinely reachable AND genuinely resolved. It is not `identity_absent_graph_traversal`: the declaration exists, has an identity, and that identity is found -- what is absent is the RELATION from the resolved target to the modules the execution loader materializes. This is the missing agreement between two denominators, not the occupancy of either one. RUNG, SCOPED AND NOT INFLATED: mitigatable at the source-to-witness-execution grain the specimen demonstrates, where the runtime refusal is typed, located and loud. THE LANGUAGE-WIDE RUNG IS NOT CLAIMED and the reason is a control that has not been run: place an observable EFFECT before the unloaded call and see whether it executes before the refusal. If it does, the class is not mitigatable at that grain without preflight or rollback, because the failure arrives after the program has already acted. Until that control answers, this row's rung is a statement about witness execution and nothing wider. CEILING: structurally guaranteed rather than structurally impossible -- source can still omit an import, so the state stays writable, but no Accepted program should contain the edge. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, naming the CAPABILITY: acceptance joins every resolved declaration identity's owner module to the exact local/import/prelude closure used to construct the execution index. DISCRIMINATING RED: a bare call with no explicit import is refused BEFORE Accepted, not after. POSITIVE CONTROL: adding the exact import admits and executes the unchanged call. REPAIR DIRECTION, and the wrong arm is the tempting one: constrain RESOLUTION and ADMISSION. Do NOT widen the runtime loader to chase bare-reference edges -- that converts undeclared dependencies into implicit imports and dissolves the direct-import law to make a symptom go away, which is the absorbing-fallback shape applied to a language rule. Both phases must consume ONE admitted dependency closure. SECOND SPECIMEN, A SECOND INDEX, AND THE REASON IT IS APPENDED HERE RATHER THAN MINTED AS ITS OWN CLASS (found 2026-09-04, import-cut lane, by executing an instrument rather than by reading): the first specimen fails through the DECLARATION index and says `no declaration named X in this execution loaded index`. This one fails through the SERVICE-OPERATION index and says `Unimplemented { what: "unknown service operation: git.Inspect.HeadCommit" }`. Different index, different diagnostic, identical mechanism -- so it is one class with two routes, not two classes. THE SPECIMEN: gunbc.namespace_cut_subject_observation subject_roster_report refused identically on the wet route and under --dry-run, reached through observe_current_live_hand_rust. Positive control, same shape as the first specimen and equally cheap: adding the one import executes the UNCHANGED call. IT IS A POPULATION, NOT AN INSTANCE -- AND THE POPULATION SIZE IS DELIBERATELY NOT CLAIMED HERE. Three members are CONFIRMED by reading their call sites and import lines: gunbc.rust_item_host_observation, gunbc.stage0.stage0_rust_host_observation, and gunbc.live_deploy.candidate. All three import extdeps.git and none imports extdeps.git.inspect -- three independent authors reaching the same wrong sibling, which is what makes the near-miss module name part of the mechanism rather than an anecdote. THREE IS A FLOOR, NOT THE CENSUS, and this sentence exists because the row first said otherwise. The confirmed set was produced by a textual scan for a call spelling joined against a top-level import spelling, and such a scan is wrong in BOTH directions: it matches the spelling inside prose and mock declarations, it misses non-top-level and differently-spelled import forms, and it cannot see a module that receives extdeps.git.inspect transitively and legitimately. A second coarse scan surfaced roughly a dozen further candidates, none of which are asserted here as defects, precisely because that scan censuses SHAPES and this row records FACTS. The true population is UNDETERMINED pending a resolved-call census that joins each resolved service-operation identity to the module closure its execution index was built from -- which is the instrument the named next-rung trigger produces, and which no grep substitutes for. Writing a confirmed lower bound into this row as though it were the size of the class would make the row a censored estimator about its own subject, and it would later be cited as the size of the class. THE AGGRAVATION THIS SPECIMEN ADDS, and it is worse than the refusal: some members of that population do NOT refuse today, which can only mean extdeps.git.inspect reaches their execution index through some OTHER module import line. So whether a service call resolves is decided by a fact stated nowhere near the call site, and it flips when an unrelated author tidies an import they own. The first specimen showed the two denominators DISAGREEING loudly; this one shows them AGREEING BY ACCIDENT, which is the quiet arm of the same defect and is not discoverable by running the program. CONSEQUENCE FOR THE REPAIR DIRECTION ALREADY STATED ABOVE: it is unchanged and this specimen strengthens it. The tempting arm here is even cheaper than widening the loader -- add three import lines -- and it is still the absorbing fallback, because it repairs the instances while leaving accidental-agreement intact for every future call site. The named next-rung trigger is what removes the class, and a population-derived execution index would remove the accidental-agreement arm with it. RECOGNITION RULE: a green typecheck is not evidence that a call can execute unless the phase that accepted it and the phase that will run it were answering to the same module set; when a name resolves but its module is not imported, ask which denominator each phase used before concluding the reference is sound.)
- **surface shorthand preempts resolved identity** (a semantic consumer dispatches on a raw lexeme after resolution has already named its declaration, so a declared constructor is reinterpreted as a host sentinel or primitive. Recognition rule: once a node carries a binding/declaration identity, value construction must be total over that identity; spelling may be interpreted only on an explicitly unresolved shorthand arm. Specimen gunbc#9741: bare and imported `None` became host `Null` while qualified `Diagnostics.None {}` became a `Variant`; a single-frame comparison hid the fork. This is not hollow alias: the binding is grounded and present, then discarded downstream. It is also separate from the per-frame symbol-ordinal defect: that defect corrupted lexeme identity while crossing frames, whereas this one ignores an identity already supplied at one construction site. Next-rung trigger: host literals and intentional primitive representations are typed parser/resolver constructions, and evaluator value construction is total over every resolved binding with no raw-spelling arm able to override it.)
- **meaning fork** (one scoped name carries more than one material semantic referent under hidden state; accuracy of meaning applies to terms in every layer, and for a contract-bearing name, differences in obligations, quality floor, refusal behavior, billing consequence, or remedy establish different referents. The subject is scoped: the key is the naming surface, the visible name, and the declared effective version or epoch — the same spelling in two explicitly distinct scopes or across a declared version transition is legitimate reuse, not a fork. Recognition rule: hold that key constant and vary the hidden state; if the material semantic referent changes without a distinct variant name, the name has forked — for a contract-bearing name, a change in obligations, quality floor, refusal behavior, billing consequence, or remedy is sufficient evidence of that change. This is the one-name/two-meanings dual of nicknaming.)
- **externalized degradation** (an actor that accepted an obligation or risk, or caused a cost, silently shifts the resulting burden to another principal while preserving the apparent name, price, or contract. Recognition rule: identify the accepted obligation or causal responsibility, trigger the adverse state, and follow who bears the marginal burden; if another principal's burden rises while the responsible actor neither absorbs it through its reserve, refusal, or remedy path nor exposes a separately named, priced, and refusable transfer, the burden was externalized. A disclosed, refusable allocation agreed before contract formation is the transfer arm; an undisclosed or unilateral later shift is refused. The honest arms are absorb-and-reserve or separately name-and-price the transfer.)
- mistyped body radiates nonlocal phantom diagnostics (LAYER, stated first because carrying this finding across it inverts its meaning: the producer is the .dag COMPILER diagnosing .dag SOURCE -- the interpreter/type-check path behind gunbc compile and the --required-regen self-compile -- NOT rustc diagnosing emitted Rust; nothing here supports discounting any rustc-diagnostic census. THE FAILURE: a type error confined to ONE module-scope fn body does not refuse at that body with a located diagnostic; it radiates as a fabricated population of field-access diagnostics at UNRELATED modules, so the line stops pointing at the wrong factories. SPECIMEN (XL-0T, PR #9720): one fn added to v1.compiler.infer_env whose body held NonEmptyStr-as-String casts in comparison/argument position plus a FilePath-vs-String if-arm mismatch produced ~700 advisory `no field 'source_indices' on type 'TypeEnv'` rows across compile.dag, 05_emit_rust.dag and 04_resolve.dag -- modules that were fine -- and blocked required-regen, which printed 12 of them and never named the defective body. A reader begins repairing the wrong files; that misdirection, not the volume, is the harm (section 5: every refusal typed AND located). FOUR-POINT DISCRIMINATOR, one instrument (entry-scoped compile of src/v1/compile.dag, counting the diagnostic): full mistyped body 774; same signature with trivial body 0; casts hoisted into let bindings with structure kept 0 phantoms plus ONE real correctly-located error at the fn's own line; that real error fixed rc=0. The series also refuted the name-capture hypothesis for this specimen, since the fn name was held constant across all four points. RUNG: outside the ladder at the diagnosed-population grain -- the fabricated rows ARE silent wrongness about the modules they name -- while the run-level verdict stayed mitigatable (the compile did refuse; it refused about the wrong subject). CEILING: structurally guaranteed -- an expression-position type failure inside a body refuses AT that body before any cross-module field-access checking consumes the poisoned inference state. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, naming the capability: body-local type-error containment, sufficient that no diagnostic about module B can be produced by a type defect whose every construction site is inside module A. RECOGNITION RULE: when N same-shaped field/type diagnostics appear at unrelated sites immediately after adding or editing ONE declaration, suspect that declaration's body typing before believing any named site; trivial-body the new declaration as the two-minute discriminator, and hoist casts out of comparison/argument position into let bindings to recover the located refusal.)
- **instrument output read as subject content** (a consumer derives a SET from a report whose completeness is a property of the REPORTING TOOL rather than of the subject. **The boundary against `execution_provenance_loss` is the whole justification for a separate row, because the two look identical at the call site and have opposite repairs**: there a value is consumed as though an observation produced it while nothing proves the observation path RAN, and the fix is to bind an execution receipt; here the observation ran and reported FAITHFULLY — the instrument answered its own question correctly — and what is wrong is that its output was complete for the REPORTER s purpose and was read as complete for the CONSUMER s. Provenance is intact; the SUBJECT is substituted, so no receipt binding can detect it and the repair is instead to derive the set from the subject itself. Distinct from `total_at_the_level_examined_blind`, which is a judgment shape (a match total over the type it examines, blind inside an arm), and from `reachability_read_as_occupancy`, which asks whether a quiet guard should exist; this is neither a match nor a guard. Specimen with a receipt, repaired on main by gunbc#9829: the required floor asked `extdeps.git` `git.Core.DiffUnified0` (`git diff -U0 <range>`) which lines a change touched, and `floor_diff_edits_from_line_ranges` attributed those lines to enclosing declarations to decide which witness identities the change established. Git prints only the hunks that DIFFER, so on a rename it prints the module line and whatever else moved — while every identity at the destination is a newly qualified `module.function` that has never executed under that spelling, because the authored module name moved with the file. **THE REASON IT SURVIVED IS THAT THE TWO ANSWERS COINCIDE BY ACCIDENT IN THE COMMON CASE**: for a wholly-added file every line is a `+` line, so line attribution reaches every declaration and the defect is invisible; it appears only when the instrument gets TERSE, which rename detection is. Measured over the window reachable in a shallow session clone — a floor on the population, not the population, since older renames are unmeasured — 8 of 8 rename-destination `.dag` files carrying test decls were under-enrolled and at least 87 of their 103 identities were never selected; a PURE MOVE with no content edit enrolled NOTHING. It was not merely a reporting gap: changed-witness membership is what OVERRIDES the cost-debt withhold and the outside-gate suppression, so the same miss on a rostered identity is a silent decline of a witness whose author is present, which is the state `v2.workflow.floor_changed_witness` `changed_witness_execution_standing` exists to red. The repair derives the set from the subject: a path `parse_unified_diff_added_paths` already rules new-at-path contributes the declarations THE FILE ITSELF PARSES TO, bounded to that file s own decl list and never the corpus, so it is the precise answer rather than an absorbing widen. Its discriminating red is `rename_destination_enrolls_every_test_decl_not_only_the_diff_touched_one`, which feeds the specimen s verbatim diff and FAILS when the rule is disabled, paired with `in_place_modify_enrolls_only_the_touched_test_decl`, which passes under both states so the first cannot be satisfied by widening. **Recognition rule, and it predicts well beyond git**: whenever a set is read out of a tool s output, ask whether that tool promised completeness over YOUR question or over its own — `--stat`, any de-duplicating or truncating reporter, and any porcelain that elides for human brevity all report faithfully and none of them promise the consumer s set. **Its near twin is `executed_conjunct_discriminates_nothing`, approached from the other side**: there the satisfied signal is EXECUTION -- the conjunct genuinely runs and is genuinely gated on -- while here it is COMPLETENESS RELATIVE TO THE REPORTER, which is why this class is not a reachability problem and looking for an unexecuted path finds everything healthy. In both, the axis the diligent reader checks already reads green, and checking it harder returns the same green.)**THREE RECEIPTS FROM ONE SESSION (2026-09-01, XL-0T), ALL READ AS A GREEN OR A ZERO, AND THE THIRD IS THE ONE THIS ROW DID NOT ALREADY COVER.** (1) A remote build read as `ctrl-build --remote -- cargo check ... | tail -8`: the pipeline exits with TAIL's status, so it is zero whatever the compiler said, and the eight-line window it keeps is executor cleanup chatter carrying no line capable of holding an error. Absence of an error read as success AFTER every line that could contradict it had been discarded. (2) A regeneration wrapped in `/usr/bin/time -v` in a container where that binary does not exist: bash died at the wrapper, exited ZERO, the program never ran, and the subsequent `git diff` was EMPTY -- which reads exactly as `no drift`, the answer that would have RETRACTED a correct finding already registered as a prediction. (3) The floor's `[over-cost]` listing, from which order statistics were computed and reported as a distribution -- `median 390ms, p95 418ms, a dense band at 78-84% of the 500ms ceiling`. The listing is not the population and SAYS SO IN ITS OWN LAST LINE: `and 260 further row(s) over the 100ms line, not printed ... this list is the 25 most expensive, ranked on the declared cost basis`, against `over_cost_line_diagnostic=285`. The true shape was the OPPOSITE: one outlier 73ms clear of the field (501 refused, runner-up 428, the remaining 260 all at or below 337ms), and the gap claimed to be empty had a row sitting in it. **WHY THE THIRD IS THE SEVERE ONE AND WHY IT NEEDED THIS ROW RATHER THAN A NEW ONE.** The first two were filters the CONSUMER wrote, and the standing guard -- select on the result pattern, never on position -- catches both. The third was truncation the PRODUCER performed, for its own readability, and DECLARED IN BAND; there was no consumer-authored `head -N` to notice, so the guard does not fire and diligence along it returns green. That is this class exactly: the report was complete for the REPORTER's purpose and read as complete for the CONSUMER's, with provenance intact and the subject substituted. **GENERAL FORM, which extends the recognition rule above from completeness to ORDER: a ranked list is a VIEW, and a view's order statistics are not the population's.** A median or a percentile computed over a top-K is an order statistic of a truncated tail; it carries no trace of the truncation, it has the same shape and units as the real thing, and it is therefore strictly more dangerous than a wrong count, which at least invites the question `out of how many`. So: before computing ANY statistic over a tool's lines, find the producer's own statement of what it printed -- a `N further rows`, a `top K`, a `ranked by`, a total beside the listing -- and where the producer names a fuller artifact, THAT is the population and the log is a preview. **THE HARM WAS NOT TO THE AUTHOR'S OWN CONCLUSIONS**, which is what separates this receipt from the two beside it: the wrong distribution had already been relayed to a lane authoring a DECLARED RUNG DROP whose population is stated as a function of headroom against observed inflation. At the measured 8-11% a row tips only from a baseline near 450ms, so the true at-risk population was ONE row -- the 501 itself -- and the relayed number would have made it twenty-five, inside the one artifact whose entire value is that its population is honest, and in the direction that makes a class look larger, which is the direction nobody audits.)**FOURTH RECEIPT, AND IT WAS FOUND INSIDE THE INSTRUMENT BEING USED TO WRITE THE REPORT ABOUT THIS CLASS (2026-09-01, same session).** `v1.tests.claim.carrier_realization_census`, run over 1320 `.dag` files, prints `subjects=1320` -- and 1320 is the count of FILES INGESTED, not modules CENSUSED. Only 845 modules produce rows; 475, or 36 percent, are silently omitted. The omissions are not obscure: `v2.compiler.parse` is absent, and `v2.std.compilers.lexing` is absent while its siblings `body_lowering`, `cli_surface`, `sugar` and `semantic_decl_emission` are all present, so it is neither a test-module filter nor obvious unreachability. A consumer reads `subjects=` as coverage, which is this class exactly: the count is complete for the REPORTER's purpose -- files it accepted -- and was read as complete for the CONSUMER's, which is modules it examined. **THE SELECTION RULE IS DELIBERATELY NOT STATED HERE**: it was not determined, and guessing it would put a fabricated mechanism in a ledger row, which is worse than an incomplete one. **WHAT IT COST, MEASURED RATHER THAN IMAGINED.** A fixture authored specifically to settle an unrelated question landed in the omitted 475 and produced ZERO rows -- including its own positive control. Its emptiness was exactly what the hypothesis under test predicted, so without the control it would have been reported as confirmation of a hypothesis on evidence containing none. The absence-versus-zero distinction this class names reproduced itself INSIDE the test built to settle it, which is the practical lesson: catching a pattern does not inoculate you against it, and the exposure is highest immediately after being careful. **THE RESOLUTION-AXIS FORM, MEASURED 2026-09-02 (bold-stag-665 specimen i, bold-carp-449 specimen ii): THE INSTRUMENT RESOLVED THE REFERENCE TO SOMETHING ADJACENT TO THE INTENDED SUBJECT AND REPORTED FAITHFULLY ABOUT WHAT IT RESOLVED TO.** The adjacency is a different VERSION or a different SPELLING; and NEITHER specimen here is temporal, which is the clearest statement of where the boundary against `stable_citation_mutable_referent` falls: the time axis went there with the attempt case. Specimen (i) reads one fixed branch against one fixed main, specimen (ii) one fixed tree against one fixed staged copy at one instant; both would answer identically tomorrow, and their adjacency is ANCESTRY and NAMING rather than time.** The forms above substitute WHAT the instrument covered for what the subject contains. Here the instrument resolves to a DIFFERENT VERSION or a DIFFERENT NAME for the same subject, and its answer carries no mark of having done so. **THE TEETH: EVERY RUNG RETURNS A WELL-FORMED PLAUSIBLE ANSWER RATHER THAN AN ERROR, SO THE WRONG ANSWER DOES NOT LOOK LIKE A FAILURE, IT LOOKS LIKE DATA.** `+76/-31` reads as a change; `five functions missing` reads as a deletion. Each arrives with a mechanism ready to explain it, which is what makes it persuasive rather than merely wrong. **THE LADDER, WHICH IS THE TRANSFERABLE PART -- EVERY RUNG BUT THE LAST IS POSITIONAL, AND EACH GOES RED FOR A DIFFERENT IRRELEVANT REASON:** line-diff is red on a REWRITE; identifier-diff is red on a RENAME; concept-mapping is the only test that decides. Identifier-absence is not the cure for line-absence, it is the SAME positional test one level up -- **absence of a NAME is not absence of a CAPABILITY, and only the second is a loss.** **SPECIMEN (i), the line rung -- an ancestry the merge destroyed.** Under SQUASH merge the squashed commit is not an ancestor of the branch, so `git diff --stat origin/main...origin/session/<branch>` re-renders ALREADY-LANDED work as unlanded: gunbc#10100 rendered 3 files, +76/-31, for content wholly present on main. The deciding test is content -- `git merge-tree --write-tree origin/main <branch>` against `git rev-parse origin/main^{tree}`, identical shas meaning the merge is a no-op. A peer hit the same rendering on gunbc#9995 and reported a no-op as a 5313-line partial revert. **SPECIMEN (ii), the identifier rung -- a rename read as a deletion (bold-carp-449, whose session is archived, so this row is the specimen's only durable form).** A worktree held staged residue: `prose_citation_census.dag` staged at 576 lines against main's 802, with 56 lines staged-only; its witness test 245 against 461, 23 staged-only. The first read reasoned from SIZE to CONTENT -- main is 226 lines further along, so these must be earlier phrasings -- which is unsound because a file can grow 226 lines while independently dropping 56. The token test that replaced it pulled distinctive identifiers from each staged-only line and grepped main: 25 of 56 and 15 of 23 were ABSENT, INCLUDING FIVE WHOLE FUNCTIONS AND FOUR WHOLE TEST FNS, which reads as a substantial deletion. IT WAS ENTIRELY RENAMES. Every construct exists in main under different wording -- staged `cross_file: Bool` became main's `ProseCitationLocality = InRetiringFile | InAnotherFile | RetiringFileAmbiguous`; `prose_citation_baseline_row` became `prose_citation_baseline_reading`; `a_citation_in_the_retiring_file_is_not_cross_file` became `a_citation_in_the_retiring_file_is_local_to_it`. **TWO WERE STRICTLY STRONGER IN MAIN** -- the `Bool` became a three-arm coproduct expressing the several-paths case a `Bool` cannot, and main carried an entire second axis the staged copy lacked -- so the instrument reported a LOSS where the truth was a GAIN. What settled it was reading main's type and function definitions and asking which CONCEPT each staged construct maps onto. **RECOGNITION RULE, COVERING BOTH, AND IT MUST FIRE AT READING TIME RATHER THAN AT RETRACTION TIME:** before reading an ABSENCE or a DIFFERENCE as a fact about the subject, establish that the instrument resolved to the subject you meant -- the same version, the same concept -- because every cheap test available resolves to something adjacent and says nothing about having done so. **THE NEAR-MISSES ARE PART OF THE RECEIPT AND ARE WHY THIS IS FILED AT ALL.** Neither reached a wrong decision and each halted one step short: the no-op diffstat was settled by tree sha before anyone acted on reopening; the deletion was settled by concept-mapping before anything was restored. A class whose specimens each halt one step from harm names exactly what the recognition rule has to beat. **BOUNDED AGAINST THE FOUR NEAREST ROWS, and these boundaries are the justification for a form here rather than a fourth authority.** `receipt_names_a_property_not_the_tree_it_holds_of` is a RECEIPT that was valid and EXPIRED when its base moved, repaired by re-deriving against the new base; here nothing expires -- every reading is live and true of its own subject, and the defect is manufactured by JOINING two of them. `positional_citation` is an authored HANDLE that decays and fails by matching zero; here the handle keeps matching perfectly and returns a confident answer about something else -- and note the sharper relation, that specimen (ii) shows an identifier GREP is itself a positional test, so `positional_citation` names the decaying-pointer half while this row names the unsound-absence half. `execution_provenance_loss` asks whether the observation ran; here it ran and reported faithfully. `stable_citation_mutable_referent` is the boundary that decides what this form does NOT carry, and it is the sharpest of the four because the two look identical at the call site -- both are a confident well-formed answer about a subject the reader did not mean. **THE TEST THAT SEPARATES THEM IS NOT WHETHER THEY FEEL DIFFERENT BUT WHETHER THE REPAIRS TRANSFER: does a reader who correctly diagnoses one perform the RIGHT FIX for the other? No -- and each fix is actively wrong applied to the other, which is why this is a boundary rather than a framing.** There the identifier resolves to its own correct subject and the WORLD replaces the content served underneath it, so the reader's test was right and re-reading CONFIRMS the wrong answer; the repair is to pin the citation to something that cannot be replaced -- an attempt number, a sha, an immutable artifact. Here nothing is replaced and nothing moves: every reading is live and would be identical tomorrow, the reader's TEST is what resolved to the neighbouring question, and the repair is to change the test rather than the citation -- line-diff to identifier-diff to concept-mapping. Crossed over, both fixes fail: PIN a squash-merge two-dot and you have pinned a merge base that was always the wrong one, and CONCEPT-MAP two attempts and you conclude a migration is real without ever establishing the two readings share a subject. That is why the ATTEMPT axis is not in the list above and why the job-id receipt is filed there rather than here: an attempt genuinely was replaced, which is that class and not this one. All of them share provenance-intact subject substitution, which is why this is a FORM of this row and not a new one.) **RECEIPT (1) IS NOW MEASURED RATHER THAN ASSERTED, AND A NAMED TOOL IS CLEARED BY THE SAME CONTROL (2026-09-03, vivid-ibex-751).** Receipt (1) above states that a `| tail` pipeline exits with tail's status; it was true and it was prose. The discriminating pair, one line apart on one command: `ctrl-build --local -- cargo check -Z definitely-not-a-real-flag 2>&1 | tail -2` reports rc=0 with PIPESTATUS[0]=101, and the same command unpiped reports rc=101. A positive control and a red on a fixed subject, so the mechanism is now established by execution rather than by description. THE EXONERATION IS THE PART THIS ROW DID NOT CARRY. On the strength of the piped reading, `ctrl-build` was reported to a manager as a fleet-wide false-green generator -- a build wrapper whose exit status did not reflect its compile -- and was one message from being filed as a defect against a shared tool. THE UNPIPED CONTROL CLEARS IT: the wrapper reports the compile's status correctly and always did. The 0 was tail succeeding at printing the failure. That is the first receipt here where a named component was ACCUSED and then CLEARED, and it is recorded beside the accusation deliberately: fabricated debt against a blameless component costs more than the misreading that produced it, because it sends every other lane hunting in the wrong place, and a clearing that lives only in a chat thread does not reach them. The three remedies are not interchangeable: `set -o pipefail` and reading PIPESTATUS[0] both work and both require the author to remember at the exact moment they are not thinking about it; NOT PIPING THE THING WHOSE STATUS YOU NEED is the only unconditionally safe one. AND THE MISATTRIBUTION SURVIVED A WRITTEN NOTE OF THIS EXACT FACT. What stopped it was being asked to file the row: a failure-mode row must name its DISTINGUISHING FACT, and that is the one field that cannot be filled without measuring. **FIFTH RECEIPT, AND IT IS THE SECOND HIT ON THE SAME ARTIFACT (2026-09-03, calm-boar-314).** The required-floor log prints, for a witness interrupted by the cpu deadline, both `cpu_at_least=503ms/500ms` and the sentence `interrupt_point=503ms is where the poll observed the ceiling; every interrupted row reports approximately whatever ceiling interrupted it, so that figure is a property of the BUDGET and not of this row -- do not compare it with the budget or with another row's`, together with `ABOVE 500ms with NO UPPER BOUND (a bound, not a near miss: it is wrong as a cost by an unbounded amount, always low)`. A 926KB log was downloaded, grepped for the blocking populations, and three figures transcribed into margins of 0.6, 0.8 and 5.4 percent against a 500ms ceiling -- percentages computed against a sentence forbidding that comparison, printed on the same line as the figures. Only one of the three was a margin at all; the other two were interrupt points, and a cost unbounded above is INDISTINGUISHABLE THROUGH THIS INSTRUMENT from the step change the reading was offered to rule out, so the conclusion was not merely unsupported but unfalsifiable from the evidence held. **What makes it the second hit rather than a first: the same log's `[over-cost]` listing had already produced the truncated-order-statistics receipt above, so this artifact had already taught this lesson once, to the same reader, on the same file.** The structural argument the margins were decorating -- that a prose string in a ledger row has no path to the CPU cost of an emit test -- was sound alone, which is the harm: the numbers added the appearance of a measured claim to an argument that was already better without them. **WHAT THE TWO 2026-09-03 RECEIPTS SAY TOGETHER, co-signed by both lanes and stated in the form that survives the difference between them.** Two lanes, one hour, unrelated subjects, both reading a STATUS or a FIGURE as a fact about a subject the instrument was not reporting on -- and in the one case where a guard rail WAS printed in band, it did not hold. The narrowing is deliberate and was calm-boar-314's: the strong form -- that both read past an in-band guard rail -- is true of the margin receipt, whose forbidding sentence sat on the line the figures were extracted from, and FALSE of the status receipt, where the `tail` window had already DISCARDED every contradicting line. Nothing was there to read past; the guard rail was absent, not ignored. This row already keeps those apart as consumer-authored versus producer-declared truncation, and the co-signed claim is written so it does not need the weaker case to be stronger than it was. **SECOND SPECIMEN, AND IT MOVES THE REPORTER PROPERTY FROM SCOPE TO VINTAGE** (eager-pike-541, 2026-09-03). The instrument was the locally built `claim_executor`; it ran and answered its own question correctly, and its question was asked under the semantics of an OLDER corpus than the tree under test. After merging a main that had added new compiler seed files, the floor refused with `function parsed_import_statements not found in scope` before any claim executed and reported zero passing claims, whose surface reading is that the branch broke the corpus. It had not; the binary predated the function. THE COMPLETENESS AXIS IS THE SAME AND THE REPORTER PROPERTY IS DIFFERENT -- above, the report was complete for the reporter's SCOPE rather than the subject's; here it was correct for the reporter's VERSION rather than the subject's, which is why this is a specimen of this row rather than a new one. **THE RECEIPT IS THE DESTRUCTIVE ARM**: the same stale binary, asked to regenerate projections, DELETED three live rows from DESIGN.md -- a merged rung drop and two failure modes belonging to other lanes -- while the .dag authorities still declared all three, because a projection generated by an instrument that cannot see a row renders its absence as authoritative. THE GENERATED-ARTIFACT GATE CANNOT CATCH THIS: it compares the projection to its authority and reports clean once both sides agree on the loss, so only the instrument's vintage separates a projection from a deletion. The operative rule is that a local refusal measured against a MOVED main is not evidence about the branch until the instrument is rebuilt against that main.**THE NEXT-RUNG FORMULATION FOR THE RESOLUTION AXIS, AND THE ROW THAT DISSOLVED INTO IT (2026-09-03).** This form was drafted as a separate class, `contingent_instrument_coincidence_read_as_equivalence`, and filed as its own row before the repair-crossing test above was run against it. IT DOES NOT SURVIVE THAT TEST, and running it deliberately rather than arguing the names is what decided this, so the reasoning is recorded here for the next author who arrives with the same shape. THE PROPOSED CLASS: a standing evidence binding from a PROXY INSTRUMENT P to a TARGET QUESTION Q, holding only inside an UNOWNED SUBJECT CONDITION C, where inside C the answers agree and outside it they diverge with the instrument's output identical either way. CROSSING ONE, does this row's repair close that class: applying ESTABLISH THAT THE INSTRUMENT RESOLVED TO THE SUBJECT YOU MEANT to a two-dot comparison means checking whether the base you diff from IS the merge base, which is checking C exactly. It closes. CROSSING TWO, does the warrant repair close this row's specimens: for specimen (ii)'s identifier-absence read as capability-absence there IS a condition -- no renames occurred -- so a warrant demanding it refuses the unsound reading. It closes too. Both repairs close both classes, and DESIGN section 4b keys rows on the repair, so this is ONE class and the separate row was a second authority for a fact this row already owned. **WHAT THE DISSOLVED ROW CONTRIBUTES, AND IT IS NOT NOTHING: A NAME FOR THE CONDITION AND A CEILING ARGUMENT THIS ROW LACKED.** The recognition rule above fires at READING TIME and asks a reader to establish resolution; it has no vocabulary for a resolution that is CORRECT NOW AND LAPSES LATER, because C is stateful while naming a subject is static. The rungs enumerated above are all POSITIONAL and all repaired by a better reader. C is repaired by a CONSTRUCTION: state P, Q, C, the population, and C'S STANDING -- which authority asserts it and what happens when it lapses. Where C's standing resolves to review, memory, habit, or a merge somebody did earlier, it is UNOWNED, and an unowned condition lapses through OTHER PEOPLE'S CORRECT WORK, which is the hardest kind to notice because the mechanism that ends it is indistinguishable from ordinary progress. **ONE SENTENCE OF THE DRAFT WAS WRONG AT EVIDENCE GRAIN AND THE CORRECTION IS THE USEFUL PART.** It said the backing is ACCIDENTAL. It is not: the observation genuinely backs P, faithfully and completely, exactly as this row's opening insists. It supplies NO backing whatever for Q absent an equivalence warrant. What is accidental is THE AGREEMENT OF THE ANSWERS -- and P HAPPENED TO EQUAL Q is not P WAS EVIDENCE FOR Q. Collapsing those two is the same substitution this row names, performed on the word EVIDENCE instead of on a set. **THE CEILING IS 4 AND IT IS DEMONSTRATED IN THIS REPOSITORY TODAY, WHICH IS WHY IT IS NOT ASPIRATION.** `gunbc.diff_baseline`'s `DiffBaseline` arm `ExactReplayBoundary` carries `observed_relation: GitObservedMergeBase` beside its base and head object identities, produced by `gunbc.git_diff_change_window`, consumed through `v2.workflow.floor_diff_observe`, with enrolled witnesses in `dag/test/claim/diff_baseline_witness_test.dag`. Its own note states the rule: the comparison is direct two-dot and NOT triple-dot BECAUSE THE PRODUCER ALREADY CLAIMS THE MERGE-BASE BOUNDARY. That field IS C, carried in the binding's own type, so the two-dot answer cannot be minted without the warrant that makes it answer Q. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, AND THE PAIRING IS THE WHOLE TRIGGER: a subject-bound typed equivalence warrant TOGETHER WITH an enumerating consumer over instrument-to-question bindings. The construction half exists today for exactly ONE pair, in one module; nothing ranges over such bindings asking which carry a warrant. Naming either half alone would satisfy the trigger while the capability stayed dead, which is the section 4b(3) defect. **THE ARGUMENT FOR THE PAIRING IS MADE BY THAT ASYMMETRY RATHER THAN BY ASSERTION: a repository can hold the correct construction and still be blind to the class, because a construction nobody enumerates protects exactly the one binding whose author happened to think of it.** **A FOURTH RESOLUTION-AXIS RECEIPT, CROSS-SECTIONAL RATHER THAN ANCESTRAL OR LEXICAL, contributed by tidy-swift-334 from their own retracted finding.** P was a success-and-failure partition of CI runs by HOST FAMILY; Q was stable per-family provisioning capability; the unheld condition was that transient runner state stayed fixed across the sample. The partition was published as a fleet-wide fact and was refuted when THE SAME HOST CHANGED SIDES while host identity stayed constant -- so the instrument had resolved to an adjacent subject, a few minutes of scheduling, and reported faithfully about that. It is the cleanest specimen of the form because the adjacency is neither version nor spelling but SAMPLING WINDOW, and because two disjoint sets of four hosts are exactly what an intermittent defect looks like when each side samples different minutes. **A SECOND INSTANCE IN A DIFFERENT SUBSYSTEM, carried compactly: the required floor's `planned=` delta read as proof that new witnesses ENROLLED.** Two arms, 2026-08-22, each cited by naming its producer -- `report_required_floor_outcome` over `v1_compiler.cli_run` `RequiredFloorOutcome`. Run 32553383403 at e82653fc1, two attempts of one sha on two runners, shows the counters agree. The commit pair af719cc6 and 951683ae, whose whole diff is one `.md` and ONE added line in `src/v2/lens/inert_carrier.dag`, shows that same `offered` counter MOVES while the decline counters stay identical, so the movement is new discovery and not re-classification. DETERMINISM IS NOT INDEPENDENCE: an instrument can be exactly reproducible per commit and still unable to isolate the change under study, and an author who verifies the first and concludes the second has checked the half that was never in doubt.
- **a conjunct that executes, is reached, is gated on, and still discriminates nothing** (a predicate wired into a live check runs on every call, sits inside a gate that really blocks, and yet deletes cleanly: remove it and every witness stays green, because no fixture ever constructs the population that would make it false. **It is invisible precisely BECAUSE reachability is satisfied.** The usual signature of an unenforced mechanism -- nothing calls it -- is absent, so every reachability-based tell reports health, and review reads the conjunct as a wall because it genuinely is evaluated.
