The fourth instance names `gh run view` as the escape from a refusing `gh api`; this instance names `gh api` as the escape from a silent `gh run view`. The two routes are each other's documented remedy and both failed on 2026-09-02, in OPPOSITE directions -- ninety-nine bytes of refusal on the PAYLOAD channel from the api route, zero bytes and NO refusal from run view -- so a reader following this row lands on whichever route happens to be broken that day. The two mechanical steps written above are route-dependent for one shared reason: each interrogates HOW THE INSTRUMENT REFUSED -- which stream the refusal travels on, what the exit status says -- and an instrument that emits no refusal at all answers neither question. Consulting the typed status here yields SUCCESS over an empty capture, which is precisely the DID NOT READ case wearing the FOUND NOTHING answer. ONLY THE SUBJECT-WEIGHT CHECK SURVIVES, and it survives because it interrogates the ARTIFACT rather than the instrument, so it is indifferent to which route broke. COROLLARY, and it is the operative addition: when the weight is implausible, RE-READ BY THE OTHER ROUTE -- not because the other route is trustworthy, it demonstrably is not, but because 442,248 bytes against zero for one subject is a DISCRIMINATING COMPARISON, where either number read alone is not a measurement of anything.)  COROLLARY CORRECTED, 2026-09-04, SAME REPOSITORY SAME DAY, BECAUSE THE SENTENCE ABOVE CAN BE READ AS NOMINATING A ROUTE AND THAT READING WOULD HAVE BEEN WRONG WITHIN HOURS. The fifth instance found `gh run view --job --log` returning ZERO while the API returned 442,248 bytes, which invites the rule re-read by the API. The inverse was then measured on three failing jobs of one run: `gh api .../actions/jobs/<id>/logs` returned ZERO BYTES for all three, while `gh run view --job <id> --log` returned 182,047, 871,334 and 6,171 bytes. NEITHER ROUTE IS THE RELIABLE ONE. THE SYMMETRIC FORM, which is what this row now asserts: a zero-byte read is UNOBSERVED whichever route produced it, and the remedy is THE OTHER ROUTE, in whichever direction you started. The discriminating comparison is what carries the evidence -- one number against another for the same subject -- never the identity of the route that happened to answer. AND A NON-EMPTY CAPTURE CAN ALSO BE PARTIAL, so the weight check is not only a test for suspiciously SMALL. One retry returned 1,048,662 bytes, which is 1 MiB plus 86 -- a cap plus a header or a partial final record, and FRONT-TRUNCATED, so the earliest lines were gone while the capture looked large and healthy. Note that a test for a suspiciously ROUND number would have MISSED it: 1,048,662 is not 1,048,576, and equality against a power of two fails. The tell is a value sitting JUST PAST a plausible limit rather than on it. RECORDED BECAUSE OF HOW IT WAS FOUND: the author was capturing log evidence ABOUT THIS VERY CLASS when the API returned zero bytes, and came within one report of writing no matching lines found -- which would have been this class committed while documenting itself. That is the strongest available evidence that it recurs, and it is why the rule has to be mechanical rather than remembered. SIXTH INSTANCE, 2026-09-04, import-cut lane, AND IT HARDENS A DIFFERENT HALF THAN THE FIFTH. The fifth hardens the READ -- an implausible capture weight, re-read by the other route. This one hardens the VERDICT ARM, and it is worth separating because a perfectly honest read can still be turned into a false green by how the arm is written. THE SPECIMEN IS AN INSTRUMENT BUILT THAT AFTERNOON TO GUARD AGAINST THIS EXACT CLASS. A watcher polled CI for a green run whose floor had actually REACHED the parse phase, because a floor that aborts early silences later phases and the silence reads as a pass. Its verdict arm fetched the floor job log and grepped for FAILED PHASE and adjudication refusals. On an empty log -- a cancelled job, an unretained log, or precisely the zero-byte read the fifth instance documents -- grep matched nothing, and the arm would have reported a candidate green run carrying no failures. The author would have delivered a release recommendation derived from a log never read. THE RULE, and it is stronger than sizing the capture at the reading site: A VERDICT WHOSE GREEN ARM IS THE ABSENCE OF BAD LINES CANNOT DISTINGUISH HEALTHY FROM UNREAD. Checking the capture helps only where the capture is checkable; the fifth instance already shows a route where the failure emits no refusal to interrogate, so the two mechanical steps are circular there. Requiring POSITIVE evidence is not route-dependent: the arm must find something that can only be present when the subject was genuinely observed. The repaired watcher requires the literal `phase parse (` announcement to be FOUND at least once before it will call anything a candidate, and prints UNOBSERVED with a reason at four separate points -- no such job, job conclusion not success, an EMPTY steps array, or an empty log. WHY THE REPAIR GENERALISES BEYOND CAPTURES: the same shape is the heartbeat requirement on a deletion census. When a check PASSES by observing that things are gone, an instrument that observed NOTHING produces a perfect pass, so one known item must be held outside the deleted population and required present. Absence-as-success and absence-of-failure-as-success are the same defect, and positive evidence is the same repair.)
- **an unhandled state falls to the NOT-YET arm, so a settled answer is reported as a pending one** (a fold reduces a producer's conclusion set to a verdict with an arm per state it knows and a default for the rest, and the default it falls to is NOT-YET-TERMINAL. A state the producer really can emit, but the fold has no arm for, is therefore rendered as STILL WAITING -- and waiting is the one disposition that never resolves, because nothing further is coming. THE DISGUISE IS THE WHOLE DEFECT: an unhandled state normally announces itself by producing a wrong answer, and this one produces NO answer, which is indistinguishable from the fold working correctly over a subject that has not finished. SPECIMEN, 2026-09-02, gunbc PR 10086: `dashboard-ops reviews` reported ready:false with requirement `checks` failing and checks_state `pending`, AT THE SAME INSTANT that GitHub reported the head CLEAN and mergeStateStatus admitted a merge -- and the merge was then taken on GitHub's word, with no override, and was correct. Every job in the run had a terminal conclusion: five `pass` and one `skipping`. The skip is the unhandled state -- a GitHub NEUTRAL, which is a THIRD conclusion beside success and failure -- and the readiness fold, carrying arms for the first two, dropped it into not-yet-terminal. THE MECHANISM IS NOT LAG AND THAT IS THE POINT: the two are the same field with the same value, and only one of them is discharged by waiting. RECOGNITION RULE, and it is the load-bearing part because the status field cannot answer it: when a readiness or aggregate verdict says NOT YET, do not wait on the verdict -- NAME THE SPECIFIC ROW THAT IS NON-TERMINAL AND ASK WHETHER IT CAN EVER BECOME TERMINAL. A row already sitting in a terminal state the fold does not recognise never will, so patience is not a remedy for it and no amount of it produces new information. The falsifier is cheap and available at every such moment: read the producer's own per-row conclusions beside the fold's summary. If every row is terminal and the summary is not, the summary has an arm missing. WHY THIS LOCATION IS WORSE THAN A LOG READER, which is the reason it is ranked rather than merely filed: this is the instrument that decides whether WORK IS DONE. A log reader that under-reports costs a diagnosis and the next reader recovers it; a readiness fold that under-reports costs the MERGE, and it fails in the direction that LOOKS LIKE CAUTION, so nobody overrides it and nobody is embarrassed by it. THE COUNTERFACTUAL IS WHAT MAKES THE ROW ACTIONABLE: a reader following the dashboard alone would still be waiting on a pull request that had been mergeable for an hour -- and would read their own patience as diligence, which is the one failure mode that generates no complaint from the person suffering it. WHY THIS IS NOT `empty_capture_read_as_clean_result`, its nearest neighbour and the class the same session had just extended: that class is about a CAPTURE that is empty, where the reader holds no observation and believes they hold one. Here the observation is complete and correct at every input -- five passes and a skip, all present, all read -- and the loss is in the REDUCTION over them. Merging the two would repeat exactly the defect that produced this row's neighbour, a recognition rule too broad to catch a second instance of its own subject: `size the artifact before grepping it` is the surviving rule there and it is silent here, because the artifact is the right size and says the right thing. WHY THIS IS NOT `state_space_conflation`, the honest neighbour on the other side: all three of that class's forms are defects in a domain THIS TREE MODELS -- an `Option` overloaded past two meanings, a prose dichotomy over three states, a real state carried as an ad-hoc spelling -- and each of their recognition rules ends in ADD THE CONSTRUCTOR OR THE ARM. That repair is unavailable here: the conclusion set is GitHub's and the fold is the dashboard's, neither of which this repository owns, so the actionable rule has to be one a READER can execute against an instrument they cannot edit. That is why the rule above is a discriminator rather than a modelling instruction, and it is the whole reason this is its own row. RUNG FOUND AT: silent wrongness for any consumer of the summary field alone, since the fold reports a value that is not merely imprecise but WRONG about whether the subject can still change, and offers no signal that an input was unrecognised. It is mitigatable for a reader who applies the rule above, which is diligence rather than a mechanism. CEILING: outside the modeled guarantee for the dashboard's own fold, which this repository does not own and can only observe; for the equivalent folds gunbc DOES own, structurally impossible, because a reduction over a CLOSED conclusion set is exactly the case DESIGN section 4's exhaustive match makes unwritable -- a state with no arm fails to compile rather than defaulting. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER for the part we own: every gunbc fold over a third-party status or conclusion set ingesting that set as a closed coproduct in `extdeps/` with a variant per documented state, so an unmodeled state is a typed, located refusal at the boundary rather than a silent fall-through -- and NOT a default arm that maps the unknown to pending, which is this row's defect re-authored one layer down.)
- **a salience instrument blind to the record it sizes** (a change's SIZE IN THE DIFF is read to decide how much attention it deserves, and for one class of artifact that number is wrong by orders of magnitude, so a substantive edit is allocated a trivial edit's scrutiny. The instrument is not merely inaccurate, it is a SALIENCE instrument -- read FIRST, to decide where to look -- which is why the error does damage a plainly wrong number would not: nobody checks a figure they have already used to decide the thing is not worth checking. SPECIMEN, 2026-09-02, gunbc#10044: `git diff --stat -- dag/gunbc/rung_drop.dag` reported `1 insertion(+), 1 deletion(-)` for an edit adding 15,286 bytes, because every `RungDrop` in that corpus is ONE VERY LONG LINE. Three consecutive independent reviews described that hunk as a small wording tweak; a fourth, earlier one attributed it to `direct_call_arg_seam_v2_exemption`, A ROW THE DIFF DOES NOT TOUCH, because git's `@@` header names the declaration PRECEDING the hunk and the edited record is a single line. So one representation produced two distinct review failures -- under-sizing and misattribution -- and n=3 on the first within one pull request. THE APPROVALS WERE NOT WRONG ON WHAT THEY READ: each verdict is defensible over the prose rows the same diff carries, which render normally. What the count concealed is that the PR had three approvals and NO review coverage of the half that carried the receipt. A review tally is a claim about attention, and this instrument silently redirects attention before any reviewer forms a judgment. RUNG FOUND AT: silent wrongness, which is not a rung -- the misallocation leaves no trace, produces a green, and is indistinguishable from a reviewer who looked and found nothing. CEILING: mechanically preventable. The size of a record's diff can be made to track the size of its content, which is a representation question and decidable; it does not reach structural impossibility because nothing stops a future record from being authored as one long line again. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, AND IT IS DELIBERATELY NOT 'REVIEWERS SHOULD LOOK HARDER': a representation in which a record's diff size tracks its content size -- the long-line record broken across lines so a diff has hunks proportional to the change, or a review surface that reads the generated projection (`docs/design-failure-modes.md` renders the same content as prose and diffs legibly) rather than the `.dag` line. 'Look harder' is advice, and advice is what a class gets when nobody wants to pay for the fix; it also cannot be discharged, since every reviewer meets the same instrument. RECOGNITION RULE: when a review's description of a hunk is smaller than the hunk, check whether the artifact's line structure and the change's content structure agree. Where one record is one line, EVERY size signal derived from lines -- the diffstat, the hunk header, a line-count budget, a review-effort heuristic -- is answering about the file's shape rather than the change's. The tell is a reviewer citing the enclosing declaration rather than the edited one.)
- **ambient process state read by a concurrent reader** (a fact that is really a PARAMETER is instead read from a mutable cell the whole process shares -- the working directory, an env var, a global -- while the runtime schedules concurrent readers of it, so the answer a reader gets depends on which writer last ran and the wrong answer is SILENT: the read succeeds, returns a well-formed value, and is simply about a different subject than the reader meant. INVALID STATE: any reader resolving a relative name against ambient state that a concurrent writer may move mid-read. WHY IT SURVIVES REVIEW: each site is locally correct -- it sets the state to the right value before its own work and restores it after -- and the defect exists only in the PRODUCT of sites, which no single diff shows. SPECIMEN (PR #10024, v1_compiler.cli_run): 38 forward set_current_dir sites in one test binary that cargo runs multi-threaded, with the idiom prior = current_dir(); chdir(ws); work; chdir(prior) -- so one test's RESTORE hands the cwd back while a sibling is mid-read. The victim population is not the writers, it is every non-ignored test in the binary that resolves a relative path during the window; the file's own entry_admission_tests note already records the measurement, a DIFFERENT pair of tests failing on each run of identical code (4/1 then 3/2). Seven of the sites were reachable from the required unit lane and in all seven the chdir was DEAD -- every consumer already took an explicit root -- i.e. residue from an unfinished parameterisation, which is the expected shape once the migration is half done. THE CENSUS THAT MATTERS IS REACHABILITY, NOT THE LITERAL AND THE RANGE THAT MATTERS IS THE POPULATION'S, NOT THE FILE'S: the first cut of that gate scanned the one file its author was editing while declaring the whole lane as its subject, which is a selection view read as a population and puts the reported rung above the executed evidence (DESIGN section 4b(1) reads a class's rung as the MINIMUM across in-scope paths). Widening it to every source file of the lane's crate cost a change of input plus a linear-time call extractor -- the per-name matcher is quadratic in lines times declarations and does not survive the wider corpus -- and it surfaced a second trap immediately: resolving cross-file call edges by BARE NAME merges every declaration of a shared spelling, and this crate declares main 18 times, so one production seed bridged into hundreds of unrelated tests. That arm is the absorbing fallback again, in the guard built to catch it. What survives is intra-file edges plus cross-file edges only for names declared exactly once, with the ambiguous remainder pinned by IDENTITY rather than assumed empty. Six forms of the same defect were found in this one guard; they are rostered as their own class at check_subject_narrower_than_its_declared_claim, which this row is a specimen of rather than an authority for: all seven reached the mutation through a helper, so a body-local grep for the call is green over every instance of the class and stays green the moment a writer is moved one call deeper -- the guard must close transitively over call edges. A SECOND, DISTINCT ARM, kept apart because it has a different remedy: memoizing a value DERIVED from the ambient state (workspace_root's OnceLock over a .git-ancestor walk from cwd) freezes whichever writer won the race for the whole process. On this specimen that arm's population is EMPTY -- every forward site targets the checkout root and 37 of 38 force the memo from the pristine cwd one line above their own call -- so it is reported UNSUPPORTED rather than disproven (see a_structural_possibility_is_not_an_occurring_phenomenon); ONE chdir to a non-checkout path arms it, and a fixture that runs git init is such a path. RUNG: found BELOW the ladder, since a redirected read is a silently wrong answer with no typed refusal anywhere. CEILING 4, structurally impossible, and decidable: state passed as a parameter has no shared cell to race on, so the invalid state has no constructor. The intermediate rung is a reachability gate over the guarded population (no_gating_test_reaches_a_process_cwd_mutator), which keeps the state ABSENT but not unwritable; its next-rung trigger is the capability that every reader in the population resolves from an explicitly passed root, after which the ambient write has no consumer and the call can be removed rather than merely counted. REVIEW TELL: a diff that sets shared process state and restores it, in code the runtime may run concurrently -- the restore is the tell, because it is what makes the site look self-contained.
- **a predicate vacuously true on an empty domain** (a universally-quantified check -- `all`, `every`, `iter().all(..)`, a for-loop that only ever narrows a flag, a SQL NOT EXISTS -- is satisfied by the IDENTITY ELEMENT when its domain is empty, so the check answers TRUE about a population it never examined. INVALID STATE: a guard whose verdict is read as evidence while its domain is empty, with nothing establishing that the domain was non-empty. WHY IT SURVIVES REVIEW, and this is what separates it from an ordinary off-by-one: the code READS as a check, it EXECUTES, it is REACHED, and on every input a reviewer imagines the domain is non-empty and the predicate genuinely discriminates. The vacuous case is not a value in the data -- IT IS PRODUCED BY A BOUNDARY, so it appears only at the end of a line, the end of a buffer, a remainder shorter than the window, an empty selection, the last chunk. Those are exactly the inputs a hand-written fixture omits, and exactly the inputs a real corpus eventually contains. SPECIMEN (PR #10033, measured): a raw-string scanner tested its closing delimiter with `chars[i+1..].iter().take(hashes).all(|h| *h == '#')`. Over a remainder shorter than `hashes` that iterator yields fewer items and `all` is vacuously TRUE, so a quote near end-of-line closes a hash-delimited raw string that is still open, and the rest of the fixture is handed back to the code scanner -- reintroducing the very brace-depth skew the raw-string handling existed to prevent, silently and in the UNDER-approximating direction. The repair is a length bound, not a different predicate: `i + 1 + hashes <= len` conjoined with the same `all`, which leaves the zero-hash case (a raw string with no hashes) trivially satisfied as it must be. RECOGNITION RULE, and it generalises past scanners: A PREDICATE THAT IS TRUE ON THE EMPTY CASE IS NOT A CHECK UNTIL ITS DOMAIN IS PROVEN NON-EMPTY. Ask of every universal: what does this return when the collection is empty, and can the boundary produce empty? If the answer is TRUE and YES, the domain bound is part of the predicate and its absence is the defect. BOUNDED AGAINST THREE NEIGHBOURS, none of whose recognition rules find it. `executed_conjunct_discriminates_nothing` is about a CORPUS that never constructs the falsifying population, so its remedy is a fixture; here the falsifying population is unconstructable at the boundary by the shape of the quantifier itself, and the remedy is a bound on the domain. `empty_observation_narrow` is an OBSERVATION that could not express what changed being rendered as a verdict -- one layer up, about evidence rather than about a predicate's truth value. `incidental_denominator_as_wall` is a guard that holds by a coincidence upstream that genuinely holds; here nothing holds at all, the guard simply returns TRUE. RUNG: found OUTSIDE the ladder, because the wrong answer is silent and well-formed -- the check reports satisfaction. CEILING 4 and decidable per-site: a windowed comparison whose window length is carried in its type cannot be asked about a short remainder. The intermediate rung is an executed control that exercises the BOUNDARY case specifically, asserted on a synthetic input rather than on the live corpus, so it keeps discriminating power when the corpus changes. **RECEIPT 2026-09-03 (jolly-ferret-412, gunbc#10141), AND THE EMPTINESS CAME FROM A PARSE DEFECT RATHER THAN AN EMPTY POPULATION.** Building the required floor's refused-carrier overlap wall, I joined every admitted cross-claim share key's observed consumer modules against each refused row's carriers to prove the wall would not red main, and reported all six keys clean against all four refused rows. The join captured nothing. The regex was `cache=... key=(\S+).*modules=(\S+)` and the ledger line carries `consumer_modules=53` BEFORE it carries `modules=a,b,c` -- `consumer_modules=` ends in `modules=`, so the non-greedy match took the COUNT and every intersection was against a set holding the single token `53`. Every key read clean because nothing was ever compared. The floor then refused on a real overlap standing in that same ledger, and in the main-vintage run I had also declared clean. **WHY THIS VARIANT MATTERS SEPARATELY: the domain was NOT empty.** The population existed, the stream was right, and the instrument took the wrong token out of it -- so every review question about whether the population might be empty answers reassuringly, and the emptiness is one field name away rather than anywhere a reader would look. Every ledger-joining instrument in this repository parses a field out of a rendered line, so the recurrence surface is the whole class of them. **RECOGNITION RULE FOR THIS VARIANT: anchor a field capture on its delimiter (` modules=`, never `modules=`), because any field name can be a SUFFIX of another; and before citing a join that found nothing, plant a positive -- intersect against a value you know is in the data and confirm it comes back.** A join whose only output is `clean` has not been shown to compare anything. **STRUCTURAL FIX, LANDED WITH THE SPECIMEN rather than only written down:** the wall now refuses `PureProducerShareObservedCarriersVacuous` when fills were recorded and not one carries a consuming module, so an empty observed domain is a typed refusal instead of a clean verdict, and its RED is enrolled beside the wall's own. The refused side of the same join was already asserted at decode -- a refused row with an empty carrier set refuses there, because it could never fire.**RECEIPT 2026-09-04 (vivid-ibex-751, gunbc#10311), THE `.is_empty()` FORM: A COMPLETION PREDICATE WHOSE EMPTINESS IS INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM ITS SATISFACTION.** This variant was first written as a separate row, `completion_predicate_satisfied_by_the_empty_population`, and that was a re-invention refused at review: `nothing outstanding` over a population of nothing is the SAME identity-element coincidence as a universal quantifier over an empty domain, and it has the same recognition rule and the same remedy. It is recorded here as a variant because the QUANTIFIER IS INVISIBLE IN IT -- there is no `all`, no `every`, no loop for a reader to interrogate, only a conjunction of `.is_empty()` terms, so the question this row tells you to ask has nothing syntactic to attach to. WHAT MAKES THE `.is_empty()` FORM WORSE THAN THE `all` FORM is that it fails toward DONE at exactly the moment someone is waiting: the population is empty precisely when work has just started or a head has just been pushed. SPECIMEN: `claim_executor` `required_floor_outcome_is_clean` is a conjunction of ELEVEN `.is_empty()` terms with no denominator anywhere in it, and zero planned claims satisfies all eleven and returns clean. THE ADJACENT SITE ALREADY REFUSES THE CLASS IN WRITING, which is what makes the pair the argument: `required_floor_runner`'s expected-red roster decode refuses `ExpectedRedRosterEmpty` at zero identities, stating that an empty roster makes the partition-sum and did-not-execute checks vacuous, and naming the shape as `a read failure quietly wearing the same face as success`. **THE PROTECTION CITED IN THE CODE IS TRUE AND VACUOUS IN EXACTLY THE CASE IT NAMES.** `run_required_floor` is documented as refusing when planned, executed and terminal identity counts disagree, `so a silently short roster cannot report as a pass`. At zero that invariant reads `0 == 0 + 0`: it holds, it is advertised as the cover for a short roster, and it is satisfied by the roster being absent entirely. STRUCTURAL VACUITY IS ESTABLISHED; REACHABILITY IS NOT -- whether any route drives the floor to a zero planned population is untraced, and a `FloorDiscoveryRefused` arm may close every one of them. THREE FURTHER RECEIPTS FROM LANE TOOLING IN ONE EVENING, ALL FAILING TOWARD DONE: a `case` partition over a check conclusion where running checks rendered EMPTY and the catch-all arm was TERMINAL, reporting SETTLED with five checks in progress; a monitor printing `pending=0 failing=[]` for a pull request whose rollup was empty, written an HOUR AFTER that first instance was written up and using the corrected predicate; and a watch whose condition was `no pending checks`, satisfied by NO CHECKS AT ALL on a just-force-pushed head, reporting CONCLUDED forty minutes early. **TWO OF THE THREE WERE WRITTEN AFTER THIS ROW EXISTED, WHICH IS WHAT DECIDES THE REMEDY.** Knowing the class did not protect the next instrument either author wrote, so the remedy does not belong in anyone's attention -- it belongs in the predicate. **RECEIPT 2026-09-05 (vivid-ibex-751, gunbc#10345), THE REGISTRATION FORM: THE EMPTY DOMAIN WAS THE SUBJECT'S OWN REGISTRATION, AND TWO PARTIES VERIFIED PROPERTIES OF IT WITHOUT EITHER CHECKING THAT IT EXISTED.** A merge took the base's roster verbatim and re-applied two identities on top; the re-application edited the WORKING TREE and was never staged, and a merge commits the INDEX, so the base's roster landed and both registrations were absent from the commit. The row files were present and unimported: 117 rostered against 119 row files. Seven verification joins ran and all seven passed. **THE PRIOR VARIANTS OF THIS ROW ARE MACHINE PREDICATES; THIS ONE IS A DELIBERATE MANUAL AUDIT BY TWO PARTIES, WHICH IS WHY IT IS RECORDED SEPARATELY.** There was no `all`, no `.is_empty()`, and no instrument to interrogate -- just two readers each enumerating a population that did not contain the missing thing. The author's joins asked whether every ROSTERED identity has a row file: true, and vacuous, because an unregistered row file is outside the roster, and the roster IS the population being enumerated. The reviewer's gate asked what identities the head ADDS to the roster do in the projection: true, and vacuous, because the added set was empty. **THE TWO AUDITS WERE COMPLEMENTARY AND BOTH LANDED ON THE SAME EMPTY SET FROM OPPOSITE SIDES, AND BOTH REPORTED CLEAN AND CONFIDENT -- THE CONFIDENCE IS THE TELL, NOT THE PASS.** **THE REMEDY FOR THE REGISTRATION FORM IS TWO INDEPENDENT ARMS, EITHER ONE SUFFICIENT, AND THE INCIDENT REQUIRED BOTH TO BE ABSENT -- WHICH IS THE USEFUL ENGINEERING STATEMENT RATHER THAN `AUDIT MORE CAREFULLY`.** The REVERSE JOIN would have caught it even measured on the wrong artifact: `comm -13 roster files` asks whether every ROW FILE is rostered, whose population is the files rather than the roster, so an unregistered file cannot hide in it. One direction finds rostered-with-no-file; only the other finds file-with-no-roster, and a roster-scoped check can never report the second. The INDEX READ would have caught it even in one direction: `git show :path` before committing and `git show <sha>:` after, because `git checkout <ref> -- <path>` STAGES while a subsequent edit does NOT, and a commit takes the index -- so a verification reading the working tree measures a CANDIDATE and not the SUBJECT, and on this path the two silently differed. **THE UNSTAGED EDIT IS AN ORDINARY SLIP THAT WILL RECUR FOREVER; WHAT MADE IT AN INCIDENT IS THAT NOTHING DOWNSTREAM COULD SEE IT.** The row is about the invisibility, not the omission, which is what decides where the remedy goes: into the predicate's DIRECTION and its SUBJECT, never into anyone's care. **NEXT TRIGGER FOR THIS VARIANT, NAMED AS THE CAPABILITY AND WITH WHAT IT MUST BE SUFFICIENT FOR:** every completion or coverage predicate on the required floor requires a POSITIVE DENOMINATOR, so a zero-size population refuses as NOT-YET-STARTED rather than passing as settled -- sufficient for no `FloorClean` verdict being derivable from an empty planned population. Adding one guard at `claims_planned` is the artifact-grained version and discharges nothing: it leaves the class alive at every predicate nobody has enumerated.
- **a check's subject is narrower than the claim it is cited for** (EVERY DEGREE OF FREEDOM IN A CHECK'S SUBJECT -- which call sites, which spellings, which lexical states, which domain, which files -- is a place its coverage can silently be narrower than the population it is read as covering. INVALID STATE: an executing, reached, green check whose declared subject is a strict superset of the population its implementation actually ranges over. RUNG: outside the ladder, because the gap is invisible from the check's own result -- a wall whose range is too narrow is green for the same reason a wall over a clean corpus is green, and DESIGN section 4b(1) reads a class's rung as the MINIMUM across its in-scope paths, so a check like this INFLATES the rung of everything it is cited for. THE ROUTE IS WHAT MAKES IT INVISIBLE: the narrowing is never a decision, it is the shape of the first implementation -- an author edits one file, so the scanner reads one file; the corpus spells the call one way, so the seed matches one spelling; the fixture has no raw strings, so the projection has no raw-string state; the window is never short, so the universal is never vacuous. Each is locally reasonable and none is written down, which is why the gap survives the review that reads the check's LOGIC and never asks what its INPUT was. SPECIMEN, and the reason this is a class rather than a bug: ONE guard in PR #10033 carried SIX instances -- (1) a body-local grep for the mutator, defeated by moving the call one helper deeper; (2) a seed matching only the fully-qualified path, defeated by `use std::env;`; (3) a lexical projection with no raw-string state, so an unbalanced brace in a fixture silently shifted every containment decision after it; (4) `take(n).all(..)`, vacuously true over a short remainder (rostered as predicate_vacuously_true_on_an_empty_domain); (5) a scan ranging over ONE FILE while the scope declaration claimed every test the required lane runs -- found by review, not by the author; and (6) after widening, cross-file call edges resolved by BARE NAME, which merges every declaration of a shared spelling (18 `main`s in that crate) and bridged one production seed into hundreds of unrelated tests. Four were found by the author, one by review, one by the widening itself, and the sixth is the mirror of the fifth: over-broad and under-broad are the same defect measured in opposite directions, and both are reported as coverage. RECOGNITION RULE: name the check's SUBJECT and the check's RANGE as two separate sentences, then ask what is in the first and not the second. If the answer is anything, the claim is the narrower one until the range is widened. The review-side tell is a scope comment that quantifies (`every test`, `all modules`, `any call site`) beside an implementation that names ONE input. BOUNDED AGAINST TWO NEIGHBOURS. selection_view_read_as_population is a set produced by a MEASUREMENT threshold and read as the population, arriving as the fix for a real objection; here nothing is measured and nothing is filtered -- the range was simply never as wide as the sentence beside it. incidental_denominator_as_wall is a guard that holds by an upstream coincidence that genuinely holds; here the guard holds over a population it never read. CEILING 3, structurally guaranteed and decidable, BUT SCOPED TO THIS CLASS'S EXECUTING FORMS AND NOT TO THE CLASS (scope amended when the ninth form landed): a check whose subject is a VALUE it receives rather than a scope its implementation re-derives cannot range over less than it claims, because the claim and the range are then one object. That derivation needs an executing check with an input. Every form above it is one, so the limit was never visible and the ceiling read as belonging to the class when it belongs to a sub-case: where the subject is a WRITTEN SURFACE a human is supposed to read completely, the claim and the range stay TWO objects and cannot inherit this ceiling. The ninth form below is that sub-case and carries no rung of its own. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, named as a capability: the guarded population is passed to the check as a constructed value carrying its own extent, sufficient that no check can name a subject its input does not contain. WHAT IS NOT CLAIMED: that widening always wins. Narrowing the DECLARATION to match the range is equally honest and is sometimes correct -- what is forbidden is leaving the two different, and the choice between them is a cost measurement, not a preference. On this specimen widening cost one change of input plus one linear-time rewrite of a quadratic edge builder, and the wider check ran FASTER than the narrow one, which is what settled it.) A SEVENTH FORM, AND THE FIRST WHERE THE NARROWING IS AN AXIS OF THE COMPARISON RATHER THAN A RANGE OF INPUTS (2026-09-03). The six above narrow WHICH INPUTS a check sees; this one narrows WHAT IT COMPARES, so it is invisible even over a complete corpus. `v1_compiler.behavioral_receipt_host` `behavioral_differential` is cited as the executing evidence for DESIGN section 7 behavioural equivalence, and its own doc comment cites section 7 by name. What it RANGES OVER is the seed-Rust versus emitted-Rust axis: `generate_receipt_driver` emits a Rust main that prints each planned call, and the differential runs that ONE driver twice -- against the tree as committed, then after the candidate overwrites its mirror and the crate is rebuilt. Both transcripts come from COMPILED RUST BINARIES, so any property the two realizations SHARE is unobservable to it by construction, however clean the corpus. THE DISCRIMINATING INSTANCE IS NOT THIS ROW'S TO STATE, AND CITING IT RATHER THAN RESTATING IT IS THE POINT (amended 2026-09-03). The interpreter-versus-emitted evaluation divergence has an owner with better evidence than prose: `realization_arms_diverge_on_whether_the_program_refuses`, filed by gunbc#10139, which carries the source reading in both directions AND an EXECUTED red -- `fn guarded_division(n: Int) -> Bool { n != 0 && (100 / n) > 1 }`, run and dated, explicitly not asserted. An earlier draft of this receipt described that divergence in its own words. That would have been a second authority for a fact this ledger already owns, committed inside the row about subjects narrower than their claims, which is the specimen writing itself. WHAT IS THIS ROW'S OWN SUBJECT is the other half and a different fact: THE INSTRUMENT CANNOT SEE IT. Both of `behavioral_differential`'s arms are compiled Rust, so they share the target's evaluation semantics and agree with each other -- wrongly, together -- on exactly the inputs where that cited class occurs. The divergence is #10139's; the blindness of the equivalence instrument to it is this row's. THE REVIEW TELL FOR THIS FORM: ask what the two sides of a comparison SHARE, not what the check ranges over -- a differential between two realizations of one target cannot see a property of that target. The ordinary tell, a green over a population nobody checked, is unavailable here: this instrument is green for a reason no input can change. ONE NEIGHBOUR CHECKED AND EXCLUDED, so the next reader does not re-derive it: `v2.compiler.emit_host` `run_test_claim_emit_vs_eval` IS enrolled on the required floor and DOES pair an executed emitted answer with an interpreted one, with a positive control and a discriminating negative. But it evaluates `expected_eval_root` while host-running the emitted `claim_input_root` -- two different roots -- so it is an ORACLE comparison and the interpreter never evaluates the expression the target runs. Its subject excludes same-expression evaluation divergence structurally, not by coverage. THE AUTHOR'S OWN INSTANCE, recorded because it happened while filing this: the first draft claimed NO required phase pairs an interpreted answer with an emitted-and-executed one, derived by enumerating the closed five-variant `RequiredCiPhase` and then asserting what its members execute without reading them. The population was closed; the claim about its members was never checked -- a real enumeration, divided by nothing. It was refuted by the very rows that refused the pull request being worked on.AN EIGHTH FORM, AND THE FIRST WHERE THE BLIND SPOT IS NOT ARBITRARY BUT CORRELATED WITH RECENCY (2026-09-03, tidy-koi-619 on gunbc#10106, with warm-seal-35 as the finder). Every form above treats the narrowing as ARBITRARY -- 'never a decision, the shape of the first implementation'. When the narrowed axis is a DIRECTION over a time-ordered subject, the narrowing stops being arbitrary and starts tracking recency, which makes it strictly worse than a random miss. THE INSTANCE: a squash-merged branch survives as a divergent-looking branch and auto-opens a pull request that would REVERT main. Diagnosing gunbc#10242 I enumerated what a merge would wrongly RE-ADD -- 47 namespace admissions consumed by their own merge, plus three files deleted on main -- and concluded, in the comment closing it, 'there is no file where this branch carries content main wants'. THAT SENTENCE IS TRUE. Its subject is one direction; its reading is completeness. The 54 differing paths were 43 common-but-differing, 3 branch-only which a merge RE-ADDS, and 8 main-only which a merge DELETES -- and the deletion category, which the sweep never ranged over, contained a witness test that had landed twenty minutes earlier. WHY RECENCY MAKES IT WORSE: the newest work on main is NECESSARILY in the merge-would-delete category, because the more recently something landed the more certain it is to be absent from a superseded branch. So this sweep's blind spot is not spread evenly over the population -- it concentrates on exactly the rows that are newest, least recoverable, and least likely to be noticed missing, since nobody is yet watching for the absence of a thing that landed twenty minutes ago. A check whose miss probability rises with the value of what is missed is a different economic object from one that misses uniformly, and the review tell is different too: it is not 'what population did this range over' but 'is the missed set correlated with anything, and with what'. RECOGNITION RULE FOR THIS FORM: when a check's subject is a COMPARISON between two states, name both directions as separate sentences and range over both -- present-on-A-absent-on-B and present-on-B-absent-on-A are two populations, and a size or presence heuristic run one way answers only one of them. WHY IT IS FILED HERE AND NOT AS ITS OWN CLASS: three instances arrived within one day from three unrelated angles -- a record comparator that could not parse a parameterized constructor and so reported None == None as agreement on the `standing` field; an assert-count-is-1 guard over a citation sweep that held because the PATTERN was narrow rather than because the file was clean; and this one-directional diff sweep. The author's first instinct was to mint a new class around the sentence 'a check that does not state its own denominator cannot be distinguished from a complete one'. That sentence is this row's INVALID STATE restated, so a new row would have been a second authority for a rostered fact -- the DESIGN section 2 failed decomposition, committed inside the ledger that exists to prevent it, which is the same self-demonstration the seventh form records. The class was already here; only the recency observation was new. A NINTH FORM, WHERE THE SUBJECT ITSELF MOVED UNDER THE CHECK (2026-09-05, adjudicated in jolly-dove-86, measured on gunbc#10552). It is numbered ninth because this row already carried an eighth; the brief that commissioned it said eighth, and the row is the authority on its own count. STATEMENT: a repair changes the SUBJECT that the earlier observation described. Completeness and correctness must be established against the FINAL SUBJECT, with the selection boundary explicit; finding the newly inserted wording is not that establishment. THE REASONING UNDER IT: checked revision A, plus edit A to B, does not equal checked revision B, without a preservation argument or a renewed inspection. The escape clause is not decoration -- RENEWED INSPECTION DISCHARGES IT, and reading the final text once IS an adjudication. No impossibility is claimed here: an earlier draft said such a sweep can never close, and that was refuted, because renewed reading is the only mechanism that actually works. RUNG: this form carries NONE. It is the sub-case the amended ceiling above excludes -- the subject is a written surface, so no input can be constructed to carry its extent, and the claim and the range remain two objects. SPECIMEN ONE, three reviews that ranged over less than the row they reviewed: 5120939460, 5121228544 and 5121282475, none of which reached `test.claim.phantom_marker_type_argument_identity_witness` -- the file the row under repair CITES -- where two corrected-away formulations stood verbatim. The enumeration found them; no reviewer did. SPECIMEN TWO, and it is what fixes the UNIT of repair: a repair landed at the QUOTED text, left the FRAMING sentence directly above it standing, and left a dependent two paragraphs BELOW it standing too -- one that never quoted the premise it rested on (`a trigger whose owner has ceased to exist is retired by nothing at all`). That dependent contained none of the repair's search terms precisely because it RESTED on the premise rather than quoting it. So the unit of repair is the ENTAILMENT NEIGHBOURHOOD of the premise -- framing above, dependents below, sibling surfaces -- and it is BIDIRECTIONAL. A phrase-keyed sweep is keyed on quotation, and entailment does not quote. REFUSED TRIGGER, recorded WITH its reason so it is not re-proposed: `enroll the sweep in CI`. Mechanisation moves the INVOCATION, not the SUBJECT -- the same incomplete phrase search would execute reliably over the same incomplete subject, so the trigger would fire while the capability stayed dead, which is exactly the DESIGN section 4b(3) grain mismatch between a loss sentence and its trigger. Rung 2 needs an adequate subject definition, a valid checking relation AND a consumer; automation supplies only the third. THE NEIGHBOUR CHECKED AND EXCLUDED, using that row's own bounding text: selection_view_read_as_population is a set produced by a MEASUREMENT threshold and read as the population. Here nothing is measured and nothing is filtered -- the range was simply never as wide as the sentence beside it, which is this row's invariant, so the form is filed here and not minted. The corroborating receipt from the row's own repair day: a phrase-keyed sweep certified that repair clean and missed four survivors, because the live one read `WHAT ACTUALLY HOLDS THIS CLASS AT 2` and matched none of the guessed patterns. The form applies to writing the form.
- **a recurrence ledger scoped below the recurrence it must find** (an instrument counts repetition WITHIN a unit of work and is reset at that unit's boundary, while the repetition that matters happens ACROSS those units. Every unit then reports the same honest answer -- ONE -- and the aggregate nobody computes is the only place the duplication exists. The instrument is not wrong at its own grain and never reads as broken: it runs, it is enabled, it prints, and its output is empty of the thing being looked for, which is indistinguishable from that thing being absent. **The recognition rule is a question about RANGE, not about correctness: name the scope over which the redundancy you are hunting is defined, and check that the ledger's lifetime is at least that scope.** If the ledger is cleared more often than the redundancy recurs, its silence is a property of its reset schedule. **THE KINSHIP THAT MAKES IT LEGIBLE, and it is `censored_estimator_drops_its_own_tail` in a different medium: a cost paid once per unit and attributed to one arbitrary consumer never reaches count>=2 inside that unit, so the ledger that ranks REPETITION cannot observe the population it exists to rank.** The estimator is censored not by a threshold but by a scope. **THE FIELD TELL, and it is the one that identifies this class from the outside before anyone reads the instrument: the repair mechanism ALREADY EXISTS and its coverage is hand-authored, because candidates are discovered when the unrepaired cost causes a failure somewhere unrelated.** A roster that grows one row at a time, each row traceable to an incident on somebody else's change, is a roster whose discovery instrument is blind -- the incidents are doing the ranking, and their cost is paid by whoever was passing. Distinct from `instrument_output_read_as_subject_content`, where a report is complete for the reporter and read past its promise -- here the report IS complete for its declared scope and the CONSUMER'S QUESTION LIVES AT A WIDER ONE -- and distinct from `selection_view_read_as_population`, where a threshold over measured values is mistaken for the class: no selection happens here at all, the population is simply unrepresentable in the ledger's key space. SPECIMEN, WITH THE FULL CAUSAL CHAIN MEASURED (2026-09-02, gunbc#10036 lane). The required floor charges each claim a per-claim CPU ceiling and builds a FRESH EVALUATION FRAME PER CLAIM -- deliberately, so one witness cannot contaminate the next -- so every claim re-derives the pure substrate its import closure reaches. `v1_compiler.v1_interpreter`'s recompute-trace ledger ranks pure calls with count>=2 within ONE `InterpContext`, and `claim_executor` prints and drops it at every claim frame exit; a producer evaluated EXACTLY ONCE per claim, in 3477 claims, has count=1 in every ledger and appears in none. The repair mechanism already existed -- `v2.workflow.floor_pure_producer_share` serves one evaluation of a declared pure producer across frames -- and its roster was six hand-authored rows, because nothing produced its candidates. **SO THE DISCOVERY MECHANISM WAS THE COST: roster coverage was found when a per-claim budget refusal landed on an unrelated lane's pull request, four lanes in one day, every one of those runs reporting failed=0.** THE DISCRIMINATOR THAT PROVED THE CHARGE WAS NOT THE CLAIM'S OWN WORK, and it is reusable: a witness and its DISCRIMINATING RED do materially different work, so when they cost the same to within 2ms while both sit near the ceiling, the cost is not the assertion's -- 296/294, 333/331 and 384/382 on main run 33615659836's `required_floor_claim_cost.tsv`, against a corpus p50 of 2ms; one 34-row module spans 0ms to the ceiling in three bands that track WHICH SHARED PRODUCER a row forces rather than what it asserts. **WHAT THE SPECIMEN REFUTES IS BOTH OF THE OBVIOUS REMEDIES, AND THAT IS WHY THE CLASS IS FILED RATHER THAN THE INSTANCE FIXED.** Raising the ceiling moves a line that the corpus distribution says is correctly placed, to accommodate a charge that is wrong, and it destroys the only signal that anything is slow; quarantining the family converts a cost problem into a coverage problem silently. Both are the absorbing arm DESIGN section 5 forbids, and the second cannot even be reached, because the family is not anomalous -- its own work is among the smallest in the corpus. REPAIR: fold each unit's ledger into a run-scoped census keyed on the DECLARATION identity plus the argument row, so cross-unit demand becomes a run product; the receipt is `v1_compiler.v1_interpreter` `absorb_claim_recompute_demand` and the artifact `gunbc.witness_floor_workflow` `required_floor_cross_claim_demand_path`, whose own discriminating red asserts the per-frame ledger's blindness beside the census's answer so re-scoping it back reds. A SECOND, INDEPENDENT CONSEQUENCE, AND IT RETIRES PART OF A DECLARED DROP RATHER THAN THE DROP: `gunbc.rung_drop` `floor_cost_claim_qualification_unavailable` names a missing carrier -- the per-claim cost artifact modeled as data a function can read -- and states that its attention subset is a HAND derivation rather than a run product. That is buildable, so what a cross-unit census plus that modeled fold retires is BLIND CANDIDATE DISCOVERY, INCIDENT-AS-DISCOVERY, AND THE MISSING CARRIER. **WHAT IT DOES NOT RETIRE, AND SAYING OTHERWISE WOULD BE THE RUNG INFLATION THIS LEDGER EXISTS TO CATCH: environment-independent claim-cost qualification.** Naming every shared producer does not make a claim's remaining evaluator work invariant across execution envelopes -- host scheduling, contention and cache state still move it -- so the per-claim line stays an ATTEMPT-SAFETY boundary and that drop keeps standing on its own subject. **THE SAME DISCIPLINE APPLIES TO WHAT THE CENSUS ITSELF EXPLAINS.** A per-unit constant is BY CONSTRUCTION identical on two runs of one tree, so it accounts for the LEVEL and not the VARIANCE; a run-to-run delta is separately measured (byte-identical evaluator steps against 1.31-1.80x cpu over a byte-identical payload). The two COMPOSE into the observed shape -- the constant puts a family AT the line and something run-to-run decides which of its rows cross -- and that composition is why the victim WANDERS rather than repeating -- the prediction this account makes and the rival 'fixed set of expensive rows' account does not. **TWO RECEIPTS, NEITHER A CROSS-RUN COMPARISON.** (i) ZERO MARGIN: two rows of one module measured 502ms and EXACTLY 500ms against the 500ms budget. (ii) ONE ROW OBSERVED CROSSING THE TWO POPULATIONS ON ONE TREE -- run 33620893203 attempt 1 records `an_empty_receipt_series_leaves_the_live_tree_unmeasured_rather_than_held` as INTERRUPTED-BEFORE-VERDICT with `cost=UNMEASURED`, and ATTEMPT 2 OF THE SAME RUN records it as COMPLETED-OVER-COST-REQUIREMENT at cpu_ms=500, reaching its verdict. **THAT PAIR IS A BOUND BESIDE A VALUE, NOT TWO MEASUREMENTS OF ONE QUANTITY, AND THE DIFFERENCE IS THE WHOLE POINT:** the interrupted arm knows only that the cost exceeded the ceiling by an unknown amount, and its reported figure names where the POLL observed the ceiling -- a property of the budget, which its own diagnostic spends three clauses saying. Writing the pair as two costs would say the row got a couple of milliseconds cheaper and crossed a line, which is a story about the row; what actually changed between the attempts is WHAT THE OBSERVER COULD SAY. Those are the two populations the budget cut separated BECAUSE THEY ARE DIFFERENT FACTS WITH DIFFERENT REMEDIES, and at this margin which one a row lands in is decided by whether the poll fired before or after the work finished: a fact about the poll, not about the row. **THE CITATION DISCIPLINE THAT MADE THAT RECOVERABLE IS PART OF THE CLASS, because the evidence was retracted and then restored on it: CITE THE ATTEMPT, NOT THE RUN OR THE JOB.** A bare run id and a bare job id both resolve to the LATEST attempt, so a rerun silently changes what a stable citation serves -- no error, and nothing visible from the citing end, which is why the first retraction looked sound. `/actions/runs/<id>/attempts/<n>/logs` serves the attempt that was actually read; a figure cited without an attempt should carry `run_attempt` beside it so a reader can tell whether the citation still points at what its author saw. **AND THE STANDING RECOMMENDATION IS UNCHANGED BY EITHER DIRECTION OF THAT CORRECTION: A REMEDY MUST NEVER BE KEYED TO WHICH ROWS TIPPED.** A producer census survived the retraction and the restoration without a word changing in what it reports; a ranking of tipped rows would have been rebuilt twice. Two independent arguments for one conclusion: dispersion shows the charge is not the ASSERTION's, wandering shows it is not the ROW's.)
- **a carefully hedged BENEFIT claim leaves the HARM axis wholly unexamined** (INVALID STATE: an actor takes an irreversible or side-effecting action, states a calibrated caution about whether it HELPS, and never asks whether it HARMS. The two are different axes, and no amount of hedging on the first reaches the second. HARM: the hedge is what makes it dangerous rather than merely wrong -- it reads as due diligence, so a reviewer or manager sees the epistemic care and endorses, and the endorsement generalises a single act into practice. Section 5 silent wrongness with an amplifier: the honest-sounding sentence is the vector. **SPECIMEN, and the actor is the reporter** (sunny-gull-270, 2026-09-02). A superseded CI run on a two-commit-stale head was cancelled to return a fleet slot during saturation, reported with the explicit caution `the cancel does not make the current run start, and I have no evidence it helps rather than merely stops waste`. The manager verified the reasoning and endorsed it as `the only lever any of us actually has`, generalising it. Both were wrong, and the authority that settles it was one grep away: gunbc.witness_floor_workflow emits cancel-in-progress false with running NeverSupersedeRunning, because the fleet's builds are bounded by a host-persistent GNU jobserver whose permits are returned COOPERATIVELY -- a cancelled run SIGKILLs cargo mid-build, so every borrowed permit is lost for the daemon's lifetime and the pool decays monotonically toward zero. So the act did not merely fail to help; it is the mechanism that produces the exhaustion symptom it was taken to relieve, and the stale runs it reclaimed are that policy's DECLARED cost rather than waste. **RECOGNITION RULE, and it is mechanical: read the hedge and ask which axis it is on.** A caution of the form `I cannot show this helps`, `this may not be enough`, `no evidence it improves things` is a BENEFIT hedge. If the action has any side effect on shared state -- a cancel, a delete, a force-push, a config write, a retry against a non-idempotent endpoint -- the benefit hedge is not the relevant one and its presence is evidence the harm question was never asked. **THE TELL A REVIEWER CAN USE: an actor who has genuinely examined harm names the mechanism by which harm would occur, and then says why it does not apply here.** An actor who has only hedged benefit names no mechanism at all, because there is none to name. **RUNG FOUND AT: 1, mitigatable, and by nothing structural** -- the harm was caught after the fact by reading an authority nobody was required to read. **CEILING: 2, mechanically preventable, and not higher.** Whether an action's side effects are harmful is not decidable from the action alone; it depends on a governing policy that may live in any authority. What IS decidable is whether the policy governing a class of action was CONSULTED. **NEXT TRIGGER: a typed standing that binds each fleet-affecting operator action to the authority governing it, so taking the action without consulting that authority refuses rather than relying on the actor to grep.** Until that exists this is a review discipline and citing it as coverage is rung inflation.
- **a receipt names a PROPERTY and never names the TREE it holds of, so it expires silently** (INVALID STATE: evidence of the form 'regenerating produces the committed bytes', 'the diff is zero', 'the census matches', 'the fixed point is reached' is a claim about A BASE, but is recorded and cited as a claim about A CHANGE. When the base moves the receipt keeps reading as valid while describing a tree that no longer exists. HARM: section 5 silent wrongness, and this is the WORST-SIGNALLING member of its family rather than merely another one -- a stale citation still shows the reader its text, an incompatible merge still conflicts, an absent observation still reports an absence; AN EXPIRED GREEN PRODUCES NOTHING AT ALL. Nothing goes red, no conflict is raised, and the author's own careful re-verification re-affirms it, because re-running the same command against the same stale working tree reproduces the same true-of-nothing answer. **DISTINCT FROM ITS TWO NEAREST NEIGHBOURS, and the repair law is what separates them.** `execution_provenance_loss` asks whether the observation RAN; here it ran and was valid, and then STOPPED being valid because its subject moved underneath it. `positional_citation` is a decaying HANDLE -- a pointer that no longer names what it named; here the handle is fine and it is the EVIDENCE BINDING that decayed. Different invalid states, different repairs. **SPECIMEN (sunny-gull-270, PR #10036, 2026-09-02).** The PR's entire acceptance test was that the emitted .github/workflows/witnesses.yml is byte-identical after regeneration -- a behaviour-identical refactor whose whole claim is that the bytes do not move. It was re-verified after each of three commits, and ALL THREE measurements were taken against a base main had already left: #10024 had landed and materially rewritten BOTH the authority the PR edits and the artifact under test -- re-derive the magnitudes with `git show --stat 7c310ed74c5 -- dag/gunbc/witness/witness_floor_workflow.dag .github/workflows/witnesses.yml`, which is an immutable commit and so answers the same forever. No signal at any point. The expiry was found by asking what had changed in main, not by any check. **RECOGNITION RULE, mechanical: before citing a zero-diff or byte-identity receipt as merge evidence, ask whether the base moved ON THE FILES THE RECEIPT IS ABOUT** -- `git diff --stat $(git merge-base HEAD origin/main) origin/main -- <those paths>` -- because a receipt about generated bytes is invalidated only by changes to the authorities that produce them, and a whole-repository is-my-branch-behind check is both too coarse to act on and too noisy to keep. **A SECOND, LESS OBVIOUS OBLIGATION: RE-DERIVE THE DISCRIMINATING CONTROL TOO, NOT ONLY THE RECEIPT.** A control taken before the merge proves the zero was readable on a tree that is not the one shipping, so a re-derived green beside a stale control is half a measurement. And rebuild the PRODUCER: the tree and the emitter are two inputs, and a regeneration with one of them stale proves nothing about the pair. **THE UPSIDE IS WORTH STATING BECAUSE IT CHANGES WHETHER PEOPLE DO IT: a receipt re-derived after a FOREIGN change to the same artifact is STRICTLY STRONGER than the original.** The first green only ever asks whether a projection reproduces its own base; the second asks whether it reproduces a base someone else moved -- which is where a projection that disagrees with the hand-kept form it replaced would actually surface. **RUNG FOUND AT: 1, mitigatable.** Today the only defence is an author remembering to re-derive before citing, which is exactly the defence that does not survive a tired hour. **CEILING: 2, mechanically preventable, and the reason it is not higher is that a receipt cannot make its subject stop moving -- what it can do is make the movement OBSERVABLE. NEXT TRIGGER, named as the capability and not as an artifact: a receipt CARRIES THE BASE IT WAS MEASURED ON, so a producer can compare that base against HEAD and refuse an expired receipt, rather than a person remembering to. THAT CAPABILITY IS NOT HYPOTHETICAL -- IT ALREADY EXISTS ONE SURFACE OVER, WHICH IS WHY THIS TRIGGER IS REACHABLE RATHER THAN ASPIRATIONAL: the review runner binds each review to the sha it was taken on and REFUSES on mismatch. On this row's own pull request, review 58587 failed with `worktree freshness check failed: HEAD is ad388184ac but PR #10036 head is c80c9d88db -- refusing to review a stale/wrong checkout`, and a refused review is exactly the signal an expired receipt does not produce. So reviews already carry their base and receipts do not; the gap is an UNAPPLIED mechanism rather than a missing one, and the trigger is discharged by giving regeneration receipts the binding reviews already have. **THIS CITATION CARRIES ITS OWN BASE, because a row about receipts that expire must not carry one that has.** What is DURABLE is review 58587 itself: it is retrievable from the reviews API with status=failed, sha c80c9d88db and that error text, and a completed review is a historical fact that does not decay. What is an OBSERVATION AT A TIME is the claim that the runner still behaves this way -- measured 2026-09-02, and NOT re-confirmed since, because the runner's source lives outside this repository and is not visible from a session container, so no gate here can check it and its absence from a local filesystem is not evidence either way. Sampling later pull requests for a freshness failure -- `dashboard-ops reviews <pr>`, reading each review's status and error -- found none, and that is EXPECTED RATHER THAN REASSURING: the check fires only when a head moves mid-checkout, so a sample drawn where the condition did not arise discriminates nothing and no count of it is worth carrying. **THAT UNCHECKABILITY IS ITSELF WHY THE TRIGGER NAMES A CAPABILITY AND NOT AN ARTIFACT** -- a trigger pointing at a file this repository cannot see would be satisfied or falsified by nothing observable here, which is the restoration-promise failure one row over. Until that exists this row is the mitigation and citing it as coverage is rung inflation.**
- **a destructive action is judged by a PROXY observation, never by the action's own semantics** (INVALID STATE: an actor destroys something -- deletes an edge, a file, a roster row, an authority -- and reads the verdict off an observation that stands in a PROXY relation to what the destruction MEANS. 'Strip the imports and see whether the compile exits 0' is the canonical shape. The proxy is only ever as strong as the checking path that produces it, and the semantic question -- does every occurrence that crossed the destroyed edge still bind to exactly one accepted declaration identity, and does that identity project a provider the closure reaches -- is never asked. **HARM: DESIGN section 5 silent wrongness, and directionally the worst member of its family, because the proxy and the semantics agree EXACTLY WHERE THE CHECKER IS SOUND AND DIVERGE EXACTLY WHERE IT FAILS OPEN.** The green is therefore densest on the population the destruction actually damages, and a count of green subjects reads as coverage while being a census of the checker's absorbing arm. **RECOGNITION RULE, and it is mechanical rather than a matter of judgement: name the acceptance path that produced the observation, and ask what that path does when the property under test is ABSENT -- refuse, or substitute.** If any arm on it fabricates, widens, defaults or falls back, the observation is a proxy and its green is a statement about that arm. **DISTINCT FROM ITS THREE NEIGHBOURS, and the repairs differ.** `absorbing_fallback` is the fail-open arm itself; here the arm may be long known, correctly filed and owned by somebody else -- the defect is the READING of a destruction's green taken across it. `instrument_output_read_as_subject_content` is a report complete for its own promise and read past it; here the observation is not incomplete at all, it answers a DIFFERENT QUESTION than the actor's. `executed_conjunct_discriminates_nothing` cannot discriminate anywhere; a proxy discriminates perfectly on the sound half of its population, which is what makes it credible. **SPECIMEN, ALREADY MEASURED AND ON THE RECORD IN THIS REPOSITORY, which is why the class is filed rather than the instance re-investigated: the import strip, `docs/plans/import-strip-witness-discovery-cascade-diagnosis.md` section 15.4.** 1,773 of the 2,574 stripped files produced no diagnostic, and that 69% was available to be read as 'these files' references are independently resolvable'. Section 14 of the same document had ALREADY established by execution that a type present in the pool but not structurally bound is FABRICATED AS AN ANONYMOUS PRODUCT rather than refused, and that the production type-reference discriminator is measurement-only -- normal compile, resolve, run and emit stay on the legacy fail-open path unless measurement mode is armed. So the 69% is a joint statement about which providers happened to be in the pool and which failures the fabrication arm absorbed, and the document says so in its own words: a clean file may be clean because its provider happened to be loaded. **THE SUBSTITUTE, STATED SO THIS ROW NAMES A TEST AND NOT ONLY A DEFECT, and it is section 15.4's own terminal test: not 'delete imports, compile exits 0', but every cross-module occurrence has exactly one accepted declaration identity; every accepted identity projects its provider file; the loader closure equals that provider projection; adding or removing an unrelated loaded module changes neither binding nor closure; type, value, callee and emitter consumers all consume the accepted edge; no fallback or fabrication arm is reachable; and compile AND execution are green.** **THE STANDING OBLIGATION THIS CLASS CREATES, AND IT LANDS ON THIS PROJECT'S OWN CENSUS METHOD: THE DESTRUCTION-WITNESS PRECONDITION.** DESIGN section 3 grounds delete-first on 'the deletion is the census -- every real dependent refuses loudly'. That sentence is CONDITIONAL on the substrate being fail-closed ON THE AXIS DESTROYED, and it is the conditional, not the sentence, that a destruction witness must discharge: before a deletion's green may be read as 'nothing depended on it', exhibit BY EXECUTION, on the SAME acceptance path the green will be read from, one case where the destroyed edge is genuinely required and the path goes RED. Absent that red the green is a property of the path's failure arm rather than of the corpus, and no number of green subjects repairs it -- a vacuous check does not become discriminating by being run more times. The precondition is carried by `docs/plans/replacement-migration-doctrine.md`, filed beside the sibling already recorded there in which the census is empty because the referent is NAMED rather than imported: same conclusion by a different mechanism, and the pair is the point -- there nothing refuses because nothing was ever an edge, here the edge exists and the checker declines to judge it. **RUNG FOUND AT: 1, mitigatable.** The divergence was caught by a reader holding the observation up against the checker that produced it, after a wave had already been sized on the proxy. **CEILING: 3, structurally guaranteed, and reachable rather than aspirational, because the disqualifying fact is decidable from the substrate:** whether the acceptance path a witness reads has a reachable fabrication or fallback arm on the axis under test is a property of that path's own graph, not of the world. **NEXT TRIGGER, NAMED AS THE CAPABILITY AND NOT AS AN ARTIFACT: a destruction witness declares the axis it destroys and the acceptance path it reads, and the executor REFUSES the witness when that path carries a reachable fail-open arm on that axis** -- so the precondition is discharged by the runner rather than remembered by an author. Until that capability exists this row and the doctrine section are the mitigation, and citing either as coverage is the rung inflation DESIGN section 4b(1) names.
- **stable citation, mutable referent** (an identifier that names a thing correctly and forever, while the CONTENT served for it is replaced underneath. Distinct from `positional_citation`, and the difference is what makes it worse: a `file:line` pointer goes stale under an edit above it, and re-reading the file EXPOSES that — the symbol is not there, or a different one is. Here re-reading the citation CONFIRMS the wrong answer, because the identifier still resolves, still returns content of the expected shape, and reports no error. There is no red anywhere. Receipt (2026-09-02, gunbc#10042): a GitHub Actions JOB ID was cited as the source of a floor measurement. A job id names the job; the logs API serves the LATEST ATTEMPT. A rerun replaces the attempt, so job 100217416636 served six budget-refused identities when first read and thirteen when read back, and the second reading was byte-identical to a DIFFERENT job id — two ids, one attempt. A cross-run comparison built on those readings appeared to compare two runs while one side had been replaced underneath, and the damage ran in BOTH directions: a near-disjointness claim that was simply false, and a real bucket migration that was retracted as unfounded because it could no longer be reproduced through the citation that found it. The second is the more expensive error — a true finding withdrawn, downstream prose rewritten in four places to remove it, and a merge rule changed — and it is the one a reader would not think to look for, because a retraction feels like the conservative move. THE DEFAULT CITATION IS THE TRAP AND THE PINNED ONE IS THE REMEDY, and both were measured rather than assumed: `gh run view --job <id> --log` serves the latest attempt, but the attempt-pinned REST path — `/repos/OWNER/REPO/actions/runs/RUNID/attempts/N/logs` still serves attempt 1 in full. Recovering it confirmed the original six-row reading exactly, and confirmed that `an_empty_receipt_series_leaves_the_live_tree_unmeasured_rather_than_held` is INTERRUPTED in attempt 1 and COMPLETED-OVER-COST in attempt 2 — a real migration across the two populations, not the artifact it was briefly retracted as. So the class is not that the earlier subject is destroyed; it is that the ORDINARY way of citing it silently resolves to a different one, and a reader who re-reads the same citation to check gets agreement instead of a contradiction. **Recognition rule: before comparing two readings of one producer, ask whether the producer serves a STABLE SUBJECT for a fixed citation — not whether the citation is well-formed. An identifier that addresses a CONTAINER (a job, a branch, a latest, a mutable tag, a rolling window) serves whatever currently occupies it, so two readings of one citation are two subjects and their DELTA is uninterpretable. Cite the thing that cannot be replaced — the attempt, the commit, the immutable artifact — or capture the content at read time and say when.** The receipt worth keeping is that this survived the diligence that should have caught it: the same author had, that morning, REFUSED to file a transcribed measurement on the §6 grounds that no producer owned the number, and then built a delta out of a producer that mutates what it serves for a fixed id. Naming the instrument is not sufficient if the instrument does not serve a stable subject. One diagnostic tell for the delta itself, preserved here from gunbc#10130 where it was measured against this same receipt: the two readings were differenced into a `3 -> 6 -> 13` upward trend with a memory-pressure mechanism attached, the `3` having additionally come from a different head — and monotone growth is precisely what independent draws at a fixed distance from a budget do NOT produce. The shape that made the artifact persuasive was the evidence against it, so the tell is available BEFORE the referent is re-checked: a delta through a container-addressing citation that arrives already fitting a story is the reading to distrust first.)
- realization arms diverge on WHETHER THE PROGRAM REFUSES (two realizations of one accepted program agree on the returned value and disagree on whether an evaluation-time refusal fires, because the fact that decides it -- evaluation order -- is modeled nowhere and each arm improvises). INVALID STATE: a connective whose operand evaluation strategy is unmodeled, realized strictly by one arm and lazily by another. gunbc v1 today: v1.compiler.interpreter eval_expr_inner's ExprBinOp arm evaluates BOTH operands through `?` before dispatching. eval_binop DOES carry And and Or arms -- `Value::Bool(left.is_truthy() && right.is_truthy())` -- and they are irrelevant to this class and worth naming precisely because they look like the repair: they run on operands ALREADY EVALUATED at the call site, so their Rust && is over two bools and short-circuits nothing. A reader grepping eval_binop for And finds a hit and concludes the case is handled, while std.operator_realization maps BinOp::And and BinOp::Or to OperatorRealization::HostOperator, rendered as Rust && and ||, which short-circuit. HARM: the guard idiom -- a cheap precondition guarding an expression that can refuse -- means two different programs. The interpreter runs the guarded expression when the guard is FALSE and refuses; emitted Rust never runs it and returns a value. Silent in both directions and invisible to any oracle comparing returned values on guard-true inputs, which is every value-comparison oracle we have. DISCRIMINATING RED, executed 2026-09-02, not asserted: `fn guarded_division(n: Int) -> Bool { n != 0 && (100 / n) > 1 }`. At n=0 the interpreter refuses DivisionByZero and the emitted Rust returns false; at n=5 both return true. Division by zero was chosen deliberately because BOTH realizations abort identically IF the expression is evaluated, so evaluation order is the only variable in the pair. CONTROLS, because a false from a harness that cannot observe an abort is a vacuous green: the same function with the guard forced true and n=0 PANICKED with 'attempt to divide by zero' and was caught and reported, so the harness can see the abort; and the guard-true positive control agrees across both arms. HONESTY BOUND ON THE EVIDENCE: the interpreter arm ran on the real acceptance path via `gunbc run`; the emitted arm executed the emitted function bodies extracted VERBATIM into a rustc harness, not the whole emitted crate. WHY IT IS NOT refusal_deferred_to_emitted_runtime: that class is a compiler writing a refusal construct INTO the artifact while reporting success. Here no refusal is written anywhere and the compile is honest; the two arms simply disagree at run time about whether one fires. Same neighbourhood, different invalid state, different repair. THE POPULATION IS SURVIVORSHIP-FILTERED AND A GREP UNDERSTATES IT BY CONSTRUCTION. The interpreter is the STRICTER arm, so any author who wrote the guard idiom over a refusing right operand hit the refusal while authoring and rewrote it -- most likely into a nested if or match. So the sites matching `cheap_guard && expensive` are the SURVIVORS, the cases where the right operand happens not to refuse; the cases carrying the correctness consequence were already edited away. A LOW COUNT IS THEREFORE NOT EVIDENCE THE CLASS IS MINOR AND MUST NOT BE REPORTED AS ONE. The honest population is sites where an author WANTED the guard idiom, which lives in the rewrites and is not reachable by the same needle. RECOGNITION RULE, stated to generalise past this operator: wherever a construct is realized independently by the interpreter and by an emission target, ask what fact decides its behaviour and where that fact is DECLARED. If the deciding fact is absent from the authority both arms are supposed to consult, the arms are not implementing one semantics, they are each inventing one, and agreement on the values you happened to test is not evidence they agree. The tell is a realization shape -- HostOperator -- that names WHO evaluates without naming WHAT is evaluated. RUNG: mitigatable at best, and only because one arm refuses loudly; against a source-to-emission path this is silent wrongness, which DESIGN section 4b places outside the ladder. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, named as a capability and not an artifact: EVALUATION ORDER MODELED AS A PROPERTY OF A CONNECTIVE IN THE .dag AUTHORITY AND CONSULTED BY BOTH REALIZATIONS -- sufficient for the interpreter's binop arm to derive its strictness from the same row the emitter derives its rendering from, so that a connective's operand strategy cannot be stated twice or left unstated. A grep across dag/, src/v2/ and src/v1/*.dag on 2026-09-02 found no such fact: every short-circuit hit is prose in a comment or a witness name. NOT REPAIRED HERE: changing the interpreter to short-circuit is a corpus-wide evaluation-semantics change and needs its own subject, population and review; filing it against the missing carrier is the point of this row.
- **a declared return type disagrees with the type its body actually produces, because the body's type comes from a GENERIC CALL'S INSTANTIATION** (INVALID STATE: a function declares `-> F<A>` and returns the result of `g<T>(...)` instantiated at `T = B`, so the produced type is `F<B>`. The declaration is authored independently of the body and nothing joins them, so `A` and `B` never meet. Every consumer then reads the DECLARATION, passes the value into a parameter typed `A`, and the mismatch surfaces -- if it surfaces at all -- as a runtime type error inside a callee that names neither the declaration nor the drift. HARM: this is the loud-but-hidden corner of section 5 rather than silent wrongness. The abort is honest when it happens; what is silent is the CLASS, because the drifted arm is commonly the one that is rarely reached, so the function reads as working while one of its inhabitants is unwritable-through. **DISTINCT FROM ITS NEIGHBOURS.** `state_space_conflation` is a domain modelled with too few constructors; here the domain is right and the CARRIER's parameter is wrong. `hollow_alias` is a second name for one concept; here there is one name and two types. **SPECIMEN (keen-ferret-172, gunbc#10109, 2026-09-02), and the two halves of it carry different warrants.** VERIFIED BY SOURCE, independently by two readers: `v2.std.runtime` `RuntimePrimitiveValue.bytes` is `List<Byte>`; `v2.std.collection` `list_at_optional<T>(xs: List<T>, index: Int) -> Optional<T>` therefore yields `Optional<Byte>`; `v2.std.native_agreement` `runtime_value_discriminant_octet` declares `-> Optional<Int>` and returns exactly that call; its `Present` arm feeds the value to `octet_display(octet: Int)`.
