Any one of those was available and cheap here, and any one would have returned the truth on first contact. **Distinct from `authority_substitution`, where prose invents an A-to-B arrow that no carrier states:** here every sentence either reader wrote about `per_page` was TRUE, and the defect is that a true fact about claim one was accepted as an answer about claim three. Distinct from `bound_shaped_closure`, which is about a CONSUMER concluding a requirement is discharged because a real number satisfied it; this is about two readers' agreement being read as evidence when it is one reading counted twice. Distinct from `reachability_read_as_occupancy`: that class is one substitution made once, this class is the reason such a substitution SURVIVES review. **DISTINCT FROM `population_selector_that_cannot_admit_a_counterexample`, and the boundary is stated rather than assumed because the two rows were filed the same night off the same specimen.** That row is about how a POPULATION is enumerated: a lexical proxy for the predicate makes the verdict unfalsifiable by construction, and this corroboration failure appears there as a receipt explaining why nobody caught it. THIS row is about when a SECOND READING is evidence at all, and it fires with a perfectly well-formed population -- two readers can share one lineage while reading a log, a dashboard or a diff, where no enumeration is involved and no proxy was adopted. **THE SUBJECT SPLIT IS THE SHARPEST TELL THAT THEY ARE DIFFERENT OBJECTS RATHER THAN TWO NAMES FOR ONE, and an earlier draft of this paragraph drew the line in the wrong place.** It claimed the discriminator was DECIDABILITY -- that the sibling row was statically decidable with ceiling 3 and a constructor for a trigger, against this row being undecidable at ceiling 1. That reading is refuted on the sibling row itself, which withdrew both the rung and the ceiling: whether a selector can admit a counterexample is a SEMANTIC relation between two expressions, not a shared-symbol question a lens can read, so that row is a ratchet at ceiling 1 for the same reason this one is. Two rows that are both undecidable cannot be told apart by decidability. **THE DISCRIMINATOR IS THE SUBJECT.** That row is about how a POPULATION WAS CONSTRUCTED -- a lexical proxy for the predicate makes the verdict unfalsifiable by construction, and this corroboration failure appears there as a receipt explaining why nobody caught it. THIS row is a property of the RELATIONSHIP BETWEEN TWO READERS, and it fires with a perfectly well-formed population: two readers can share one lineage while reading a log, a dashboard or a diff, where no enumeration exists and no proxy was adopted. A repair that closes either one leaves the other exactly where it stood. **RUNG FOUND AT: 1 (mitigatable)** -- the error was caught by a third reader who changed the axis, which is a person and not a mechanism. **CEILING: 1, and the reason is decidability rather than unbuilt work.** Whether two readings share an unstated premise is a property of how two agents derived a conclusion, not of any artifact in the tree; DESIGN section 5 names this the ratchet-forever case, and calling it mechanically preventable would be the `never` trap. What IS decidable, and was therefore repaired rather than rostered, is the specimen's own class: a truncated listing is unconstructible FOR ANY CONSUMER THAT READS THROUGH THE FOLD, because `std.page_fold` `PageFoldOutcome` carries the items only on the arm reached when the serving authority itself declared the listing closed, every other arm is a typed, located refusal carrying no partial result, and `PageFoldState` is `sole_constructor` so a closed-looking state cannot be forged to reach that arm without an authority that said the listing ended. **THE SCOPE OF THAT SENTENCE IS THE FOLD, NOT THE TREE, AND THE DIFFERENCE IS THE WHOLE REASON THIS ROW EXISTS.** No production listing operation consumes `std.page_fold` yet: the GitHub listing surfaces still expose only a page-size parameter, nothing imports `extdeps.github.pagination`, and `page_fold_live` is reached today only by its own witnesses. So every real listing read in this repository is STILL capped at its first page, and the repair to date is that the capability now exists and is executable rather than that any caller has been moved onto it. Writing this row as though the class were closed would be the same substitution the row is about -- a shape that exists read as a capability that runs -- committed by the very entry that names it. **THE REMAINING INTEGRATION OBLIGATION, stated so it can be checked rather than remembered:** one real paginated operation -- `extdeps.github.workflow_runs` `ListForRef` is the intended first -- must demand pages through `page_fold_live` and close on the authority's own Link-header end, with its own live-upstream witness. Until a production caller reads through the fold, the tree's truncation is mitigated by nothing at all; it is merely now REPAIRABLE without further modeling. That work is deliberately scoped out of the PR that landed this row, and this sentence is what makes its absence countable instead of forgotten. The `Pagination` type with no consumers was deleted in the same motion, since a declared shape with no execution behind it is the surface this class reads as capability. **NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER: none, and stating that honestly is the point** -- this row is a review-time recognition rule, and enumerating agreements more carefully does not retire it. What retires an INSTANCE is the axis change: name, before looking, what a second reading would have to execute for its agreement to mean anything.)
- **a correct detector is wired to a side effect that is only valid on a subset of what it detects** (INVALID STATE: a predicate that TRULY and COMPLETELY describes a condition is promoted to select the population an ACTUATOR runs over, without the additional clause that separates the members needing the action from the members for which the same condition is the CORRECT and HEALTHY state. The detection is not wrong at any point. The promotion is. **WHAT MAKES IT DANGEROUS IS AN ASYMMETRY BETWEEN READING AND ACTING, and it is the whole reason the classes must be kept apart: OBSERVING a member the predicate correctly returns costs nothing, and ACTING on it can be destructive.** So a predicate can be perfectly validated as a description and still be unsafe as a trigger, and no amount of confirming the DESCRIPTION discovers it. **THE RECOGNITION RULE, and it is checkable in one question before any code changes: WAS THE PREDICATE VALIDATED AGAINST THE SPECIMEN THAT PROMPTED IT, OR AGAINST THE POPULATION IT WILL ACT ON?** These are different sets and the second is the only one that matters. The prompting specimen is by construction a member needing the action -- that is why it prompted -- so it can never expose the healthy majority. Nobody asks a detector what ELSE it returns, because it was built from the one thing it was supposed to return. **SPECIMEN WITH A RECEIPT, 2026-09-04, gunbc CI sweep, and both halves are mine.** A fleet sweep for parked workflow runs filtered on `status == completed AND conclusion == action_required`. A peer found a THIRD parked shape that filter could not reach -- `conclusion == cancelled` with an empty job list on a live head -- and correctly diagnosed the cause: the population was selected by a CONTAINER FIELD, so it admitted only the shape already seen. That is `population_selector_that_cannot_admit_a_counterexample`, committed inside the sweep whose author had relayed that row hours earlier. The proposed repair was to drop the conclusion predicate and filter on TERMINAL AND ZERO JOBS, which is a true and complete description of the parked state. **AND IT WOULD HAVE BEEN CATASTROPHIC AS A TRIGGER.** Run at member grain over the last 100 runs it returns TWENTY-ONE hits across ELEVEN branches, all `conclusion=cancelled` -- and roughly twenty are SUPERSEDED HEADS, cancelled before scheduling because the author pushed again. Zero jobs is exactly CORRECT for those: the run was killed as obsolete. The branch clustering shows the healthy population is the BULK and not an edge -- four runs on one branch, three on another, two on a third. Rerunning them would resurrect dead heads, register checks against commits nobody is trying to land, and spend runner capacity in a queue already measured at eighty-plus minutes deep. **n=1 CONFIRMING, n=20 REFUTING, AND THE TWENTY WERE NEVER QUERIED.** **THE MISSING CLAUSE IS LIVENESS, AND IT IS A DISCRIMINATOR RATHER THAN A HEURISTIC: `terminal AND zero jobs AND head_sha == the CURRENT head of an OPEN pull request`.** A superseded run fails the third clause BY CONSTRUCTION -- being superseded is precisely what made its head stale -- so the clause separates the two populations structurally rather than by threshold or confidence. Detect at member grain; GATE THE ACTUATOR ON LIVENESS; then branch on the conclusion only to choose the instrument, since `approve` releases an existing run and `rerun` starts a new attempt. **THE CONTROL THAT MADE THE CORRECTED SWEEP'S ZERO READABLE, recorded because the sweep returned zero and a zero is the value a broken join also returns.** After adding the liveness clause the sweep found no parked runs on live heads. That zero was checked by running the head-matcher against a KNOWN-LIVE head -- the author's own open PR -- and confirming it returned one. Without that control the result is indistinguishable from a join that matches nothing, which is `absent_reads_identically_to_never_looked`. **A NEIGHBOURING DEFECT, NOT THIS ONE, FOUND IN THE SAME HOUR AND RECORDED HERE ONLY TO SEPARATE THEM.** The sweep's author had been reporting `the fleet is clear` all night. That was true of the population the filter owned -- no runs AWAITING APPROVAL -- and was stated as the wider claim, no PARKED runs. A summary is a predicate too, and it inherits the narrowness of the query beneath it while its WORDS name the wider set. THIS ROW IS NOT ITS AUTHORITY: there is no actuator and no side effect in a status summary, so the distinguishing fact below -- detection and actuation having different admissible populations -- does not obtain, and filing it here would widen this class past its own boundary and fork an authority that already exists. It belongs to `green_reported_over_a_population_the_instrument_does_not_own`, whose subject is exactly an instrument reporting over a population it does not own. Recorded as a neighbour because the two were discovered together and a later reader tracing this specimen should be sent there rather than back here. **THE BOUNDARIES, since three roster rows sit adjacent and none covers this.** `population_selector_that_cannot_admit_a_counterexample`: there the QUERY IS WRONG and cannot return the negative; here the query is RIGHT and returns the whole population faithfully. `repair_enumerates_its_own_blast_radius_by_inspection`: there a COUNT of affected sites is stated with no decidable search behind it; here the search is decidable, executed, and correct, and the count is not in dispute. `guard_precondition_discharged_by_the_route_that_uses_it`: there a check stops discriminating because its precondition moved; here nothing has moved and the check discriminates exactly as designed. `green_reported_over_a_population_the_instrument_does_not_own`: there a REPORT names a wider population than the query beneath it owns, with no effect anywhere in it; here the report is not at issue and the defect is an EFFECT admitted on the wrong population. The distinguishing fact for this row is that DETECTION AND ACTUATION HAVE DIFFERENT ADMISSIBLE POPULATIONS and one predicate was used for both. **RUNG: 1 (mitigatable)** -- caught by asking the recognition question before wiring a predicate to an effect. **CEILING: 4 (structurally impossible), AND THE ROUTE TO IT IS NOT A STATIC SHAPE TEST.** An earlier draft of this row justified its ceiling by saying the property is decidable from the modeled call graph -- whether a predicate reaching an effectful operation carries a clause the pure-observation path does not. That justification is withdrawn: it is SYNTACTIC, and an arbitrary or irrelevant conjunct satisfies it while preserving the invalid state exactly. A check satisfiable by editing the declaration while the realization still lies is the validation-standing-where-construction-was-available tell of DESIGN section 5, so the shape test establishes no rung above 2. What it can honestly do is ENUMERATE CANDIDATES -- effect paths carrying no additional clause at all -- which is a useful lens and not a wall. **NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, the capability and not an artifact, stated so that satisfying it entails the guarantee.** The actuator must DECLARE ITS ADMISSIBLE POPULATION as a predicate over the member, and the effect constructor must require a witness produced by EVALUATING THAT DECLARED PREDICATE on the member being acted on -- so that a detection result has no constructor into the effect at all, and the promotion fails to construct rather than being caught by a reviewer who thought to ask. EXCLUSIVITY IS THE WHOLE OF IT AND EXCLUDING THE DETECTION RESULT ALONE IS NOT ENOUGH: a successful evaluation must be the ONLY constructor of the permit, so that a caller-authored receipt, a bare member, or a true boolean cannot inhabit it either. A permit type carries no safety property of its own -- the property lives entirely in an exclusive constructor that proves the actuator's declared policy over the EXACT subject acted upon, and any additional constructor silently returns the class to rung 1 while the type name still reads as coverage. Both halves are load-bearing and a trigger naming only the second is the grain mismatch section 4b(3) names as its own review tell: a receipt type whose constructor does not require that evaluation is vacuously inhabitable, claims admissibility that no constructor entails, and leaves the class exactly where it was while reading as coverage. The receipt must be grounded in the actuator's own policy or it is decoration, and this trigger is retired by nothing less.
- **a transport mock reports success independently of its request, so a hermetic success is mistaken for evidence that a real effect could succeed** (INVALID STATE: a single success-valued mock_response answers every endpoint and command, including the explicitly unreachable control. SPECIMEN: extdeps.ssh.session ssh.Session.ExecPortableWords returns exit_code 0, success true and mock-ssh-portable-words for every client_args. The HostEffect.ShellCommand dissolution exposed this when its wet failure controls moved to typed ReadEffectivePosixPrincipal over SSH. The two error directions must stay distinct: success assertions can pass vacuously; failure assertions turn false because the mock reports success. The existing test.claim.host_effect_apply_witness witness_ssh_shell_unreachable_fails_closed is already enrolled as an expected semantic RED in v2.workflow.floor_expected_red, not evidence that an unreachable host was consulted. By contrast, extdeps.access.posix_effective_principal_read_op access.PosixEffectivePrincipal.Read returns an explicit failed hermetic observation. It cannot fabricate a principal, but its failed mock also cannot establish the wet success claim. Moving a test from an unmodeled shell operation to either typed operation therefore changes its hermetic outcome, and retaining the old shell route-gap enrollment would be stale evidence. RUNG: no construction or admission rule prevents the unconditional-success mock; the general class remains unremedied and silent false success is outside the ladder. Named failure assertions expose individual instances, which is not a guarantee over transport mocks. This filing adds no prevention. NEXT-RUNG CAPABILITY: transport-mock admission requires request-sensitive success and failure controls, and refuses a mock whose success claim has no discriminating RED; where external reachability cannot be established hermetically, an explicit unobserved refusal and an executing wet control must remain distinct. The existing gunbc.hermetic_mock_fidelity records the same defect previously repaired for shell.Exec; this is the surviving SSH instance, not a claim that this PR repairs that class.)
- **a deconfliction plan does not enumerate its writers** (two or more lanes reason about how to avoid colliding on one subject, agree on a scheme, and collide anyway -- because the scheme is a PREDICTION about who will write the subject next, and the predicted population omits a writer. The omitted writer is not an adversary: it is a peer who agreed to stand down and then WROTE ANYWAY -- an earlier revision of this sentence said "and then did not act", which is the opposite mechanism and could not produce the collision the row models, because a writer that does not write collides with nobody. The result merges CLEAN, so the collision is silent. INVALID STATE: a lane holding a deconfliction property that quantifies over a writer population it never discovered. WHY IT SURVIVES REVIEW: the plan is examined for whether it WOULD prevent a collision among the writers it names, and it always would. Nothing in a diff exhibits the writer it omits, and the omitted writer's own change is independently correct. SPECIMEN (main's parse break, 2026-09-04). Four lanes independently authored a repair for one orphaned annotation. Deconfliction ran and SUCCEEDED AS A DISCUSSION: #10414 was named the winner on an earliest-with-proof rule, and the other authors assented. Three of the four then merged anyway (#10425, #10408, #10428), each deleting the same nineteen lines and each adding its own relocated copy, leaving one paragraph standing three times in the module. One copy carried an inverted double negative asserting the opposite of the sentence it was restating. Repaired by removing two copies in #10435. THE ASSENT IS LOAD-BEARING, NOT INCIDENTAL, AND IT IS WHAT BOUNDS THIS CLASS: the population was not unknown and the peers were not uncooperative. Every author was identified, every author agreed, and the scheme still failed -- so the missing thing is not discovery of the writers but an INTERLOCK on them. A class where the colliding writer was never identified is a different and easier problem, because enumerating it is the whole remedy. WHY GIT CANNOT SEE IT, which is the load-bearing mechanism: identical DELETIONS on every side reconcile silently, and each side's ADDITION lands at a different offset, so no hunk overlaps. Three independently correct repairs of one defect merge without a single conflict. A collision that produces a conflict is the benign case; this class is defined by the absence of one. THE SECOND-ORDER TRAP: the ranking conversation consumes the attention that the standing-down would need. Agreement about WHICH repair wins reads as resolution, and it is not -- closing a pull request is the act, and here one of four was closed. Naming a winner is not the losers standing down. NOT TO BE MERGED WITH a_branch_property_falsified_by_a_derived_push, which shares only the SYMPTOM that no conflict is produced so nothing notifies. That symptom has at least three mechanisms -- this one, the derived-push one, and the generated-artifact merge driver refusing with no markers -- so it does not discriminate, and uniting on it is the bare alias section 3 forbids rather than the proven coincidence section 6 requires. The concrete cost of merging them is a grain mismatch section 4b(3) names as its own review tell: this row is genuinely blocked on cross-head knowledge, the other is decidable from a single ref TODAY, and one row carries one stated ceiling because RecurringFailureMode has no ceiling or trigger FIELD -- so the affordable class would be parked behind this one's capability and never rank for a climb it does not need. RUNG: found BELOW the ladder. The duplicated meaning is a silently wrong answer -- every copy parses, every gate is green, and the projection of a triple-landed repair is indistinguishable from a correct one by any executing check. CEILING 2, mechanically preventable, and the reason it is not higher is that the writer population is not decidable from the repository: it includes future human decisions about whether to close a head. What IS decidable is the SUBJECT overlap between open heads. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER: the capability of resolving an open pull request head to the set of DECLARATIONS it modifies, which is the same Node-tree read the namespace authority already performs and which no instrument currently exposes. A path-grain fence does not discharge it, because this specimen was a single path and the collision was within one declaration's neighbourhood. REVIEW TELL: a lane stating why it CANNOT collide with another lane. The claim is a quantifier over writers, so ask which writers it ranges over and whether an unclosed peer head is inside it. And after a CONTESTED fix lands, re-measure the SUBJECT for duplication rather than re-measuring whether it is green -- green is exactly what triple-duplication looks like.
- **a branch property falsified by a derived push** (an author asserts a property about their OWN branch -- most often "this change touches only the authority and never its generated projection" -- and an automated actor then DERIVES the projection onto that same branch after CI. The asserted property was true of the commit the author wrote and false of the branch a reader inspects. This is single-party: no competing author exists and none was omitted. INVALID STATE: a published claim about a ref's contents that the ref's own automation can falsify after publication, with nothing that re-checks or withdraws the claim. WHY IT SURVIVES REVIEW: the claim is TRUE when written and true at review time if review reads the author's commit. The falsifying commit is authored by CI, is independently correct, and arrives after the human attention has moved on. Nothing notifies, because deriving a projection produces no conflict. SPECIMEN (PR #10417). The scheme was authority-only editing: touch gunbc.recurring_failure_mode and never docs/design-failure-modes.md. Heal generated the projection onto the branch anyway -- d695b3ab63e is a heal commit adding docs/design-failure-modes.md on top of the author's authority-only commit. The collision property is a fact about the branch AFTER CI, never about the commit the author wrote. Confirmed recurrent rather than incidental, and the OBVIOUS INSTRUMENT FOR THAT IS CONTAMINATED IN THE DIRECTION OF THE CONCLUSION, which is why the count is stated with its discriminator. Selecting heal commits by AUTHOR does not work in this repository: every session commits under the identity gunbc-ci-auto-heal, so that author matches 161 commits touching docs/design-failure-modes.md, of which most are ordinary session work. Heal is separated by its exact commit SUBJECT instead -- "chore: regenerate drifted generated artifacts (ci auto-heal)" -- which yields 54 on that path, against a negated-subject control of 253 that establishes the filter is not itself the answer. THE DISCRIMINATOR THAT BOUNDS IT, which is what makes the row usable rather than a warning: it bites authority-only changes on carriers that HAVE a projection. A sibling lane's authority-only PR on gunbc.guarantee_stall drew zero heal commits, because no generated artifact consumes that carrier. Check whether the carrier has a projection before relying on the property. NOT A WRITER-POPULATION PROBLEM, and this is the distinction that keeps it separate from a_deconfliction_plan_does_not_enumerate_its_writers: no other author is involved, none is omitted, and the author predicted nothing about anyone else. Heal does not COMPETE for the subject -- it derives an artifact from what the author already wrote. Counting it as a member of a writer set would widen that set to "anything that can append a commit", which includes the author's own CI, and the concept stops discriminating. What the two share is only the SYMPTOM that no conflict is produced so nothing notifies -- a symptom with at least three mechanisms, the third being the generated-artifact merge driver refusing with no markers. RUNG: found BELOW the ladder. A public claim silently goes false and no typed refusal fires anywhere. CEILING 3, structurally guaranteed, and REACHABLE WITHOUT NEW CAPABILITY -- which is the whole reason this is its own row. The predicate is single-ref and decidable today: whether the ref changed after the revision the claim was asserted against is answerable from that one ref's history, needing no cross-head enumeration, no open-head discovery and no declaration resolution. Filing it with the writer-population class would state one ceiling for both, park this affordable half behind that class's unbuilt capability, and reproduce exactly the grain mismatch section 4b(3) names as its own review tell. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER: the capability of binding a published branch claim to the revision it was asserted against, so the claim carries its own subject and a later derived push makes it re-adjudicable rather than merely stale. The affordable intermediate is a check that refuses when a claim about a path set is read at a ref whose head postdates the asserted revision. REVIEW TELL: an author explaining that their change CANNOT collide because of which paths it touches. Ask whether the carrier has a generated projection, and whether the property is being asserted about the commit or about the branch a reader will actually fetch.
- **a roster is its own denominator, so the instrument reading it cannot notice the roster is short** (INVALID STATE: a report enumerates the rows a hand-authored roster happens to hold, renders a verdict per row, and presents the result as an answer ABOUT THE SUBJECT -- while no independent authority states how many identities that subject should have, so a subject missing rows renders as a complete block and reads as a complete answer. HARM: every way a roster can lose a row -- a partial edit, a bad merge, a branch mix-up, a deletion nobody reviewed, a row never added -- produces a receipt indistinguishable from the whole truth. The receipt is not wrong about the rows it names; it is silent about the ones it was never asked. And because the silence is total, the reader's only defence is to already know the answer, which is exactly what they consulted the instrument to learn. THE SPECIMEN, found by execution and by an author's own mistake rather than by review (2026-09-04, import-cut lane): gunbc.namespace_cut_subject_observation subject_roster_report was run to establish that the namespace cut's probe identities are PRESENT before the cut. It rendered the ResolverImportNameUniverse subject as three PRESENT rows and stopped. Three further probes belonging to that same subject existed on a different branch and were absent from the tree that ran. The block was well-formed, fully green, and short by half, and nothing in the output distinguished it from the complete subject. The defect was caught only because the author independently remembered that three more probes should be there. WHY IT IS NOT ONE OF THE ROWS BESIDE IT, checked rather than assumed, because three neighbours are close enough to be tempting. It is not `selection_view_read_as_population`: that class turns on membership computed from a MEASURED quantity, so the set moves when the instrument or the load moves. Nothing here is measured -- the roster is hand-authored and perfectly stable -- and the set moves only when an author edits it. The defect is that the authored set is UNJOINED to any statement of what it should be, not that a threshold slid. It is not `check_subject_narrower_than_its_declared_claim`: there, an implementation ranges over less than the claim it is cited for, and the claim exists to be compared against. Here there is NO independent claim about cardinality at all -- the roster is simultaneously the subject definition and the evidence of its own completeness, which is why the comparison that would expose the gap has nothing to stand on. That class is a gap between two statements; this one is the absence of the second statement. It is not `green_reported_over_a_population_the_instrument_does_not_own`: that instrument denominates a real population correctly and merely does not own all of it. This instrument denominates NOTHING -- it has no population, only a list. RUNG FOUND AT: outside the ladder, and this is the honest placement rather than a low one. The gap is invisible from the instrument's own result for the same reason a wall over a clean corpus is green, so there is no executed evidence that could currently discriminate a complete subject from a short one. Naming it mitigatable would be inflation, because nothing contains the harm: the receipt is consumed as though it were total. CEILING: structurally guaranteed. Whether a subject's probe set is complete is decidable ONCE an independent authority states the expected identities, and that authority is derivable rather than hand-countable -- which is what keeps this off the ratchet-forever arm and out of a numeric-literal oracle. A literal expected-count per subject would be the count equality DESIGN section 5 forbids: completeness is an identity join. TRIGGER, FIRST FORM, AND WHY IT WAS UNDER-SPECIFIED -- recorded rather than silently replaced, because the correction is the instructive part. The row first named the trigger as: an independently derived statement of each subject's expected identities, joined at identity grain, refusing a subject the roster does not cover. That is a real climb and it is NOT the structural one. Two lists plus an exact set-equality gate reaches MECHANICALLY PREVENTABLE and stops there, because BOTH lists stay writable and an omission is still an edit the two can share -- a single author dropping a subject from the expected list and the roster in one change satisfies the gate perfectly. Naming a second authority as the trigger for a STRUCTURAL ceiling was the artifact-shaped trigger this ledger warns about, committed inside the row that exists to warn about denominators. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, NAMING THE CAPABILITY: the operational roster is DERIVED as a TOTAL PROJECTION from a closed expected-root coproduct -- so every expected root has a constructor, the projection is exhaustive over it, and a subject omitted from the roster has no way to be written rather than merely being caught. At that point the roster cannot disagree with its denominator because it is not a second list; it is the denominator viewed through a total function. The set-equality gate remains legitimate as the RUNG-2 step on the way there, and it must not be reported as the ceiling reached. WHY IT IS URGENT RATHER THAN INTERESTING: this instrument sits inside the exit criterion of the namespace cut. The final head is to be accepted on its testimony that both import authorities are ABSENT -- and absence is precisely the verdict this defect cannot distinguish from not-having-been-asked. A short roster at the final head would report the cut complete. The class must climb before that receipt is cited, not after. RECOGNITION RULE: when a report's rows come from a hand-authored list, ask what would happen if a row were deleted -- if the answer is 'the report renders one fewer row and says nothing', the list is acting as its own denominator and the report's completeness is an unstated assumption rather than a checked fact.)
- **an instrument is asked about a subject outside its domain and ANSWERS ANYWAY** (INVALID STATE: a query ranges over a domain narrower than the subject it is being read as covering, and a subject falling outside that domain does not come back as UNKNOWN or as a refusal -- it comes back as a confident, well-formed, WRONG answer, whose direction is set by which way the domain gap happens to point. Deletion, modification, or silence, depending on the gap rather than on the subject. HARM: the wrong answer is manufactured work or a manufactured accusation. It is not a missed defect -- it is a defect that does not exist, reported with the authority of a command that ran successfully and exited zero. Downstream, someone debugs a branch nobody tested, reverts a change nobody made, or re-derives a figure that was already correct. TWO RECEIPTS, ONE TREE, ONE DAY, AND EACH INSTRUMENT WAS CORRECT EXACTLY WHERE THE OTHER FAILED (2026-09-04, import-cut lane). FIRST: `git diff --name-only <commit>` was used to derive which paths had changed since a backup ref. It ranges over TRACKED content, so an untracked file -- present in the ref, absent from the index -- rendered as `deleted file mode 100644`, 38 deletions, for a file that was on disk and byte-identical to the ref. The report would have been that a ledger row had been deleted. SECOND: a per-path content comparison was built specifically to escape that tracked-content blindness, hashing every worktree path against the ref. It followed SYMLINKS. Git stores a symlink as mode 120000 whose blob content is the TARGET STRING, so `CLAUDE.md` and `README.md` -- both the literal string `DESIGN.md`, both the same blob -- compared unequal against the hash of DESIGN.md's contents. The report would have been that the design document had been modified, under a hold that forbade tree changes. THE PAIR IS THE FINDING, NOT EITHER HALF. The second instrument was built to fix the first, and it acquired its own blind spot at precisely the place the first was correct: git diff was wrong about untracked files and right about symlinks; the hash loop was right about untracked files and wrong about symlinks. THERE IS NO SAFE INSTRUMENT TO SWITCH TO. Replacing a tool that has a known domain gap with one that does not share it exchanges the gap for a different gap, and the new one is unmapped precisely because it is new. THIRD SPECIMEN, AND THE ONLY ONE WHERE THE INSTRUMENT WAS A PROCEDURE RATHER THAN A TOOL. A reviewer specified a registration check for a newly minted ledger row: confirm `grep -c <identity>` returns 2 in the roster, confirm the row file exists, confirm the projection join is clean. Every one of those checks passed. The merge that satisfied them was a roster conflict in which two lanes had appended to the same list, and a resolution taking only the author's side would have passed ALL THREE while silently deleting two rows the other lane filed that day. The check's domain was THE AUTHOR'S OWN ROWS. Asked about the roster -- a shared, append-only carrier -- it answered confidently and wrongly, because a procedure that verifies your additions cannot see another party's deletions. The repair is the same shape as the tool cases: the union resolution was verified in BOTH directions, the other lane's identities as well as the author's. Worth carrying because a hand-written procedure feels domain-free in a way a tool does not, and it is not: every check ranges over something, and the thing it ranges over is usually what its author was thinking about. THE CONSEQUENCE THAT MAKES THIS THE SHARPEST OF THE THREE: a domain gap in a hand-written verification procedure is INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM CORRECTNESS BY ANY CHECK INSIDE THAT PROCEDURE. A tool's gap can at least be found by reading its documentation; a procedure's gap is bounded by what its author had in mind, and the procedure contains no record of what that was. WHY IT IS NOT `empty_capture_read_as_clean_result`: that class is an EMPTY capture consumed as a finding of nothing -- zero bytes on a redirect, a log that reads zero with no refusal. Every instance on it is about reading NOTHING. Both instruments here returned rich, plausible, specific content. Nothing was empty; the content was about the wrong subject. WHY IT IS NOT `check_subject_narrower_than_its_declared_claim`: there the harm is a false GREEN -- coverage smaller than the claim it is cited for, so a real defect passes unseen. Here the harm is a false FINDING, a defect reported where none exists. Opposite direction, opposite consequence, and the repairs do not transfer: widening coverage does not stop a narrow instrument from confidently answering outside itself. RUNG: outside the ladder. Neither near-miss was caught by a mechanism. Both were caught because a human found ONE ROW SURPRISING and investigated it -- and in both cases the fallback defence was the reader already knowing the answer, which this ledger criticises elsewhere and which is therefore not a defence that may be cited here. There is currently no executed evidence that would discriminate a domain-gap answer from a true one. CEILING: mechanically preventable, and stated as rung 2 rather than reaching for higher, because the domain of an ad-hoc query is not in general decidable from the query. What IS decidable is whether the subject population and the instrument population agree, so the honest ceiling is a gate that compares them and refuses on any subject the instrument cannot range over. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, NAMING THE CAPABILITY AND NOT THE TWO SPECIMENS: the class closes when an instrument CANNOT ANSWER AT ALL until it has established a DECLARED DOMAIN -- its admitted subject kinds, its acquisition population, and its identity semantics -- and the requested subject carries a TYPED RELATION to that domain: SubjectWithinDomain, or InstrumentOutOfDomain { uncovered_subjects }. From the second, NO count, delta, deletion, modification, absence or zero is constructible. Fixing git diff and making a hash loop symlink-aware would discharge nothing: those are the two gaps we happen to have met, and the pair above is the receipt that the next instrument has the next gap. WHAT THE DOMAIN MUST SPEAK ABOUT, for the file-population instruments this repository actually uses: tracked, untracked, deleted, renamed, regular file, SYMLINK-AS-LINK-IDENTITY, symlink target, and generated or ignored where relevant. A census need not ADMIT every one of those kinds -- it must REFUSE when asked about a kind it does not admit. A TWO-WAY IDENTITY JOIN IS NECESSARY AND NOT SUFFICIENT, which is the subtlest part of this class and was found only by pushing on it. A two-way join closes substitution and unilateral omission. It does NOT close a shared domain gap: if the real subject contains X, the acquisition omits X because X is untracked, and the EXPECTED side is derived through that same acquisition, then expected omits X, observed omits X, and the two-way join reports EQUAL while the class survives untouched. So an expected-identity authority must be independent in TWO dimensions -- at IDENTITY grain and at ACQUISITION grain. An expected set projected from the same scan that produces the observation is not independent; it is the same blindness counted twice. SCOPE, STATED SO THIS ROW CANNOT BECOME AN ABSORBING BLOCKER: the obligation is NOT that every instrument in the repository proves its domain. It is that every instrument whose answer is CONSUMED by a program relying on this class must establish its domain before its verdict is admitted, and that population is derivable from the consumers. The honest immediate climb for those routes is OutsideTheLadder to MechanicallyPreventable; structural impossibility across the repository is not being claimed. THE COROLLARY FOR ANY DELETION CENSUS, which is where this class does the most damage: a corpus-deletion census has an IMPLICIT DENOMINATOR -- every source entity capable of belonging to the subject. If its acquisition is git-tracked files, then an untracked source is NOT ABSENT, it is NOT OBSERVED, and the census reports the deletion as complete. Where symlinks occur in the population, the census must decide WHICH FACT it claims: repository object identity, repository path identity, link target bytes, or resolved contents. Those are four different subjects, and a verification that had to be made symlink-aware after the fact is the evidence that 'same contents' was ambiguous until link semantics were fixed. WHY THIS ROW EXISTS BEFORE ITS CENSUS RUNS: the namespace cut's own machinery is delta instruments end to end -- a corpus delta, a census worked to zero, an exit criterion joining two populations. Each is a place a domain gap returns a confident wrong COUNT rather than a refusal, and today's two receipts are the evidence that reading the output does not reveal it. Filed before the census, this is a class the census can be designed against; filed after, it is a postmortem. RECOGNITION RULE: when an instrument reports a change you did not make, do not reconcile around it and do not reach for a different tool -- ask what population the query actually ranged over, and whether the surprising subject is inside it. A surprising row is the only signal this class emits, and it emits it exactly once, before the wrong answer becomes a report.)
- **a check's discriminating RED is authored as rows in the LIVE SUBJECT LIST the check reads** (the probe is genuine, the arm it exercises is genuine, and the expected verdict is a refusal -- so every ordinary run of the instrument refuses about one of its own subjects, and the instrument's answer over the real population is knowingly wrong by construction). NOTHING LOOKS WRONG AT THE AUTHORING SITE, which is what separates this from ordinary bad data. The evidence is correct, the arm is correct, the annotation explaining why the rows are deliberately wrong is correct, and the author can defend every one of those facts individually. The defect is not the evidence but its HOME: it is the exact inverse of DESIGN section 4b's boundary, which locates an authorable invalid state in SOURCE HANDED TO THE COMPILER BY A FIXTURE. A fixture population is disposable and private to the check; an operational population is what the instrument testifies about. THE MECHANISM THAT HIDES IT IS THE MISSING VERDICT, and this is the sharp part. The contamination is invisible exactly IN PROPORTION to how badly the instrument reports: an instrument that cannot refuse on its own uncertainty emits the honest per-row word -- UNOBSERVABLE -- into a report nobody adjudicates, and exits 0. So the report is truthful and the verdict is not, and no gate anywhere can see the difference. Strengthening the verdict is therefore what SURFACES the class rather than what fixes it, and a lane that adds the refusal without moving the evidence will simply start failing on its own fixtures. THE DETECTOR IS A QUESTION ABOUT INTENT, ASKED STRUCTURALLY: does any row in the operational population have an EXPECTED verdict that is a refusal? If yes, the population is carrying evidence and the instrument's answer over it is not a measurement. It does not require reading the prose -- an annotation explaining that rows are deliberately wrong is itself the tell, because a correct operational row needs no such explanation. SPECIMEN, gunbc#10454, and it is the reason this row exists. gunbc.namespace_cut_subject_roster carried two HostRustItemProbe rows citing src/v1/stage0/src/cli_run.rs for build_import_adjacency and import_closure_from_adjacency, both of which had MOVED to src/v1/stage0/src/cli_run/entry_resolve.rs. They were retained deliberately, with an annotation, as the discriminating RED for the stale-citation refusal arm in gunbc.namespace_cut_subject_observation host_probe_over_items -- an arm that is real and whose red is real. But gunbc.namespace_cut_subject_observation subject_roster_report wrote its report and returned ExitSuccess without ever inspecting whether any probe was Unobservable, so every pre-cut run of the cut's own exit-criterion instrument reported that it could not observe two of its subjects and exited 0. codex review 60319 named it. The repair was all three at once: correct the citations, move the RED to test.claim.namespace_cut_stale_citation_witness where host_probe_over_items was already parameterized on its item population for exactly that purpose, and make the command refuse on any Unobservable probe. IT GENERALISES PAST INSTRUMENTS to any check whose regression probe lives in the population it polices -- a lint whose failing case is committed source, a migration census whose expecting-red subject is a live row, a conformance suite whose counterexample is an enrolled module. Wherever the check's denominator and its evidence are the same set, the check's answer over the real population is off by its own evidence, and the report cannot say so. Rung found at 1: the harm was contained only because a reader adjudicated a report the command had already greened. Rung after gunbc#10454 is 2, mechanically preventable -- the command now refuses on any Unobservable probe, folded from the observation coproduct with no catch-all arm, so a new verdict variant fails to compile rather than defaulting to not-a-problem; but the invalid state is still WRITABLE and safety depends on that refusal executing and staying enrolled. CEILING 4, structural impossibility, and it is genuinely reachable because the distinction is decidable at construction: an operational subject roster and a fixture population are different kinds, and only one of them may carry a row whose expected verdict is a refusal. NEXT TRIGGER, capability and not artifact: a probe-population type distinguishing operationally-asserted subjects from fixture-authored ones, SUFFICIENT FOR a probe whose expected verdict is a refusal to have NO CONSTRUCTOR in an operational roster. The fixture file that discharged this specimen is not the trigger -- it relocated one instance and leaves the next roster equally able to accept evidence rows. 
- **a printed remedy is mangled by the medium that prints it** (an instruction is authored correctly, and the channel that carries it to its reader REWRITES it in transit. The author reads the authored string and sees the remedy; the reader receives something shorter that no longer does what it says; and no error crosses the boundary, because the rewriting is the medium behaving exactly as specified. THE RECOGNITION RULE, and it is the whole row: THE SENDER SEES INTACT TEXT, THE RECIPIENT GETS A SILENTLY SHORTENED SENTENCE, AND NOTHING REPORTS A FAILURE. The send succeeds. So every instrument that reads the AUTHORED artifact -- grep, review, a byte-exact drift gate over the source -- agrees the remedy is present, because it is present. The defect is in the rendering, which means the subject has to be what the reader RECEIVES and never what the author wrote. FIRST SPECIMEN, calm-owl-417, gunbc#10460, and it is the one that names the class: gunbc.generated_artifact_merge_driver prints its repair recipe through a double-quoted `echo`, because the lines are templates and `$merged_path` must interpolate. Double quotes leave the backquote active, and the recipe's sed program wrote its capture group between backquotes. Bash ran `\([a-z0-9_]*\)` as a COMMAND SUBSTITUTION at echo time: two `command not found` lines on stderr, and the printed instruction came out with the capture group DELETED. Pasted, it extracted zero rows from both sides of a set difference, and a difference over two empty sets is empty -- which the recipe's own words read as `MUST BE EMPTY`. The mangled remedy reported the vacuous pass the intact remedy exists to prevent, at the exact moment rows were at risk. SECOND SPECIMEN, same PR, and it is the same boundary read from the other side rather than a repeat: `$` stays active ON PURPOSE, so a `$1` written into a recipe line is the DRIVER's argv and not the reader's. An extractor drafted as a shell function taking `$1` rendered as `git show "O:docs/design-rung-drops.md"` -- git's own `%O` placeholder substituted into the instruction it was printing. Escaping does not save that one, because the escaper doubles backslashes first, so the remedy is to name only the variable the printer DEFINES. The two specimens together are why the class is the MEDIUM and not the escaping: one is a character the medium consumed, the other is a character the medium substituted, and no single escaping rule addresses both. THE DAMAGE IS NOT PROPORTIONAL TO THE CORRUPTION, and this is the sharpest available statement of the class. The repaired extractor carries TWO sed clauses, because the two delegated projections spell a row identity differently -- a backquoted slug bullet and a `### Title` heading. Mangling one character in the FIRST clause makes sed refuse the ENTIRE program on an invalid backreference, so the second clause, which nothing touched, extracts nothing either. Measured against a fixture carrying both spellings and 157 rostered rows: the intact program names 157, the mangled program names ZERO -- not 36, which is what a reader assuming per-clause degradation would predict. One corrupted character silenced a projection it never appeared in. THIRD AND FOURTH SPECIMENS, 2026-09-05, AND THEY ARE WHAT MAKE THIS A CLASS RATHER THAN A FACT ABOUT MERGE DRIVERS: the same mechanism fired twice more within ninety minutes, in a completely different medium, in messages ABOUT this class. An agent messaging body was passed to `dashboard-message --body` as a double-quoted shell argument; the body contained backquoted identifiers; bash substituted them away and the recipients received sentences with phrases missing. One of the two was a message whose SUBJECT was instruments that report success without carrying their claim. The other was sent by the author repairing the first specimen, while writing about the first specimen, roughly an hour after reading the analysis describing it. WHAT THOSE TWO ADD: the class is not a property of emitted artifacts or of merge drivers. It is a property of ANY text handed to a shell-quoted channel, and the agent messaging channel is one of them and is uninstrumented at both ends. THE OBVIOUS DEFENCE IS THE THING THAT FAILED, and a reader will supply the wrong remedy here unless it is stated. `Remember to quote correctly` was in active working memory in both messaging specimens and did not prevent either. The diagnosis in both cases was identical: the protection was attached to a command SPELLING rather than to the hazard -- one command was being single-quoted correctly all evening, and a DIFFERENT command was reached for with double quotes without re-deriving the rule. Vigilance that is bound to a habit does not transfer to the next call site. THE MECHANICAL FORM, which is the operative remedy and is cheap: write the body to a file with a QUOTED heredoc, then pass `"$(cat file)"`. Single-quoting the argument is insufficient, not merely awkward, because prose reliably contains apostrophes and one apostrophe ends the quoting. THE MANGLING IS NOT MERELY LOSSY -- IT EXECUTES, IN THE SENDER'S ENVIRONMENT, AND THAT IS THE MOST UNDER-STATED PROPERTY OF THIS CLASS. Discovered as physical residue rather than by reasoning: the third specimen's message contained the phrase `-> Bool` inside a double-quoted argument, and the shell read it as a COMMAND `-` that was not found, followed by a REDIRECTION that created an empty file named `Bool` in the repository working tree. It sat there untracked for fifteen minutes and was found by `git status`, not by anything watching the channel. The general statement is worse than the instance: a backquoted span inside double quotes is COMMAND SUBSTITUTION, which is arbitrary execution, and an unquoted redirection operator writes files. So the sender's mistake is not that a sentence arrived shortened; it is that a fragment of English prose was EVALUATED AS A PROGRAM against a live working tree. The lossy reading of this class is the benign one. AND IT WAS ONE ROUTINE COMMAND FROM BEING COMMITTED: the author of that instance stages with `git add -A`, so the next commit would have swept an artifact of a mangled chat message into a pull request, with a plausible-looking name and no origin anyone could reconstruct. THE REASON IT DID NOT LAND IS COMMIT ORDERING AND NOTHING ELSE: the last commit happened to precede the corruption by fifteen minutes. Recording that as `it did not land` would be luck reported as safety, which is the reporting failure this ledger exists to refuse -- no mechanism was involved, nothing detected it, and nothing would have blocked it. The file's name resembles a type this corpus uses everywhere, so review would most likely have read it as an intentional stray rather than as residue, and its provenance is unreconstructable from the diff. THE CHECK THIS LICENSES, and it caught something on its FIRST application in a second tree: inspect `git status` for unexplained worktree changes after any session that has sent shell-quoted messages, and do not stage with `git add -A` in a tree where that has happened. Applied immediately by a second author, it found no execution residue but did surface a forgotten STAGED modification to a generated artifact, checked out during verification and left behind -- a different cause with the same shape, on a branch that was main. GENERALIZATION: the hazard is not restricted to mangled messages. ANY command that writes into the worktree as a side effect of verification leaves residue a reviewer cannot distinguish from intent. The heredoc form prevents the cause; the status check catches damage already done, whatever wrote it. THE TRANSPORT'S SUCCESS SIGNAL WAS NEVER ASKED THE RELEVANT QUESTION, and this is the precise form of `no error crosses the boundary`. Successful delivery answers whether the SUBMITTED payload reached its destination. It does not answer whether that payload preserved the INTENDED message. Those are two different claims, and every send in every specimen above was truthful about the first while nothing anywhere was asked the second. A PRESERVATION CHECK MUST BE ANCHORED BEFORE THE DANGEROUS TRANSFORMATION, AND THE OBVIOUS IMPLEMENTATION IS WORTHLESS. The natural control is to digest what was sent and compare it with what arrived. If that digest is computed AFTER the shell has consumed the backquoted spans, it faithfully attests the CORRUPTED payload and reports a perfect match: both sides agree, the comparison is sound, and the defect is invisible, because the damage happened upstream of the first observation. The comparison must STRADDLE the transformation that does the damage -- intended payload captured BEFORE it, against payload received after it -- which is this row's own recognition rule applied to its own remedy. It is the same shape as the neighbouring instruments in this ledger, one layer further out: `comm` cannot report that its own operand was empty, a digest over emitted driver bytes proves an escape was APPLIED but not that it was CORRECT, and a preservation digest taken downstream of the corruption proves only that the corruption was transmitted reliably. QUALIFICATION, so a correct transport does not read as corruption: where a channel LEGITIMATELY transforms encoding or line endings, the comparison needs an explicit preservation contract stating what is allowed to change. THE RECEIVING END FAILS TOO, AND THAT COMPLETES THE PICTURE. Recorded as evidence about DETECTABILITY rather than about the mechanism: in the third specimen a reader received the corrupted message, read it, and did not identify it as corrupted. The missing expressions and an injected directory listing were both present in what they received, and the surrounding prose still supported the conclusions the conversation reached -- so the damage was survivable enough to be invisible, and they flagged only afterwards that those spans should have been treated as corrupt. So there are THREE parties and no signal at any of them: the sender sees intact text, the transport reports success against a question about a different payload, and the recipient reads fluent prose with holes in it. THE CLASS FIRED IN PRODUCTION ON THE BRANCH THAT CLOSES IT, AT EQUAL ROW COUNTS, and this receipt is a discriminating RED on the real acceptance path rather than a fixture. Merging main into that branch reached this driver on docs/design-failure-modes.md. It refused as designed -- zero conflict markers, three index stages, the ours side left clean -- and the repaired step 2, run verbatim, measured: base 126 rows, ours 126 rows, COUNTS EQUAL, with the ours side dropping exactly one row that main had added, `duplicate_record_literal_field_silently_last_wins`, and adding one of its own. A cardinality check passes on that comparison and reports nothing. Only the identity join names the casualty. So the case a reviewer had challenged the branch to cover -- a substitution that preserves the count -- arrived unprompted, on the same projection, within minutes of the controls for it being enrolled; the branch did not have to argue that the case was reachable, because it walked in. RUNG FOUND AT: silent wrongness wherever the subject read by any instrument is the authored string, because there the defect is invisible by construction; mitigatable where a witness reads the EMITTED artifact, which is where gunbc#10460 put it. CEILING: mechanically preventable for media this repository emits into -- a witness that executes the emitted artifact and reads what the reader receives cannot be satisfied by an authored string, and the shell-quoting encodings have single authorities that a hand-spelled call site can be made not to bypass. It stays MITIGATABLE for channels this repository does not own, such as an external tool's argv handling, which is observed rather than proven. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER: the bash medium read forward from grammar rows, so a printed line's variable references and shell-active characters are DECLARED rather than spelled -- at which point no emitter concatenates a shell literal and the escaping arm has nothing to get wrong. That is the same trigger gunbc.generated_artifact_merge_driver generated_artifact_merge_driver_emit_dissolution_trigger already carries, and it is named here rather than restated because one capability retires both. DISTINGUISHING FACT AGAINST ITS NEAREST NEIGHBOURS. It is NOT `empty_capture_read_as_clean_result`: there a well-formed refusal travels on a stream the reader did not keep, and the reader's error is consuming the emptiness; here the text is rewritten before anyone reads it and the reader's conduct is irrelevant. The empty operand is one CONSEQUENCE of this class, not the class, and the second specimen proves it -- a substituted positional argument is not an empty capture at all. It is NOT `restoration_promise_names_a_route_that_does_not_exist`: there the named route is absent; here it exists and the rendering breaks it.)
- **a comparison keys on a PROPER COMPONENT of the identity it is comparing, while both sides carry the whole one** (INVALID STATE: a decision over elements identified by a composite -- module path plus declaration name, path plus revision, host plus port, run id plus attempt -- reduces the deciding key to a component of that identity, and the discarded component is present, in scope, and used elsewhere in the same function. The decision may be a membership or equality test between two populations, or a LOOKUP over one; the shape of the decision is not the defect, the projection of the key is. HARM: two distinct elements that agree on the retained component are ONE key, so a member of one population is accepted as a member of the other by an element it is not. The wrong answer is per-element and silent: nothing is missing, nothing is unreached, and the diagnostic surface never fires. Recognition rule: at the deciding line, is the key equal to the identity, or to a projection of it? The tell is that the DISCARDED component reappears a few lines later to RENDER the finding -- the code already knows the full identity and uses it for the message, having thrown it away for the decision. NO ACCOUNTING EQUALITY DETECTS THIS, WHICH IS WHY IT NEEDS ITS OWN ROW. Denominator reconciliation -- checked == declared, checked + excluded == population, subject-narrowing checks of every kind -- verifies that every element was EXAMINED, and every one of them survives here, because the subject was complete and each element received an answer. The defect is in the ANSWER, not in the population, so the whole family of narrowing checks is structurally blind to it. Specimen gunbc#10496, measured across two arms in one dispatch over one corpus: every COUNT line was byte-identical between the correct key and the defective one, and only the discriminating control moved. IT PRESENTS AS LATENT AND ITS LATENCY IS AN ACCIDENT, NOT A GUARD. The retained component is usually unique in the corpus that exists on the day it is written, so the defective key answers correctly for every element and the class ships green. Current-corpus uniqueness is not an enforced precondition and stops being true the week someone adds a row, so a repair justified by a collision census expires; the repair has to stand on the key being wrong. THE DISCRIMINATING CONTROL IS THE COLLISION, AND NO ORDINARY CONTROL SUBSTITUTES FOR IT: two elements sharing the retained component, differing in the discarded one, with only one of them a member. A control that drops a member and expects it to be reported passes under both the correct and the defective key -- they agree whenever the retained component is unique -- so it proves the check runs and proves nothing about its key. Specimen gunbc#10503: the collision control refuses under the defective key by reporting NOTHING, which the join reads as ControlDidNotFire. WHAT THE NEIGHBOURING ROWS DO NOT COVER, checked before this row was filed. check_subject_narrower_than_its_declared_claim is the opposite shape: there the subject is a strict subset of the claim, here the subject is complete and the verdict on a member is wrong. surface_shorthand_preempts_resolved_identity is the nearest neighbour and is genuinely adjacent -- deciding on a spelling where a resolved identity was available -- but its rule is CONSTRUCTION totality over a resolved binding and its trigger repairs evaluator value construction, which leaves a COMPARISON keyed on a component untouched. identity_absent_graph_traversal is the case where no identity is available to key on at all; here it was present on both sides and discarded. incidental_denominator_as_wall names the accidental-safety shape but its invalid state is a destructive operation guarded by an unrelated upstream filter, where this class has nothing guarding it -- the key itself is wrong. Next-rung trigger, named as the capability rather than as an artifact: composite identities are carried as a TYPE whose equality is the identity, so a comparison cannot be written against one of its components -- at which point the key and the identity cannot differ and there is nothing for a control to discriminate. Until then this is review diligence plus a per-site collision control, which is mechanically preventable at best.)**A THIRD AXIS: CI EVIDENCE IDENTITY, WHERE THE COMPOSITE IS NOT A DECLARATION BUT A RUN** (calm-eagle-759 and bright-ram-778, 2026-09-05, filed here rather than as a new row because the mechanism is identical and only the subject differs). A verdict about a pull request was assembled by keying on `run_id`, when the identity of a set of job results is (run_id, ATTEMPT) -- a re-run KEEPS THE SAME RUN ID. One level up, the same reduction happens on the check-suite when the identity is (suite, EVENT), because a workflow_dispatch run emits the SAME job names as the pull_request run at the same head. Both times the discarded component was present and available, and both times it reappeared afterwards to EXPLAIN the result, which is this row's own tell. THIS INSTANCE REPAIRED THIS ROW'S OWN INVALID STATE, AND IT IS A REPAIR RATHER THAN AN EXTENSION -- which is the distinction a reviewer should check. The three parts of this row did not agree with each other. The HARM sentence says two distinct elements that agree on the retained component are one key; the RECOGNITION RULE asks whether the key is equal to the identity or to a projection of it. Both are already general over ANY keyed decision. Only the invalid state said 'between two populations'. So the row already asserted the damage this instance exhibits, and its opening sentence had simply failed to admit the shape that produces it. The invalid state above is now written to what the harm sentence already covered -- comparison and lookup alike -- rather than enumerating a second admitted shape beside the first, which would invite another append the first time someone finds a keyed join or a keyed dedup. THE FOUR NEIGHBOURS THIS ROW ARGUES AGAINST WERE RE-READ AT THE NEW WIDTH, because a widening can silently swallow a neighbour and merge two classes instead of repairing one. All four arguments still hold. check_subject_narrower_than_its_declared_claim is about POPULATION completeness and says nothing about the key, so widening the decision shape does not touch it. identity_absent_graph_traversal still turns on AVAILABILITY -- there no identity exists to key on, here it was present and discarded. incidental_denominator_as_wall still turns on an unrelated upstream filter supplying accidental safety, where this class has nothing guarding it. surface_shorthand_preempts_resolved_identity is the one worth stating precisely, because it is nearest and the gap narrows: there the key is a SURFACE SPELLING standing in for a resolved binding, which is a different REPRESENTATION of the identity, not a COMPONENT of it -- and its harm is a wrongly constructed value rather than two distinct elements colliding under one key. Representation substitution and identity projection remain different relations at the new width.)THE TELL THAT NEEDS NO CONCEPT OF ATTEMPTS OR EVENTS, and it is the one to teach: A DUPLICATE JOB NAME AT ONE HEAD. `GET /commits/<sha>/check-runs` returned SIX rows for THREE job names, with `heal-generated-artifacts` present twice carrying DIFFERENT statuses. A reader who has never heard of run attempts can see that a name-keyed read is ambiguous, because the same name answers twice and disagrees with itself. The repair is to resolve the latest run at the head FILTERED BY EVENT and then read THAT RUN'S OWN jobs endpoint, which returns the current attempt's jobs as a set rather than an accumulated history. AND THE ACCUMULATION IS NOT INCIDENTAL -- IT IS THE NORMAL CASE FOR AN ENTIRE CLASS OF PULL REQUEST, WHICH IS WHAT MAKES THIS A WALL RATHER THAN A CURIOSITY (confirmed by execution 2026-09-05, not inferred: bright-ram-778 posted to the run's approve endpoint and watched attempt 1 become attempt 2 on the SAME run id, with no new run created). The generating sequence is mechanical: heal repairs a generated projection by pushing a BOT-AUTHORED commit to the branch; GitHub withholds workflow execution on a bot-authored head, so the gating pull_request run lands completed/action_required with ZERO JOBS; a human approves; and the approval executes as attempt 2 of the same run. Every ledger pull request that heal touches therefore carries an accumulated head, and a gate reading per-job results off the head's check-runs mixes attempts on essentially all of them. TWO ADJACENT READINGS THAT THE SAME SEQUENCE PRODUCES, AND BOTH READ AS GOOD NEWS. `completed` DOES NOT MEAN RAN: an action_required run is terminal and executed nothing, so a break condition keyed on status alone reports a verdict that does not exist. And counting failures over that run's jobs array returns zero, because the array is EMPTY -- the empty-population reading of empty_observation_narrow, arriving on a run that did no work at all. Read the CONCLUSION, and refuse to score any run whose job count is zero.HOW IT WAS FOUND IS THE PART WORTH KEEPING, AND IT IS NOT ONE LANE'S CLEVERNESS. The suite-level half was found by wise-koi-96 and confirmed by calm-eagle-759 on gunbc#10504. The instrument that the attempt-level half broke was bright-ram-778's, which had bound run identity out of a details_url and treated one run id as one coherent verdict. It surfaced only because bright-ram-778 went to VERIFY calm-eagle-759's event discriminator rather than adopt it, and calm-eagle-759 had separately disclosed that its own replacement watcher was attempt-safe by luck rather than by design. Two instruments checking each other found what neither found alone, and each author's report of their own tool's weakness is what made the check possible.)
- **a scripted edit locates its subject CORRECTLY and then delimits the extent with a landmark the subject does not own, so it consumes whatever sits between** (INVALID STATE: a rewrite whose START bound is the subject's own identity and whose END bound is the next occurrence of an unrelated construct -- the following declaration, the next blank line, the closing delimiter of an enclosing form. HARM: everything between the subject's true end and that landmark is rewritten or deleted, and it is material the edit was never about. THE KEY IS NOT THE DEFECT, WHICH IS WHAT SEPARATES THIS FROM ITS NEAREST NEIGHBOUR. comparison_keyed_on_part_of_the_identity_it_compares is about a DECISION keyed on a projection of a composite identity; here the locator is exactly right and matches exactly the intended subject. A correctly-keyed operation over the wrong range is still wrong, so the class survives when the key is the whole identity, and repairing keys does not touch it. Its sibling edit_pass_that_matched_nothing_reports_success is the case where the locator binds to nothing at all; here it binds correctly and then overreaches. WHAT IT EATS IS SYSTEMATICALLY THE MATERIAL NOTHING CAN CHECK. The space between two declarations is where prose lives -- DESIGN section 4c annotations, which no Accepted program can read. So the collateral is invisible to every compiler, lens and witness by construction, and no gate anywhere can fire on it. RECEIPT (calm-eagle-759, 2026-09-05, on the gunbc#10547 authoring path): a pass replacing four predicate bodies cut from each function's opening line to the NEXT function's opening line, and swallowed the annotation blocks between them, including a scaffold-note declaration and a long emit-disposition rationale. Intended scope was four function bodies. THE ONLY SIGNAL THAT EXISTED was the diffstat being roughly five times the expected deletion count; nothing else in the toolchain distinguished the two outcomes. THE AGGRAVATING FACTOR IS A SEPARATE FACT AND IT IS WHAT TURNS A CAUGHT MISTAKE INTO A PERSISTED ONE: an edit-then-restore chain has an UNPROTECTED WINDOW. Sequencing a restore after the command is not the same as guaranteeing it. RECEIPT, same lane and same day: a mutate-compile-restore chain hit a foreground timeout during the compile, which killed the chain BETWEEN the mutation and the restore. The restore was not skipped by a bug -- it was never reached, and the mutated file survived the failure. A restore that is a later step in the same sequence is not a restore; it is a step that usually runs. Recognition rule: for any scripted edit, ask what defines the END of the range and whether the SUBJECT owns it. A bound named by the next sibling, the enclosing form, or a file-wide landmark is owned by something else, and everything between belongs to neither. The review tell is a diffstat whose deletion count exceeds what the change describes, since the eaten material is by definition not what the author was talking about. Next-rung trigger, named as the capability and not as an artifact: EDITS ARE EXPRESSED AGAINST A PARSED DECLARATION'S OWN SPAN RATHER THAN AGAINST TEXT BETWEEN LANDMARKS, sufficient that an edit cannot name an extent its subject does not own -- at which point the invalid state has no constructor. The substrate already computes that span: it is what v1.compiler.core diagnostic_to_span answers for any node. Until an edit path consumes it, this is mitigatable only, and the mitigation is the diffstat check plus a restore that is guaranteed rather than sequenced.)
- **several independent total matches over ONE coproduct jointly define a single disposition, each defaulting through a wildcard, so no one of them is the authority and agreement rests on an author remembering to edit all of them** (INVALID STATE: a subject's classification is answered by N separate predicates, each an allowlist under its own catch-all, where the answer a consumer wants is a JOIN across them. HARM: a member added to one list and omitted from the others lands in every catch-all at once and is classified by default in each -- silently, since every match is total and every one compiles. IT IS NOT parallel_representation_debt, AND THE DIFFERENCE IS WHY THIS ONE IS WORSE. That class is a hand-authored fold DUPLICATING a canonical relation that already exists, and its remedy is deletion in favour of the authority that is sitting right there. Here the matches JOINTLY DEFINE the disposition: none of them is canonical, there is no relation to defer to, and there is nothing to delete toward. The repair is not removal but CONSTRUCTION of the authority that was never written. THE WILDCARD IS WHAT MAKES IT SILENT, AND ALSO WHAT MAKES IT LOOK FINE. Every catch-all is chosen to be the safe answer, so the default is usually correct and the class ships green for years. What is lost is not correctness but INTENT: a member classified by omission and a member classified by decision are indistinguishable, so the next member that genuinely needs a non-default answer re-enters the gap in exactly the same way. A reviewer reading any single match sees a short, plainly-correct allowlist. SPECIMEN, and the shape is stronger evidence than any count: v1.compiler.core CompilerDiagnostic was classified by is_error_diagnostic, is_interpreter_blocking_diagnostic and is_discovery_corpus_advisory_typecheck_diagnostic -- three independent wildcard matches over one coproduct -- with is_discovery_corpus_blocking_diagnostic short-circuiting on the second. The great majority of variants were named in NONE of them and defaulted through every wildcard simultaneously. The predicted fork had already occurred: ComplexityUnknown sat in the blocking-false list and in no advisory list, so it was rendered while counted by neither total, and the gate's two headline figures did not partition what the compiler emitted. Repaired in gunbc#10547; read that diff for the before state rather than a number here. SEVERITY AND ITS SIBLING AXIS ARE TWO FACTS, WHICH IS WHY THE REPAIR IS A PRODUCT AND NOT A SUM. Collapsing N matches into one N-armed disposition is the tempting shape and it is lossy whenever two members agree on one axis and differ on another -- in the specimen, two variants shared a gate answer while disagreeing on error-ness, so a single disposition sum could not have answered is_error without silently flipping them. Check for that before choosing the carrier's shape; a lossy consolidation is a behaviour change wearing a refactor's description. Recognition rule: when a consumer joins two or more predicates to get one answer, ask which of them is the AUTHORITY. If the honest answer is that the disposition exists only in the join, the authority has not been written. The mechanical tell is N total matches over the same coproduct in one module where the union of their named arms is a small fraction of the declared variants. Next-rung trigger, named as the capability and not as an artifact: THE DISPOSITION HAS ONE TOTAL, WILDCARD-FREE AUTHORITY FROM WHICH EVERY PREDICATE IS DERIVED, sufficient that a new member cannot compile until an author states its disposition -- at which point classification-by-omission has no constructor. That is structurally impossible for the omission half. Two regressions remain below it and are NOT covered by exhaustiveness, because both are legal source: re-adding a wildcard to the authority, and minting a fresh predicate beside the derived ones. Those stay mechanically preventable and need a reader that ranges over the subject; see the specimen's own residue statement in gunbc#10547.)
- **a gate waits for a shared resource to become idle, and that resource's ARRIVAL RATE exceeds its SERVICE RATE, so the release condition is an absorbing state no amount of patience resolves** (INVALID STATE: a wait whose release predicate is 'nothing is currently in flight on R', where work arrives at R faster than R completes it. HARM: the gate does not gate. The occasions it releases are GAPS IN TRAFFIC rather than the condition being satisfied, so what looks like the guard working is the arrival process pausing. THE FAILURE IS SILENT IN THE DIRECTION OF PATIENCE, WHICH IS WHY IT SURVIVES REVIEW AND OPERATION ALIKE. Nothing goes wrong. Nothing is corrupted, nothing is skipped, and no one is visibly blocked -- the waiter simply waits, reports that it is waiting, and is believed. The entire cost is throughput that nobody attributes to the guard, and a guard that never fires is indistinguishable from a guard that is working from every seat including the author's. Contrast the failure mode everyone watches for, where a broken wait releases too early and something downstream breaks loudly. IT IS NOT absorbing_fallback, AND THE MECHANISMS ARE OPPOSITE. That class WIDENS in order to avoid refusing -- it answers, and the answer is the superset. This one never reaches its release condition at all and therefore never answers. They are adjacent because both convert a deficit into something that reads as normal operation, and they need opposite repairs: the absorbing fallback must be made to refuse, and this one must be given a release condition that is actually reachable. RECOGNITION RULE, AND IT MUST BE ASKED BEFORE THE WAIT IS WRITTEN, because after-the-fact detection is close to impossible by construction. The rule is NOT 'check whether the resource is busy' -- that is precisely the observation the guard already makes, and it reads healthy every time. It is the RATE COMPARISON: what is the arrival rate of the thing I am waiting to drain, and what is its service rate? If arrivals win, the wait is an absorbing state, and the correct move is to key the gate on something that terminates. SPECIMEN, and the CLAIM IS THE RELATION rather than any number (bright-ram-778's measurement, 2026-09-05, cited to its producer so a reader re-derives it rather than trusting a transcription). A merge gate was keyed on 'main has no required run in flight'. The producer is `gh api "repos/gunb-ai/gunbc/actions/workflows/witnesses.yml/runs?branch=main&per_page=8"`: created_at deltas give the arrival rate, created_at to updated_at gives the service rate. Arrivals are faster than service, so an in-flight run on main is the STEADY STATE and the release condition is almost never true. The one occasion the gate released was a gap in pushes. Note that created_at is ISO with a Z and is safe to compare as a string, unlike git's %ad, which carries per-commit timezone offsets and is not. THE REPAIR IS TO KEY ON A TERMINATING FACT: main's LAST COMPLETED run and its verdict, rather than the absence of a running one. A completed run always exists once the workflow has ever run, so the predicate terminates, and it answers the question the gate actually cared about -- is the base healthy -- rather than a proxy that happens to be unreachable. Next-rung trigger, named as the capability and not as an artifact: A WAIT'S RELEASE CONDITION IS ACCOMPANIED BY EVIDENCE THAT IT IS REACHABLE, sufficient to refuse a gate whose predicate is an absorbing state -- for a rate-based wait that means the arrival and service rates of the awaited resource, measured rather than assumed. Until such evidence is required, this is review diligence at the point the wait is authored, which is mitigatable only, and the review question is the rate comparison above.)
