- INVALID STATE: a dispatcher's residual arm renders input it could not classify as text that is INERT IN THE TARGET LANGUAGE -- a comment, a docstring, a blank -- rather than refusing. The emitted artifact is well-formed and silently INCOMPLETE: the declaration the input carried is simply not there. This is DESIGN section 5's fabricated-plausible-output with the failure arm widening instead of stopping, and it is worse in an emitter than elsewhere, because the consumer of the omission is a downstream COMPILER that has no way to know a declaration was expected.RECEIPT, 2026-09-04, in v1.compiler.emit_python emit_py_typed_item. Its else arm rendered `concat("# unhandled node: ", item_text)`. An item claimed by none of the shape predicates was dropped from the emitted module with no diagnostic, no exit code and no counter. The SAME residual position in the two sibling emitters did stop: v1.compiler.emit_rust rendered compile_error! (a stop at the target compiler) and v1.compiler.emit_go rendered a panicking init() (a stop at target runtime). One dispatcher out of three fails open, and the three sit in files that are read as a family.HOW IT STAYED INVISIBLE, which is the distinguishing fact rather than an aside. It was found by a REFUSAL CENSUS taken for a different purpose -- enumerating what each emit-side item shape predicate refuses, before deleting the family in favour of a carried item kind. Nothing was watching this arm: it produced valid Python, so no target compiler complained; it produced no diagnostic, so no gate counted it; and the population that reaches it was empty in the accepted corpus, so no output differed. A defect whose only symptom is a MISSING declaration in a target nobody compiles in CI has no observable, and its recorded frequency without this row is ZERO.WHY IT IS FILED AT THE MOMENT IT DISAPPEARS. The repair that surfaced it also removes it: the three dispatchers become total matches over v1.std.core ParsedModuleItemKind, so the residual position no longer exists for the five real item kinds, and the two remaining arms -- ModuleItemUnrecognized and NotAModuleItem -- refuse in all three targets. A silently repaired defect that is never rostered cannot rank for a wall, and the next emitter added to the family would reproduce it with nothing to consult.RECOGNITION RULE: for every dispatcher that RENDERS INTO A TARGET LANGUAGE, read its residual arm and ask what the target does with the bytes. If the answer is 'nothing' -- a comment, whitespace, an empty string -- the arm is not a refusal however it is worded, and the emitted artifact is a fabricated plausible output. Compare siblings: a family of emitters that stop differently in the same position is the tell, because the divergence is invisible from inside any one of them. The generalisation beyond emitters: an arm whose output is VALID IN ITS DESTINATION but carries none of the input's meaning fails open while looking like it handled the case.CEILING AND NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER. Ceiling STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE, DESIGN section 4b rung 4, and the mechanism is total dispatch: when the dispatched-on subject is a closed variant, the residual arm HAS NO CONSTRUCTOR and the inert-text arm cannot be written. Where a residual must remain -- input that is genuinely outside the modeled variant set -- the class drops to MECHANICALLY PREVENTABLE and the trigger is a refusal helper per target that the residual arm is obliged to call, so the stopping behaviour is one authority rather than a per-dispatcher spelling. As of this row the three .dag emitters are at rung 4 for item dispatch and no other emitter dispatcher has been censused; that uncensused population is the class's open coverage obligation, not a claim of absence.
- INVALID STATE: a carrier gains a positive KIND variant while the fields that kind implies stay OPTIONAL on the same carrier, so a consumer that matches the kind still cannot reach the payload without an unwrap. The kind answers WHICH construct this is and answers nothing about what the construct contains, yet the climb reads as if it had closed both -- which is the shape of DESIGN section 4b(1) rung inflation, because the honest rung is the MINIMUM across the paths and the field path did not move.RECEIPT, 2026-09-04, in v1.std.core Node. Node gained module_item_kind carrying ParsedModuleItemKind, stamped by the parse item constructors, which dissolved the emit-side predicates that decided an item's kind from the ABSENCE of fields. Node.body and Node.type_annotation stayed `Node?`, because Node is one carrier for items and expressions alike and an expression has no body. So a ModuleItemFunction whose body is Absent, or a ModuleItemDataValue with no declared type, remains fully AUTHORABLE, and twelve dispatch sites across the three emitters that previously used is_function_item / is_data_def_item as PROOF THAT AN UNWRAP WAS SAFE now match on the optional field and refuse when it is Absent. The refusal is real and it is a wall, not a construction.THE DISTINGUISHING FACT is that the surviving guard reads as leftover ceremony rather than as load-bearing. A reviewer who accepts the kind as authoritative sees `match item.body { Present ... Absent => refuse }` under `ModuleItemFunction =>` and reads the Absent arm as unreachable -- and deleting it, or replacing it with an unwrap, is a one-line edit that no test names. The tell is a coproduct arm whose body immediately reaches for a field the coproduct does not guarantee.CEILING AND NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER. Ceiling STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE, DESIGN section 4b rung 4; current rung MECHANICALLY PREVENTABLE for the field-presence half, while the kind half sits at rung 4. THE TRIGGER IS THE CAPABILITY AND NOT AN ARTIFACT: a per-kind item carrier in which each kind's implied payload is NON-OPTIONAL BY CONSTRUCTION -- a function item whose body is a `Node` and not a `Node?`, a data item whose declared type and value are both required -- so that a kind-stamped item with a missing payload has no constructor and the Absent arms have no RED to catch. Anything less than that, including a check that every stamped item carries its fields, is a validator standing where construction was available and does not retire this row.RECOGNITION RULE: when a positive variant lands on a shared carrier, ask which fields that variant IMPLIES and whether they are still optional on the carrier. If they are, the climb covered the classification axis only; report the rung for that axis and name the payload axis separately. A single carrier serving several variants is the structural reason the payload cannot be tightened in place, so this row and the carrier split are the same work seen from two ends.
- **a population selector that cannot admit a counterexample** (the population under study is enumerated by a LEXICAL PROXY FOR THE PREDICATE being asked, so the selector and the evidence are one object. Every member is then found to satisfy the claim -- not because the claim holds, but because no member that would refute it was reachable by the query that defined membership. The output is a CATEGORICAL VERDICT that is UNFALSIFIABLE BY CONSTRUCTION: no member of that population could have come back the other way. **THE ONE-QUESTION REVIEW TEST, and it is the reason to key the row on this rather than on the cause: WHAT MEMBER OF THIS POPULATION COULD HAVE FALSIFIED THE CLAIM?** If the answer is none, the query was structurally incapable of returning the negative and the verdict carries no information. The question costs one sentence and is askable before any result is read, which is when it is worth asking. **THE BOUNDARY AGAINST `selection_view_read_as_population`, the nearest row, and it fails to fire here.** There membership is a property of THE MEASUREMENT -- the set moves when the instrument moves, when load moves, when the reporter truncates, while the subject is unchanged. Here membership is a STABLE PROPERTY OF THE SUBJECT: the files matching a token do not move with load or with the instrument. Every recognition rule that row offers is silent on this one, which is why it needs its own. **THE BOUNDARY AGAINST `censored_estimator_drops_its_own_tail`, which is the other near miss.** Censoring drops an EXTREME and biases an ESTIMATE low with no bound, so the excluded region is a tail. Here the excluded region is THE ENTIRE COMPLEMENT, and its members are not extreme -- they are the ONLY POSSIBLE COUNTEREXAMPLES. The output is not a biased number but a categorical capability verdict, so there is no estimator to debias and no bound to state; the repair is to re-derive the population, not to recover a tail. **THE BOUNDARY AGAINST `corroboration_without_an_independent_derivation_axis`, filed the same night from the same specimen, and they are two classes rather than one.** That row is about WHEN A SECOND READING IS EVIDENCE AT ALL: two readers agree, the agreement is counted as confirmation, and both ran the same query over the same lexical projection -- two signatures, one evidence lineage. It applies with a perfectly sound population; two people can share a lineage reading a log, a dashboard or a diff. THE DISCRIMINATOR IS WHERE THE DEFECT LIVES, not decidability -- AN EARLIER DRAFT OF THIS PARAGRAPH SAID DECIDABILITY AND CLAIMED THIS CLASS WAS DECIDABLE WITH CEILING 3, WHICH THE CEILING PARAGRAPH BELOW WITHDRAWS ON EVIDENCE; the two statements stood in one file at once, which is the single-authority defect this roster exists to catch. Both classes are undecidable in general: whether a selector can admit a counterexample is a question about the semantic relation between two expressions, and whether two readings share an evidence lineage is a question about how two people arrived at them. What separates them is the SUBJECT -- this class is a defect in HOW A POPULATION WAS CONSTRUCTED, which leaves a trace in a declared enumeration and is therefore partly reachable by construction over a declared sub-population; that one is a property of the RELATIONSHIP BETWEEN TWO READERS, which no artifact records at all, so it carries ceiling 1 and declares no trigger. **A repair that closes either leaves the other untouched:** deriving the population independently does not make a second reader's agreement informative, and changing the second reader's axis does not un-derive the population. They co-occurred here because one specimen exhibited both. **AND AGAINST THE SELF-REFERENCE FAMILY** (`admission_predicate_evidenced_from_inside_its_own_subject`, `self_authorized_dissolution`, `predicate_vacuously_true_on_an_empty_domain`, `repair_enumerates_its_own_blast_radius_by_inspection`): each is about a SUBJECT supplying its own admission. This is about a POPULATION supplying its own verdict, which is a different object -- the subject here is perfectly ordinary and it is the enumeration that is circular. **SPECIMEN WITH A RECEIPT, measured 2026-09-04 across gunbc#10388's scaffold escalation, and it is filed as a PAIR because the second half is what makes it a class rather than an incident.** The question was whether bounded pagination is modelled in this repository. The population used was the files matching `default_per_page`, seven of them. That token is a LEXICAL PROXY for the predicate: it names a page-SIZE default, and every route that pages WITHOUT importing it -- a reflective host bridge, an endpoint carrying its own page parameter -- was unreachable by the query that defined the population. So the verdict `bounded pagination is established` could not have come back any other way. The truth, established later by reading operation graphs rather than tokens: `extdeps.github` DOES declare a page-SIZE parameter and DOES bind it into the request query -- `pulls.dag` carries `per_page: Int = default_per_page` in an operation input and `query: { state: state, per_page: per_page }` on the wire, across seven files -- and it contains NO PAGE FOLD ANYWHERE: no cursor advance, no link-header read, no continuation loop, no terminal closure. `type Pagination` is declared with ZERO consumers. **THE PARAMETER'S PRESENCE IS WHAT MADE THE PROXY CREDIBLE, and that is a stronger statement than the capability being absent.** The population was not selected by a token that meant nothing; it was selected by a token that meant something ADJACENT AND REAL, which is the only reason two readers believed it. A proxy nobody would adopt is not a hazard -- the dangerous proxy is the one that is genuinely about the neighbourhood of the predicate. **THE SECOND HALF: TWO ANALYSTS REPRODUCED ONE EVIDENCE LINEAGE AND READ IT AS CORROBORATION.** The second reader deliberately refused to relay the first's claim and re-measured independently -- and re-measured THE SAME SUBJECT WITH THE SAME TOKEN THROUGH THE SAME LEXICAL PROJECTION, making the same unstated substitution of `page size is configurable` for `continuation is consumed until closure`. One lineage, two signatures, and the agreement was read as confirmation. It then reached an operator escalation as a seconded premise, which is worse than one analyst being wrong: a second confirms a first without adding information. **A SECOND READING CORROBORATES ONLY WHEN IT CHANGES A LOAD-BEARING DERIVATION AXIS** -- operation-graph reachability, an executing transition, a controlled mutation. Any one of the three was cheap here and would have returned the truth on the first pass. **THREE CLAIMS WERE TREATED AS ONE, which is the specific substitution to watch for at an external boundary: page SIZE configured, cursor SHAPE declared, and continuation CLOSURE executed are independent.** A declared carrier proves the second and neither of the others; a configured size parameter proves the first and neither of the others. Reading any one as the conjunction is how the proxy gets adopted in the first place. **THE SEAL, and it is why per-member discipline does not repair this: `one disposition per member` is satisfiable with the predicate never executed.** A caller can map members to fabricated satisfied rows and obtain perfect cardinality and perfect key coverage over a population that was itself derived from the proxy. So the fix is not a count and not a coverage join: PROVENANCE MUST LIVE IN THE CONSTRUCTOR -- no path from a member, a file path, a symbol occurrence or a count to a disposition, and only the executor mints one. That is a structural statement rather than a discipline, which is the form that survives a reviewer who agrees with it and then does not do it. **RUNG: NONE ESTABLISHED BY EXECUTED EVIDENCE, and the earlier draft of this paragraph claimed 1 (mitigatable) on the strength of the class being nameable and the review question being one sentence.** That was rung inflation and it is withdrawn. DESIGN section 4b defines rung 1 as harm CONTAINED -- by total operations, typed outcomes, bounds, rollback, isolation -- and names a plan, a diagnostic variant and an inert lens as establishing nothing. A recognition rule is none of those. Nothing in this repository refuses, bounds or isolates a population selected by a proxy for its own predicate; what actually happened in every receipt above is that a PEER ASKED, which is attention rather than mechanism, and 4b(1) explicitly does not count it. The receipts are executed evidence that the class OCCURS and that review sometimes catches it -- they are not evidence of containment, and reading them as a rung is the same substitution this row is about. **THE HONEST POSITION IS THAT THE CLASS IS CURRENTLY UNCONTAINED**, which is worse than sitting at rung 1 and is the reason it ranks for a climb at all. **CEILING, AND THE PREVIOUSLY DECLARED ONE WAS WRONG IN A WAY THE SPECIMEN ON THIS ROW REFUTES.** The earlier draft claimed ceiling 3, decidable because whether a population's enumerating expression SHARES A SYMBOL with the predicate evaluated over it is a static question over the same `Node` graph a lens reads. Run that procedure against the receipt directly above it: the selector keyed on `default_per_page` while the claim was about CONTINUATION AND FOLD EXECUTION. Those expressions share no symbol and need not, so the proposed decider does not fire on the specimen that motivated the row -- a check permanently green on its own founding case, which is the decoration 4b warns is worse than absent because it will be cited as coverage. **THE GENERAL PROPERTY IS NOT DECIDABLE**: whether a selector can admit a counterexample to a claim is a question about the SEMANTIC relationship between two expressions, not their syntax, so by section 5's triage this class is a ratchet, not a wall, and no amount of static reading makes it one. **CEILING: 1 (mitigatable). THE EARLIER DRAFT'S 2 IS WITHDRAWN ON REVIEW 60734, WHICH IS RIGHT, AND IS RIGHT FOR A REASON THIS ROW ALREADY CONTAINED.** Rung 2 requires a mechanism that RELIABLY EXPOSES AND BLOCKS the class. The constructor discipline this row used to name as its trigger does neither: an executor can evaluate every member of a proxy-selected population perfectly honestly while every counterexample stays outside the enumeration, so the verdict is unfalsifiable exactly as it was and nothing has been exposed. That capability is real, but it guarantees a DIFFERENT property -- that no disposition exists which nothing executed -- and pricing it as this class's ceiling was the substitution this row is named after, committed by this row. What contains THIS harm is narrower: a verdict must not be readable as broader than the enumeration it was taken over. That is containment rather than prevention, which is rung 1. **NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, AND IT NAMES THE CAPABILITY THAT REACHES THE CEILING, not a stronger one borrowed from another class.** A claim carrier that binds a verdict to the enumeration it was evaluated over, and refuses to render or aggregate that verdict unless the subject enumeration travels with it. That contains the harm this class actually does: a verdict taken over a proxy-selected population stops being readable as a claim about the population the reader has in mind, because the carrier will not state it unqualified. **WHY THE CEILING STOPS THERE, stated so no later author reads this row as promising a wall:** whether a selector CAN admit a counterexample to a claim is a question about the semantic relationship between two expressions, not their syntax. DESIGN section 5 triages that as a ratchet forever, and the never-trap is exactly the move of letting a ratchet wear a wall-shaped ceiling. **AND WHAT IS DELIBERATELY NOT CLAIMED HERE:** the constructor discipline described in the seal above -- provenance in the constructor, dispositions mintable only by an executor -- remains a worthwhile capability, and it is NOT this row's trigger. It closes FABRICATED DISPOSITIONS, a different invalid state with a different subject. Citing this row as coverage for that class, or that capability as a climb of this one, is the same conflation running in the other direction. 
- **a dissolution trigger cites a MECHANISM by name, that mechanism is later deleted, and nothing points back -- so the scaffold it governs becomes permanent while still reading as tracked** (INVALID STATE: a declared scaffold or rung drop names its exit as a mechanism -- a lens, a gate, a cadence, a flag -- and the citation is CORRECT WHEN WRITTEN. A later lane deletes that mechanism for its own good reasons, and the deletion is complete and reviewed at ITS end. The trigger is not a consumer of the deleted symbol -- it is prose naming it -- so no compile refuses, no census counts it, and the scaffold now carries an exit condition that can never occur. HARM: the row keeps presenting as temporary debt with a named exit, which is exactly the presentation that removes it from every audit that looks for stalls; DESIGN section 4b(2)'s separation of `cannot climb further` from `can climb after one grounding` silently reclassifies the row into the first category without anyone deciding that. DISTINGUISHING FACTS, and they are what make this its own class rather than an instance of the two neighbours: (1) THE TENSE AND THE DIRECTION OF INVALIDATION. `restoration_promise_names_a_route_that_does_not_exist` is a promise whose named route NEVER EXISTED, discoverable by grepping the tree at the moment the promise is read; `trigger_satisfied_before_the_row_was_written` is a trigger already TRUE at authoring. Here the trigger was both real and unsatisfied when written, and a THIRD PARTY's edit made it unreachable afterwards, so neither of those rows' origin arms applies and neither of their remedies finds it. (2) THE DELETING LANE IS BLAMELESS AND CANNOT SEE IT. A mechanism's deletion PR reviews the mechanism's consumers -- imports, calls, roster membership -- and a trigger citing it is none of those, so the class is invisible from the only side that knows the deletion is happening. This is DESIGN section 3's positional-citation defect moved from `file:line` to `mechanism-name`: a citation that decays because the thing it names is edited elsewhere. SPECIMEN, MEASURED 2026-09-05 AT HEAD 4da9c1a3fdd AND UNREPAIRED AT FILING: `v2.compiler.self_host.candidate_generation` carries `SCAFFOLD: feature:medium_structure_containment` over `direct_rust_door_cargo_toml`, the hand-authored Cargo manifest literal the direct Rust door writes. The cited lens WAS DELETED IN #6831, stated by two independent surviving carriers -- `v2.std.compilers.target_model` `target_text_carrier_scaffold_note` (`The retired no-smuggled-programs / medium_structure_containment lens (#6831) is not live enforcement`) and `gunbc.plans.self_applying_lenses` (`medium_structure_containment / no-smuggled-programs was deleted in #6831`). The name occurs three times in the corpus and NOT ONCE as a live declaration: the annotation that cites it, and the two carriers recording its death. THE SECOND HALF OF THE SPECIMEN IS WHAT MAKES THE REPAIR NON-COSMETIC: the same annotation's `dissolve-on` SENTENCE names a different and real capability -- emit the manifest through the target-model / serialize_target path or a cited Toml medium carrier -- and THAT capability does not exist either, measured: there is no TOML authority anywhere under `src/v2/extdeps/formats` (json, sql, spice, icestorm_pcf) or `extdeps/languages`. So a reader who repairs only the dead NAME still holds a trigger nobody can satisfy; the honest repair states the capability and prices it as absent. RUNG FOUND AT: 1, mitigatable. Nothing silent is produced -- the scaffold is declared and its content is honest about being a literal -- and the only thing containing the harm is that a reader may notice the cited mechanism is gone. CEILING: 3, structurally guaranteed. A trigger that cites a repository symbol is a CITATION, and DESIGN section 3's standing rule already requires citations to name symbols the namespace authority can resolve; a trigger citing a symbol that no longer resolves is decidable by the same `Node`-tree read that decides any stale name, so an `Accepted` program need not contain one. NOT 4: the trigger is prose and a trigger naming a capability in words rather than a symbol is legitimate and must stay writable, so the invalid state remains authorable and the guarantee is a refusal rather than an absent constructor. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, at capability grain: dissolution and restoration triggers carrying their cited mechanism as a RESOLVED DECLARATION REFERENCE rather than as free text, so deleting the mechanism reddens every trigger that names it in the deleting lane's own build -- sufficient because the failure is precisely that the deleting lane cannot see the citation, and a convention that authors should check would be satisfied while the capability stayed dead. WHAT IS HARDER TO DO NOW THAN BEFORE THIS ROW WAS FILED: NOTHING, and this row is reported UNREMEDIED on that basis, per `a_written_row_is_not_a_firing_mechanism`. The one friction delivered with it is local and expires: the specimen's own trigger text is rewritten in the same change to name the absent capability instead of the deleted lens.)
- **stale claim survives its own correct edit** (INVALID STATE: a prose claim that was TRUE when written and became false through a CORRECT edit made elsewhere -- the deletion of a CI job, the removal of a symbol, a rename, a withdrawn change, or the closing of a referenced node -- while nothing in the citing artifact changed at the moment it became false. HARM: THE CHANGE CORRECTLY MODIFIED ONE ARTIFACT AND THE EXISTING CHECKS DID NOT VALIDATE THE DEPENDENT ASSERTION AGAINST THE CHANGED PREMISE. That is the accurate statement and it is weaker than the one this row carried first, which said the defect is invisible to every mechanism we have and that no test, lint, type check or review of either diff can detect it. THAT CLAIM IS FALSE AND THIS PR DISPROVES IT: a review that joined the retained assertion to the changed premise at the candidate tree detected it, twice, and detected instances the author's own phrase-keyed searches had missed. The stale assertion sits outside the changed lines, but THE DELETION THAT INVALIDATED ITS PREMISE IS IN THE DIFF, so the join is available to any reader who makes it. What is true is that nothing MECHANICAL makes that join today and no check is obligated to. The impossibility claim also weakened the row: a class asserted to be undetectable never ranks for a detector, which is the same move DESIGN 4b(1) names when an inflated rung stops a class from ranking for a climb. A fluent, well-reasoned wrong claim survives on readability -- one specimen below passed four review rounds. DISTINGUISHING FACTS, and the reason this is one class rather than four: the mechanism is identical -- a claim whose PREMISE is elsewhere, with no link from the premise back to the claim -- and only the severity differs. FOUR TIERS, each with a measured specimen. (i) MISDESCRIBES CANDIDATE EVIDENCE: `v1.compiler.compiler_tests_rust` `ct_evaluation_budget_consequence_falsifier_test` justified an opt-in arm by saying the --lib suite runs on every push; it wastes a reader's time. (ii) CONFERS STANDING IT DOES NOT HAVE: the same file's block above `emit_entry_point_absent_main_rs` announced itself as a WALL 'ENROLLED WHERE IT ACTUALLY EXECUTES', riding a required `cargo test --release -p v1-compiler --lib` step -- and `.github/workflows/witnesses.yml` contains ZERO occurrences of `cargo test`, the job having been deleted by the 2026-09-04 runner-capacity ruling under declared drop `rust_unit_tests_off_the_merge_path`. That is DESIGN 4b(1) RUNG INFLATION, and it is worse than (i) because an inflated wall means the class never ranks for climbing: everyone believes it is already held. (iii) THE REASONING ITSELF INVERTS WHILE THE WORDS STAY CORRECT-LOOKING: that same block rejected a wet transport because it 'would have been an inert lens asserting a safety property nothing runs', and the home it chose instead became exactly that. Not one word had to change for the argument's conclusion to reverse. (iv) AN OUTSTANDING OBLIGATION LOSES ITS ACCOUNTABLE OWNER, when that owner closes with neither discharge nor valid transfer; THE CLOSED OWNER MAY REMAIN A PERFECTLY VALID HISTORICAL REFERENCE. AN EARLIER REVISION DEFINED THIS TIER AS A CITATION NAMING SOMETHING CLOSABLE, WHICH CONDEMNED EVERY CITATION OF A CLOSED THING; FIXING THE TELL DID NOT REPLACE THAT DEFINITION, WHICH IS THIS ROW'S OWN CLASS. `test.claim.phantom_marker_type_argument_identity_witness` named `node://adhoc-da8c65c0-b3b` as the owner of a next-rung trigger; that node reached DONE without the capability it owed existing, so the trigger was retirable by nobody. The repair repointed it to `node://adhoc-f044ad97-222` -- THE AUTHORING LANE'S OWN WORK ITEM, which would have closed when that lane finished WITH THE OBLIGATION NEITHER DISCHARGED NOR TRANSFERRED. The defect is that ending, not the closing itself. The same defect with a longer fuse, authored by the change that diagnosed the class, ratified through three review rounds, and caught only by a dashboard completion notice. AND ONE SPECIMEN IS SHARPER THAN ALL FOUR TIERS: A CURRENT-TREE-ONLY RETROSPECTIVE TRUTH CHECK CANNOT RECOVER AN EARLIER FALSE INTERVAL AFTER THE CLAIM HAS BECOME TRUE AGAIN -- a claim made false by one change and made true again by an unrelated later one. The clause 'rust-unit-tests is not a `needs` of the required aggregate' stood in `gunbc.emitted_closure_compile_seed_growth`. It was TRUE when written, made FALSE by gunbc#10078 promoting that job into the required aggregate, and made TRUE AGAIN by the 2026-09-04 runner-capacity ruling deleting the job altogether. Read it today and it is green. Read it during the #10078 window and it is red. Read it today AGAIN and note WHY it is green: an unrelated deletion handed the truth back, and the sentence never earned it. SO A RETROSPECTIVE SWEEP -- THE ONE-SHOT 'is this sentence true now' AN AUTHOR RUNS OVER THE TREE IN FRONT OF THEM -- RETURNS GREEN TODAY AND CANNOT RECOVER THE PERIOD THE CLAIM WAS FALSE. AN EARLIER REVISION OF THIS PARAGRAPH SAID SUCH A CHECK WOULD HAVE LICENSED THE CLAIM THROUGH THAT PERIOD, AND THAT IS WRONG: a point-in-time truth check ENROLLED ON EVERY CHANGE would have evaluated the clause at gunbc#10078 and REFUSED, because the clause was false at that moment. The distinction the wrong sentence erased is between RETROSPECTIVE DETECTION, which can only ever see the tree it is run on, and CONTINUOUS ENFORCEMENT, which sees every transition as it happens. Continuous enrollment is sufficient for THIS specimen, and saying otherwise would steer a future author away from a remedy that works. That is the strongest argument in this row for validating an assertion AGAINST THE CHANGED PREMISE rather than checking it in isolation, and it is an observed specimen rather than a constructed one. WHAT IT DOES AND DOES NOT BOUND, stated carefully because the first version of this row overreached. It bounds the RETROSPECTIVE form: a sweep of the current tree cannot distinguish TRUE AND PRESERVED from TRUE AGAIN BY COINCIDENCE, because both read green, and the difference lives only in the history of the premise. It does NOT bound a per-change check, which sees the falsifying transition at the moment it occurs and needs no history at all. The practical consequence for a repair is that a currently-green sentence is not evidence of a healthy sentence -- coincidental re-validation is indistinguishable from durability at the moment of reading, and the difference only appears in the history of the premise. THE TELL THAT DISTINGUISHES (iv) FROM A HEALTHY CITATION IS ONE QUESTION, and it is decidable: AFTER THE LIFECYCLE TRANSITION, DOES EVERY STILL-UNDISCHARGED OBLIGATION RETAIN AN ACCOUNTABLE OWNER OR A VALID TRANSFER? AN EARLIER REVISION ASKED WHETHER THE CITATION WOULD STILL HAVE A REFERENT, AND THAT WAS OVERBROAD: a CLOSED work item is a perfectly valid HISTORICAL reference, and nothing is wrong with citing one for what it did. The failure is not that a referent ended; it is that an OUTSTANDING OBLIGATION lost accountable responsibility with neither discharge nor transfer. Historical reference and active responsibility are different uses of one citation, and only the second is at risk when a lifecycle ends. A WORK ITEM'S CURRENT `in_progress` STATUS DOES NOT ESTABLISH THIS PROPERTY, AND ITS CLOSURE IS SAFE FOR THE OBLIGATION ONLY WHEN THE OBLIGATION IS DISCHARGED OR RESPONSIBILITY VALIDLY TRANSFERRED. AN EARLIER REVISION SAID A WORK ITEM FAILS BY CONSTRUCTION, WHICH NO LONGER FOLLOWS FROM THE QUESTION ABOVE: a work item can discharge before it closes, or transfer to a successor that accepts responsibility, and either is a clean ending. Checking that a candidate node is currently `in_progress` answers the STATUS question and never the RESPONSIBILITY-SURVIVAL question -- which is precisely the check on which the defective repoint was ratified. An earlier revision called the second one the LIFETIME question, which was the withdrawn framing: what must survive is responsibility, not the owner. RUNG FOUND AT: 1, mitigatable. The population is discovered by sweeping prose for the dead premise and reading every hit in context, which is manual and has no denominator. CEILING, STATED PER SUB-CASE BECAUSE THE SUB-CASES DO NOT SHARE ONE. THE FREE-PROSE FORM CARRIES NO RUNG AT ALL AND IS NOT A CEILING OF 2: whether an arbitrary sentence is still true is not a property the source decides, so no mechanism EXISTS to reliably expose and block the invalid state, and DESIGN 4b's rung 2 requires exactly such a mechanism. The absence of an evaluator is what DISQUALIFIES a class from carrying a rung; it is not a low rung. That puts free prose in 4b's ADJACENT COLUMN -- OUTSIDE THE MODELED GUARANTEE -- which the ladder deliberately excludes so that 'we do not model this' cannot masquerade as a weak implementation that should climb. TWO EARLIER REVISIONS OF THIS ROW GOT THIS WRONG IN TWO DIFFERENT WAYS, AND THE ROW CONTRADICTED ITSELF BETWEEN THEM: the header claimed a ceiling of 2 for the free-prose form while the body said it stays at mitigation, which is rung 1 -- two rungs for one sub-case in one row, neither of them derived. THE STRUCTURED SUB-CASES DO CARRY CEILINGS, and they are the whole of this row's ladder claim. THE ABSENCE OF A GENERAL FREE-PROSE EVALUATOR DOES NOT DERIVE A CEILING FOR THE STRUCTURED SUB-CASES, AND AN EARLIER REVISION DERIVED ONE FROM IT: a declaration-existence check establishes EXISTENCE, not that a sentence stayed true, and not that the named declaration OWNS the cited obligation. Those are three different properties and the check decides only the first. A DECLARATION-EXISTENCE CHECK ESTABLISHES EXISTENCE ONLY; IT ESTABLISHES NEITHER THE TRUTH OF A DEPENDENT ASSERTION NOR CONTINUED RESPONSIBILITY, AND ANY GUARANTEE FOR EITHER STRUCTURED RELATION MUST FOLLOW FROM THAT RELATION'S OWN MODEL AND ENFORCEMENT BOUNDARY -- not from the limits of free-prose evaluation. AN EARLIER REVISION INSTEAD ASSERTED THAT THE THIRD PROPERTY, OWNERSHIP, IS UNDECIDABLE, AND THAT WAS NOT ESTABLISHED: ownership is carried in a `String` here, and a current representation is a fact about this model rather than a proof that ownership cannot be modeled. What IS decidable is the two structured sub-cases, and the ceiling is stated per sub-case rather than for the class: a citation naming a DECLARED SYMBOL is already checked by the parse-phase `v1_compiler.declaration_index` route in `v1_compiler.bin.claim_executor`, and a citation naming a LIFECYCLE-BOUNDED REFERENT is decidable the same way if such referents are modeled rather than spelled into a `String`. Free prose asserting a fact about CI shape is reachable by neither, which is why it sits outside the modeled guarantee above rather than at any rung, and the honest disposition for it is a declared boundary obligation -- observed, refused or mitigated at that boundary -- and never a number on the ladder. THE COINCIDENTAL-RE-VALIDATION SPECIMEN DOES NOT LOWER THIS CEILING, AND AN EARLIER REVISION USED IT TO DO SO. It bounds a RETROSPECTIVE sweep only; a check enrolled on every change would have refused at the falsifying transition. What actually distinguishes the sub-cases is the EVALUATION limit already stated above -- whether a free-prose sentence is true is not a property the source decides -- and not the timing of the reading. That limit is why the free-prose form carries NO RUNG rather than a low one, as stated at the top of this ceiling; it is not a derivation of 2 for anything. The two are independent, and conflating them makes continuous enforcement look futile when it is the effective remedy for every sub-case whose assertion can be evaluated at all. The next trigger names a premise-join because a join is DECIDABLE where a general truth test is not, which is a decidability argument and not a timing one. NEXT TRIGGER, naming the CAPABILITY and not an artifact, and its SUBJECT IS RESPONSIBILITY SURVIVAL rather than declaration well-formedness: RESPONSIBILITY FOR AN OUTSTANDING OBLIGATION CANNOT BE RETIRED THROUGH A MODELED CLOSURE TRANSITION UNLESS DISCHARGE EVIDENCE FOR THAT EXACT OBLIGATION IS CONSUMED, OR AN ACCEPTED TRANSFER TO A VALID SUCCESSOR IS ESTABLISHED; OTHERWISE RESPONSIBILITY IS RETAINED AND THE CLOSURE IS REFUSED. This PREDICTS nothing -- it does not ask whether a discharge condition will ever become true, which is impossible -- it CONSTRAINS what an accepted closure operation may do, which is the ordinary fail-closed move. ITS BOUNDARY, stated because the guarantee is only as wide as the transition it constrains: an external action that bypasses the modeled transition does NOT acquire this guarantee merely because a source declaration exists. TWO WEAKER TRIGGERS ARE RECORDED AS REFUSED SO THEY ARE NOT RE-PROPOSED. (1) BANNING work-item and session identities outright is decidable but forbids what this row explicitly PERMITS, and a trigger that refuses a legitimate program is a different requirement wearing this one's name. (2) REQUIRING ONLY THAT A CLOSABLE OWNER DECLARE A CONTINUATION -- a successor or a discharge condition -- at authoring time. That was proposed here and is WITHDRAWN, because A CONDITION'S PRESENCE IS NOT ITS FULFILMENT: attach discharge condition D to obligation O owned by A, never satisfy D, close A, and let no successor ever accept O -- every field the rule demands is present and responsibility is still lost, which is this tier exactly. Naming B is likewise not B ACCEPTING O. So the authoring-time rule removes the MISSING-DECLARATION state and nothing more; 'claim and continuation are one object' is a true derivation about DECLARATION WELL-FORMEDNESS and a false one about RESPONSIBILITY SURVIVAL, and attaching it to the second was rung inflation of the same shape this row was twice corrected for. Declaration well-formedness remains a real and cheaper subject, and a rung for it would be honest -- it is simply NOT THIS TIER'S SUBJECT, and claiming it here would make the trigger narrower than the failure it serves. Until that exists the honest arm is the one taken here: name a STANDING AUTHORITY that owns the capability rather than any node. A grep, a sweep, or a one-off repair of the known instances does NOT fire this trigger; they are mitigation of a population, not prevention of the state. RECOGNITION RULE: when prose asserts a fact whose subject lives in ANOTHER file -- a CI job, a symbol, a step, a node, a rung -- ask what would have to change elsewhere for the sentence to become false, and whether anything would edit this file when it did. If the answer is 'nothing', the sentence is a candidate instance whether or not it is currently true. The corollary for authors: after any edit that DELETES or WITHDRAWS something, look for the ANNOUNCEMENT of it, because the announcement is in a diff you are not looking at. A GREP IS A CHEAP FIRST PASS AND NEVER CLOSES THAT SEARCH -- it can find a site and cannot establish that none remains, which the MEASUREMENT HAZARD below records six times over. WHAT CLOSES IT IS READING THE ENTAILMENT NEIGHBOURHOOD OF THE CHANGED PREMISE: the framing sentence and heading ABOVE the replaced text, the dependents BELOW it, and the sibling surfaces that rest on the same premise. Those dependents are unreachable by any pattern derived from the replaced text, because they do not QUOTE the premise, they REST on it -- 'a trigger whose owner has ceased to exist is retired by nothing at all' contained none of this repair's search terms and went false the moment the rule above it changed. AN EARLIER REVISION OF THIS COROLLARY SAID SIMPLY 'GREP FOR THE ANNOUNCEMENT', directly above the paragraph recording that grepping is what failed -- this row's own class, inside the row about it. MEASUREMENT HAZARD, recorded because it cost six detector failures across two sessions on 2026-09-05: every sweep for this class returned a CONFIDENT CLEAN ANSWER for the rows it could not see. `grep -c` counts lines and misses phrases wrapping across emitted string literals (2 where the truth was 4); a normaliser stripping `///` but not `//` leaves a comment marker mid-phrase; an exact sentence misses a shorter variant; backticks around an identifier defeat a word-boundary match; and enumerating by premise returns files that correctly RECORD the deletion alongside files that assert it. A record QUOTING a false claim inside its own errata is not asserting it, and no regex separates quotation from assertion. Strip both comment forms and backticks, collapse whitespace, always run a control that MUST hit, and read every hit in context. SCOPE NOT CLAIMED: the four tiers were measured on one lane's sweep of one premise. No claim is made that the tiers are exhaustive, nor that the population outside `v1.compiler.compiler_tests_rust`, `gunbc.emitted_closure_compile_seed_growth` and `test.claim.phantom_marker_type_argument_identity_witness` has been enumerated.) 
- **a claim edit changes execution or adjudication policy outside the reported cost delta** (INVALID STATE: a cost-change report accounts for the claims the author intended to optimize while omitting execution-membership or policy transitions induced by other changed test declarations. HARM: correct per-claim savings and a green semantic gate support a net-saving conclusion that the complete run contradicts. The gate and the report answer different questions; approval of the former does not establish the latter. DISTINGUISHING MECHANISM: `floor_diff_edits_from_line_ranges` attributes changed lines to test declarations. `v2.workflow.required_floor` `required_floor_disposition_with_changed_selection` replaces the ordinary disposition with `PlannedAsChangedWitness`. For an otherwise withheld `DeclinedCostDebt` identity, this starts execution. `changed_witness_cost_policy` returns `ChangedCostDebtVerdictOnly` only at the intersection of changed-witness selection AND `floor_cost_debt_holds`; `changed_witness_cpu_deadline` then selects `CpuDeadlineObservedOnly`. An ordinary changed witness retains its enforced CPU deadline. Wall deadlines, semantic failures and missing cost observations still block. This is the declared changed-cost-debt policy, not a defect in admission and not a waiver for every edited claim. SPECIMEN gunbc#10538: an incidental parameterization rewrote `v2.test.claim.dag_acceptance` `acceptance_translate_row_is_determinate`, activating its existing cost-debt identity. Reconcile main run 33954515928 against PR run 33955309905 using artifact `required-floor-claim-cost`: the intended claims became cheaper, but the added execution outweighed those savings and the complete corpus became more expensive. That claim passed above the configured CPU line under the declared verdict-only policy. Approvals preceded the corpus reconciliation. The repair restored the production helper and this test body verbatim while giving the intended identity-only claims a separate fixture; it did not weaken the production subject or change its roster. THE POLICY WAS ANNOUNCED, NOT SILENT: the log of run 33955309905 reports the override population in `withhold_overridden_for_changed_verdict` beside `enrolled`, names the overridden identity, emits its `floor-cost-debt-observation`, and publishes its `changed-witness` standing. The run's configured CPU line and the wave's lower optimization objective are different boundaries. The author and review omitted an available transition from their accounting. Calling this a hidden waiver would erase the evidence that distinguishes the reporting failure from an admission failure. SECOND COST RECEIPT, reported by the wave coordinator for gunbc#10530 from its required-floor cost artifact: `v2.test.claim.rust_crate_partition_witness` `witness_partition_perturbation_member_coverage_holds` was activated; approval and a request to land preceded the reconciliation. Its observed CPU cost was below the configured deadline, so this receipt establishes the missed execution addition, not an over-deadline verdict admitted by the waiver. CONTRASTING RECEIPT gunbc#10542: editing `v2.test.execution.emit_on_demand_family_crate_witness` `family_crate_one_build_members_warm_holds`, `family_crate_member_change_cold_rebuild_holds` and `family_crate_dispatch_change_cold_rebuild_holds` made their existing route gaps blocking under changed-witness admission. Run 33956888387 and baseline 33954515928 contain identical executed-identity sets; the named claims' outcomes remain `host_effect_refused` at `emit_host_native_cache_evict` / `FilesystemRemoval`. CI refused with `route-gap-before-verdict`; it did not waive that failure. The whole family-module change was withdrawn, including its helper changes. `floor_route_gap` and `floor_expected_red` already EXECUTE their enrolled identities, whereas ordinary `floor_cost_debt` membership withholds execution. Their union is not a dormant population, and an unchanged executed-identity set alone cannot detect an adjudication transition. INSTRUMENT AND CURRENT MITIGATION: `claim_executor --required-ci --source-root dag --source-root src/v2 --required-lane witnesses` produces the run receipts through `v1.cli_run` `write_required_floor_claim_cost_tsv` and `write_required_floor_disposition_tsv`, published as `required_floor_claim_cost.tsv` and `required_floor_disposition.tsv`. The wave's reconciliation was a manual identity join of those run products, not a repository-enforced reconciliation producer. RECOGNITION: bind baseline and candidate to immutable run subjects, join every identity in both receipts, list added and dropped executions, retain outcome and changed-witness policy transitions, and reconcile common-row deltas plus additions minus removals to the complete corpus total. Report the intended subset separately. Treat unavailable or censored costs as such, never as exact zeros. This manual reconciliation exposed the cost receipts; the existing changed-witness gate itself detected the route-gap receipt. Restoring an unintended source edit is a scope correction; moving it into a helper merely to avoid selection is not a repair. RUNG FOUND AT: mitigatable for the cost-report boundary. Complete run products and explicit policy diagnostics exist, and manual reconciliation detected the discrepancy, but no executed evidence here establishes that cost reports must consume that reconciliation. CEILING: mechanically preventable for report completeness, because finite identity sets, dispositions and exact step deltas are decidable at the receipt boundary. Whether a newly executed claim was intended or its additional coverage is worth its cost remains an author/operator judgment, outside that mechanical guarantee; this row does not propose overriding the declared verdict-only policy. NEXT TRIGGER: a cost-change reporting consumer that binds both run subjects, refuses incomplete or unreconciled accounting, and publishes execution and adjudication transitions beside the total. It must discriminate both a newly admitted debt identity and an unchanged-membership route-gap transition, with an unchanged-policy positive control; an added warning alone is insufficient. BOUNDARY AGAINST NEIGHBORS: `non_execution_undifferentiated_by_what_it_silenced` concerns missing purpose information for checks that do not reach a verdict; this class concerns edits changing execution or adjudication while the cost report preserves the author's old scope. `selection_view_read_as_population` concerns a selected view promoted to a population; this instance additionally requires a source-edit-induced admission/policy transition and a report that reconciles those transitions across two run subjects. The distinct repair is transition accounting, not purpose ranking or a different cost threshold.)
- **which modules get COMPILED is derived from which WITNESSES get EXECUTED, so production authority no selected claim imports is never prepared and the ordinary compiler floor goes unchecked over it** (INVALID STATE: a gate names a roster of TEST-MODULE identity prefixes, the run prepares the import closure of that roster, and preparation membership is therefore a downstream consequence of execution selection. A production module whose only importers are test modules outside the roster -- or which no test module imports at all -- is never loaded, never resolved, never typechecked, by any required run. HARM: the floor DESIGN section 4b names first -- names resolve, values inhabit declared types, closed variants eliminate exhaustively -- is unchecked over that population while both required lanes report green, so `main is green` and `the corpus holds the floor` are different propositions and nothing in the run says which one it established. **THE DISTINGUISHING FACT AGAINST EVERY NEIGHBOURING CLASS IS THAT NOTHING EVER LOOKED.** No diagnostic was produced and dropped, no lane swallowed an error, no aggregation lost a red, no exit status was mis-propagated. The first missing edge is ADMISSION TO THE PREPARED POPULATION, upstream of body resolution, which is why every instrument downstream of preparation reads clean and why looking for a suppressed diagnostic finds nothing. SPECIMEN WITH ITS POSITIVE CONTROL, both executed on main at 4da9c1a3fdd through the real acceptance path (`claim_batch --source-root dag --source-root src/v2 --entry dag/test/claim/systemd_property_directive_overlap_test.dag`, GUNBC_MEMORY_BUDGET_BYTES set): `extdeps.systemd` declares `RuntimeDirectoryProperty` as a variant of `SystemdUnitProperty`; `gunbc.systemd_property_directive_overlap` `systemd_property_writability` does not eliminate it. The run refuses at closure-strict-resolve with `non-exhaustive match: missing variant(s) RuntimeDirectoryProperty`. That module's only importer is a test module the gate roster does not name, so no required run had ever prepared it. **THE SPECIMEN CARRIES TWO OBLIGATIONS AND THE COMPILE-GREEN DISCHARGES ONLY ONE**, which is the half of this row a repair will get wrong: the classifier is a total match, so adding the arm is compelled by the compiler, while `systemd_unit_property_members` -- a hand-authored roster of the same coproduct's variants, consumed by both wire projections -- stays short with a clean compile and every count derived from it silently under-reports. That module's own annotation predicted exactly this in 2026-08 for `MainPID` and recorded it firing; it fired again here. The manual-enumeration face is `parallel_representation_debt`, and its own next-rung trigger -- a variant-enumeration surface over a declared coproduct -- is unrelated to this row's. **WHY WIDENING THE ROSTER IS NOT A CANDIDATE REPAIR, and the argument is structural rather than economic.** The roster keys on the identity of a WITNESS module, so the population it can address is bounded by which production modules some test module happens to import. Measured on 4da9c1a3fdd by a static module-import closure over `dag` and `src/v2` (module-declaration line plus import lines; roots = the modules matching `v2.workflow.required_floor` `required_gate_prefixes`; deliberately an OVER-estimate of the real closure, since long-home, fixture-home and cost-debt declines were not subtracted, so every figure is generous to the status quo): 2667 production modules, of which 1208 sit OUTSIDE the closure; of those 1208, 589 are imported by NO test module at all and 319 by nothing at all. No prefix string can name those 589, so the vocabulary cannot express half the exposed population; and reaching the remainder would mean enrolling 501 further test modules, which is the wall-clock the `required_gate_bankruptcy` drop was declared to cut. Widening also fuses WHAT TO COMPILE with WHAT TO EXECUTE into one roster, which is the DESIGN section 3 fork, and that drop already rules that widening the static roster moves its population rather than retiring it. **WHAT THE EXPOSURE NUMBER IS AND IS NOT: 1208 is production modules nobody compiles, NOT 1208 defects,** and it is a lower bound on the omitted authorities rather than an upper one, because the run's own `required-floor-disposition` artifact cannot count them -- they are DEPENDENCIES of witnesses, not witness identities, so the 12386 `declined_outside_gate_closure` rows on run 33967952362 are a different population and none of them is a production authority. **THE DEFECT CENSUS OVER THAT POPULATION IS UNMEASURED, AND THE DENOMINATOR BELONGS INSIDE THE CLAIM.** An executed instrument -- one synthetic witness module per outside-closure production module, importing exactly one of its exported symbols, fed to `claim_batch` as N entry groups sharing one module index so per-entry attribution survives -- found ZERO further defects IN THE 30 MODULES IT ACTUALLY MEASURED. 30 of 1199 is 2.5 percent coverage and supports no statement about the population; `no further defects found` is not `no further defects exist`. THAT INSTRUMENT HAS A STRUCTURAL CEILING, stated so a later complete-looking run is not read as covering 1208: it works by importing an exported symbol, so the 9 modules exporting none are unreachable by construction and stay unreachable however long it runs -- `extdeps.fixture.external_authority_missing`, `gunbc.auth.optional_impersonation`, `gunbc.toolchain_home_interference_probe`, `gunbc.tools.bmc_read_telemetry`, `tools.cheap_claim_pool_gate`, `tools.extdeps_external_authority_gate`, `tools.floor_effect_gate_witness`, `v2.workflow.ci_heal_revalidation_preflight_emit_test`, `v2.workflow.source_root_ingest_gate`. A SECOND, SHARPER LIMIT WAS NOT RESOLVED AND MUST BE RESOLVED BEFORE ANY CENSUS IS BELIEVED: the specimen run reported ONE error and stopped, so if resolve halts at the first defect per closure then a full census yields a LOWER BOUND with an unquantified gap rather than a count -- and a lower bound would immediately be quoted as `the census`. That question costs one module with two planted defects, not a corpus sweep. **WHY THE CENSUS WAS NOT COMPLETED, WHICH IS THE SAME FACT AS THIS ROW'S TRIGGER.** Measured on a BuildBuddy runner (MemTotal 7.45 GiB): one process resolves 30 outside-closure production modules and then dies rc=137 at VmHWM 7.20 GiB, having spent 361s of which roughly 60s is a fixed whole-tree warm-up every process pays regardless of subject; resident size before any subject is resolved is 5.9 GiB and grows monotonically at roughly 40 MB per module resolved, with nothing evicted between entries. Preparation cost is therefore denominated in the ACCUMULATED UNION of everything already resolved, which is the same property that makes the whole corpus refuse under 16 GiB against its measured 17.16 GiB demand. RUNG FOUND AT: below the ladder for the affected population -- this is silent wrongness rather than a weak rung, since an ordinary floor violation stands on main under two green required lanes and no mechanism reports it. For the population the gate DOES prepare the floor is mechanically preventable and unaffected. CEILING: 3, structurally guaranteed. Membership is decidable -- whether a declared module was prepared by a run is a set question over the declaration index the tree already carries -- so a run can derive its own preparation coverage and refuse rather than report a green whose population it never names. **NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, AT CAPABILITY GRAIN: a preparation whose RESIDENT COST IS BOUNDED BY THE SHARD rather than by the accumulated union of everything it has already resolved.** That is what makes a preparation selector disjoint from witness selection affordable, and what makes the change-derived population the `required_gate_bankruptcy` restoration trigger names cheap rather than merely possible. **WHAT DOES NOT DISCHARGE IT: a runner with 17.16 GiB or more.** That buys the census while leaving cost denominated in the CORPUS instead of the CHANGE, so the deficit stops being visible and never ranks for repair -- the absorbing arm in its exact form, where nothing is missed and the signal that the precise mechanism is anemic is destroyed. It may be taken as a mitigation for one bounded run; it is never the discharge. RECOGNITION RULE: for any gate, ask what its selector is KEYED ON and whether the population it must protect is expressible in that key. If the gate protects a property of modules and selects on the identity of tests, the two are joined only by whatever imports happen to exist, and the unprotected remainder is invisible by construction rather than by omission.)
